# Vibhaṅgapāḷi(Wrong Version) ^0

Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa ^1

Abhidhammapiṭake ^2

Vibhaṅgapāḷi ^3

1. Khandhavibhaṅgo ^4

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^5

1. Pañcakkhandhā – rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^6

viññāṇakkhandho. ^7

1. Rūpakkhandho ^8

2. Tattha katamo rūpakkhandho? Yaṃ kiñci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapaccuppannaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā ^9

bahiddhā vā oḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūre santike vā, tadekajjhaṃ ^10

abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati rūpakkhandho. ^11

3. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ atītaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vigataṃ vipariṇataṃ atthaṅgataṃ ^12

abbhatthaṅgataṃ uppajjitvā vigataṃ atītaṃ atītaṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhūtā catunnañca ^13

mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ atītaṃ. ^14

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ anāgataṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ asañjātaṃ anibbattaṃ ^15

anabhinibbattaṃ apātubhūtaṃ anuppannaṃ asamuppannaṃ anuṭṭhitaṃ asamuṭṭhitaṃ anāgataṃ ^16

anāgataṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhūtā catunnañca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – idaṃ ^17

vuccati rūpaṃ anāgataṃ. ^18

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ paccuppannaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ sañjātaṃ nibbattaṃ ^19

abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ uppannaṃ samuppannaṃ uṭṭhitaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ paccuppannaṃ ^20

paccuppannaṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhūtā catunnañca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – idaṃ ^21

vuccati rūpaṃ paccuppannaṃ. ^22

4. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ ^23

niyakaṃ pāṭipuggalikaṃ upādinnaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhūtā catunnañca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – ^24

idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ ajjhattaṃ. ^25

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ bahiddhā? Yaṃ rūpaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ^26

ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakaṃ pāṭipuggalikaṃ upādinnaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhūtā catunnañca ^27

mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ bahiddhā. ^28

5. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ oḷārikaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ…pe… phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^29

rūpaṃ oḷārikaṃ. ^30

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ? Itthindriyaṃ…pe… kabaḷīkāro [kabaḷiṃkāro (sī. syā.)] āhāro – ^31

idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ. ^32

6. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ hīnaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ uññātaṃ avaññātaṃ hīḷitaṃ ^33

paribhūtaṃ acittīkataṃ hīnaṃ hīnamataṃ hīnasammataṃ aniṭṭhaṃ akantaṃ amanāpaṃ, rūpā saddā ^34

gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ hīnaṃ. ^35

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ paṇītaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ anuññātaṃ anavaññātaṃ ^36

ahīḷitaṃ aparibhūtaṃ cittīkataṃ paṇītaṃ paṇītamataṃ paṇītasammataṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ, rūpā ^37

saddā gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ paṇītaṃ. Taṃ taṃ vā pana rūpaṃ upādāyupādāya ^38

rūpaṃ hīnaṃ paṇītaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. ^39

7. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ dūre? Itthindriyaṃ…pe… kabaḷīkāro āhāro, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi ^40

rūpaṃ anāsanne anupakaṭṭhe dūre asantike – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ dūre. ^41

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ santike? Cakkhāyatanaṃ…pe… phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, yaṃ vā panaññampi ^42

atthi rūpaṃ āsanne upakaṭṭhe avidūre santike – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ santike. Taṃ taṃ vā pana rūpaṃ ^43

upādāyupādāya rūpaṃ dūre santike daṭṭhabbaṃ. ^44

2. Vedanākkhandho ^45

8. Tattha katamo vedanākkhandho? Yā kāci vedanā atītānāgatapaccuppannā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā ^46

oḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūre santike vā, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā ^47

– ayaṃ vuccati vedanākkhandho. ^48

9. Tattha katamā vedanā atītā? Yā vedanā atītā niruddhā vigatā vipariṇatā atthaṅgatā ^49

abbhatthaṅgatā uppajjitvā vigatā atītā atītaṃsena saṅgahitā, sukhā vedanā dukkhā vedanā ^50

adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati vedanā atītā. ^51

Tattha katamā vedanā anāgatā? Yā vedanā ajātā abhūtā asañjātā anibbattā anabhinibbattā ^52

apātubhūtā anuppannā asamuppannā anuṭṭhitā asamuṭṭhitā anāgatā anāgataṃsena saṅgahitā, sukhā ^53

vedanā dukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati vedanā anāgatā. ^54

Tattha katamā vedanā paccuppannā? Yā vedanā jātā bhūtā sañjātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā ^55

uppannā samuppannā uṭṭhitā samuṭṭhitā paccuppannā paccuppannaṃsena saṅgahitā, sukhā vedanā ^56

dukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati vedanā paccuppannā. ^57

10. Tattha katamā vedanā ajjhattā? Yā vedanā tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakā ^58

pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, sukhā vedanā dukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati vedanā ^59

ajjhattā. ^60

Tattha katamā vedanā bahiddhā? Yā vedanā tesaṃ tesaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ajjhattaṃ ^61

paccattaṃ niyakā pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, sukhā vedanā dukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ^62

ayaṃ vuccati vedanā bahiddhā. ^63

11. Tattha katamā vedanā oḷārikā sukhumā? Akusalā vedanā oḷārikā, kusalābyākatā vedanā ^64

sukhumā. Kusalākusalā vedanā oḷārikā, abyākatā vedanā sukhumā. Dukkhā vedanā oḷārikā, sukhā ca ^65

adukkhamasukhā ca vedanā sukhumā. Sukhadukkhā vedanā oḷārikā, adukkhamasukhā vedanā sukhumā. ^66

Asamāpannassa vedanā oḷārikā, samāpannassa vedanā sukhumā. Sāsavā vedanā oḷārikā, anāsavā vedanā ^67

sukhumā. Taṃ taṃ vā pana vedanaṃ upādāyupādāya vedanā oḷārikā sukhumā daṭṭhabbā. ^68

12. Tattha katamā vedanā hīnā paṇītā? Akusalā vedanā hīnā, kusalābyākatā vedanā paṇītā. ^69

Kusalākusalā vedanā hīnā, abyākatā vedanā paṇītā. Dukkhā vedanā hīnā, sukhā ca adukkhamasukhā ca ^70

vedanā paṇītā. Sukhadukkhā vedanā hīnā, adukkhamasukhā vedanā paṇītā. Asamāpannassa vedanā hīnā, ^71

samāpannassa vedanā paṇītā. Sāsavā vedanā hīnā, anāsavā vedanā paṇītā. Taṃ taṃ vā pana vedanaṃ ^72

upādāyupādāya vedanā hīnā paṇītā daṭṭhabbā. ^73

13. Tattha katamā vedanā dūre? Akusalā vedanā kusalābyākatāhi vedanāhi dūre; kusalābyākatā ^74

vedanā akusalāya vedanāya dūre; kusalā vedanā akusalābyākatāhi vedanāhi dūre; akusalābyākatā vedanā ^75

kusalāya vedanāya dūre; abyākatā vedanā kusalākusalāhi vedanāhi dūre; kusalākusalā vedanā ^76

abyākatāya vedanāya dūre. Dukkhā vedanā sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi dūre; sukhā ca ^77

adukkhamasukhā ca vedanā dukkhāya vedanāya dūre; sukhā vedanā dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca ^78

vedanāhi dūre; dukkhā ca adukkhamasukhā ca vedanā sukhāya vedanāya dūre; adukkhamasukhā vedanā ^79

sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi dūre; sukhadukkhā vedanā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya dūre. Asamāpannassa ^80

vedanā samāpannassa vedanāya dūre; samāpannassa vedanā asamāpannassa vedanāya dūre. Sāsavā ^81

vedanā anāsavāya vedanāya dūre; anāsavā vedanā sāsavāya vedanāya dūre – ayaṃ vuccati vedanā dūre. ^82

Tattha katamā vedanā santike? Akusalā vedanā akusalāya vedanāya santike; kusalā vedanā ^83

kusalāya vedanāya santike; abyākatā vedanā abyākatāya vedanāya santike. Dukkhā vedanā dukkhāya ^84

vedanāya santike; sukhā vedanā sukhāya vedanāya santike; adukkhamasukhā vedanā ^85

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya santike. Asamāpannassa vedanā asamāpannassa vedanāya santike; ^86

samāpannassa vedanā samāpannassa vedanāya santike. Sāsavā vedanā sāsavāya vedanāya santike; ^87

anāsavā vedanā anāsavāya vedanāya santike. Ayaṃ vuccati vedanā santike. Taṃ taṃ vā pana vedanaṃ ^88

upādāyupādāya vedanā dūre santike daṭṭhabbā. ^89

3. Saññākkhandho ^90

14. Tattha katamo saññākkhandho? Yā kāci saññā atītānāgatapaccuppannā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā ^91

oḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūre santike vā tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā ^92

– ayaṃ vuccati saññākkhandho. ^93

15. Tattha katamā saññā atītā? Yā saññā atītā niruddhā vigatā vipariṇatā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā ^94

uppajjitvā vigatā atītā atītaṃsena saṅgahitā, cakkhusamphassajā saññā sotasamphassajā saññā ^95

ghānasamphassajā saññā jivhāsamphassajā saññā kāyasamphassajā saññā manosamphassajā saññā – ^96

ayaṃ vuccati saññā atītā. ^97

Tattha katamā saññā anāgatā? Yā saññā ajātā abhūtā asañjātā anibbattā anabhinibbattā apātubhūtā ^98

anuppannā asamuppannā anuṭṭhitā asamuṭṭhitā anāgatā anāgataṃsena saṅgahitā, cakkhusamphassajā ^99

saññā sotasamphassajā saññā ghānasamphassajā saññā jivhāsamphassajā saññā kāyasamphassajā saññā ^100

manosamphassajā saññā – ayaṃ vuccati saññā anāgatā. ^101

Tattha katamā saññā paccuppannā? Yā saññā jātā bhūtā sañjātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā ^102

uppannā samuppannā uṭṭhitā samuṭṭhitā paccuppannā paccuppannaṃsena saṅgahitā, cakkhusamphassajā ^103

saññā sotasamphassajā saññā ghānasamphassajā saññā jivhāsamphassajā saññā kāyasamphassajā saññā ^104

manosamphassajā saññā – ayaṃ vuccati saññā paccuppannā. ^105

16. Tattha katamā saññā ajjhattā? Yā saññā tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakā ^106

pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, cakkhusamphassajā saññā sotasamphassajā saññā ghānasamphassajā saññā ^107

jivhāsamphassajā saññā kāyasamphassajā saññā manosamphassajā saññā – ayaṃ vuccati saññā ajjhattā. ^108

Tattha katamā saññā bahiddhā? Yā saññā tesaṃ tesaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ajjhattaṃ ^109

paccattaṃ niyakā pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, cakkhusamphassajā saññā sotasamphassajā saññā ^110

ghānasamphassajā saññā jivhāsamphassajā saññā kāyasamphassajā saññā manosamphassajā saññā – ^111

ayaṃ vuccati saññā bahiddhā. ^112

17. Tattha katamā saññā oḷārikā sukhumā? Paṭighasamphassajā saññā oḷārikā, ^113

adhivacanasamphassajā saññā sukhumā. Akusalā saññā oḷārikā, kusalābyākatā saññā sukhumā. ^114

Kusalākusalā saññā oḷārikā, abyākatā saññā sukhumā. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā oḷārikā, ^115

sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā sukhumā. Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi ^116

sampayuttā saññā oḷārikā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā sukhumā. Asamāpannassa ^117

saññā oḷārikā, samāpannassa saññā sukhumā. Sāsavā saññā oḷārikā, anāsavā saññā sukhumā. Taṃ taṃ ^118

vā pana saññaṃ upādāyupādāya saññā oḷārikā sukhumā daṭṭhabbā. ^119

18. Tattha katamā saññā hīnā paṇītā? Akusalā saññā hīnā, kusalābyākatā saññā paṇītā. ^120

Kusalākusalā saññā hīnā, abyākatā saññā paṇītā. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā hīnā, sukhāya ca ^121

adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā paṇītā. Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā ^122

hīnā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā paṇītā. Asamāpannassa saññā hīnā, samāpannassa ^123

saññā paṇītā. Sāsavā saññā hīnā, anāsavā saññā paṇītā. Taṃ taṃ vā pana saññaṃ upādāyupādāya saññā ^124

hīnā paṇītā daṭṭhabbā. ^125

19. Tattha katamā saññā dūre? Akusalā saññā kusalābyākatāhi saññāhi dūre; kusalābyākatā saññā ^126

akusalāya saññāya dūre; kusalā saññā akusalābyākatāhi saññāhi dūre; akusalābyākatā saññā kusalāya ^127

saññāya dūre. Abyākatā saññā kusalākusalāhi saññāhi dūre; kusalākusalā saññā abyākatāya saññāya ^128

dūre. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttāhi ^129

saññāhi dūre; sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā dukkhāya vedanāya ^130

sampayuttāya saññāya dūre; sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca ^131

vedanāhi sampayuttāhi saññāhi dūre; dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā ^132

sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttāya saññāya dūre; adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā ^133

sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttāhi saññāhi dūre; sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā saññā ^134

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttāya saññāya dūre. Asamāpannassa saññā samāpannassa saññāya ^135

dūre; samāpannassa saññā asamāpannassa saññāya dūre. Sāsavā saññā anāsavāya saññāya dūre; anāsavā ^136

saññā sāsavāya saññāya dūre – ayaṃ vuccati saññā dūre. ^137

Tattha katamā saññā santike? Akusalā saññā akusalāya saññāya santike; kusalā saññā kusalāya ^138

saññāya santike; abyākatā saññā abyākatāya saññāya santike. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā ^139

dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttāya saññāya santike; sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā sukhāya ^140

vedanāya sampayuttāya saññāya santike; adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saññā ^141

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttāya saññāya santike. Asamāpannassa saññā asamāpannassa ^142

saññāya santike; samāpannassa saññā samāpannassa saññāya santike. Sāsavā saññā sāsavāya saññāya ^143

santike; anāsavā saññā anāsavāya saññāya santike. Ayaṃ vuccati saññā santike. Taṃ taṃ vā pana ^144

saññaṃ upādāyupādāya saññā dūre santike daṭṭhabbā. ^145

4. Saṅkhārakkhandho ^146

20. Tattha katamo saṅkhārakkhandho? Ye keci saṅkhārā atītānāgatapaccuppannā ajjhattā vā ^147

bahiddhā vā oḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā ye dūre santike vā, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā ^148

abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati saṅkhārakkhandho. ^149

21. Tattha katame saṅkhārā atītā? Ye saṅkhārā atītā niruddhā vigatā vipariṇatā atthaṅgatā ^150

abbhatthaṅgatā uppajjitvā vigatā atītā atītaṃsena saṅgahitā, cakkhusamphassajā cetanā sotasamphassajā ^151

cetanā ghānasamphassajā cetanā jivhāsamphassajā cetanā kāyasamphassajā cetanā manosamphassajā ^152

cetanā – ime vuccanti saṅkhārā atītā. ^153

Tattha katame saṅkhārā anāgatā? Ye saṅkhārā ajātā abhūtā asañjātā anibbattā anabhinibbattā ^154

apātubhūtā anuppannā asamuppannā anuṭṭhitā asamuṭṭhitā anāgatā anāgataṃsena saṅgahitā, ^155

cakkhusamphassajā cetanā sotasamphassajā cetanā ghānasamphassajā cetanā jivhāsamphassajā cetanā ^156

kāyasamphassajā cetanā manosamphassajā cetanā – ime vuccanti saṅkhārā anāgatā. ^157

Tattha katame saṅkhārā paccuppannā? Ye saṅkhārā jātā bhūtā sañjātā nibbattā abhinibbattā ^158

pātubhūtā uppannā samuppannā uṭṭhitā samuṭṭhitā paccuppannā paccuppannaṃsena saṅgahitā, ^159

cakkhusamphassajā cetanā sotasamphassajā cetanā ghānasamphassajā cetanā jivhāsamphassajā cetanā ^160

kāyasamphassajā cetanā manosamphassajā cetanā – ime vuccanti saṅkhārā paccuppannā. ^161

22. Tattha katame saṅkhārā ajjhattā? Ye saṅkhārā tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakā ^162

pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, cakkhusamphassajā cetanā sotasamphassajā cetanā ghānasamphassajā cetanā ^163

jivhāsamphassajā cetanā kāyasamphassajā cetanā manosamphassajā cetanā – ime vuccanti saṅkhārā ^164

ajjhattā. ^165

Tattha katame saṅkhārā bahiddhā? Ye saṅkhārā tesaṃ tesaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ^166

ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakā pāṭipuggalikā upādinnā, cakkhusamphassajā cetanā sotasamphassajā cetanā ^167

ghānasamphassajā cetanā jivhāsamphassajā cetanā kāyasamphassajā cetanā manosamphassajā cetanā – ^168

ime vuccanti saṅkhārā bahiddhā. ^169

23. Tattha katame saṅkhārā oḷārikā sukhumā? Akusalā saṅkhārā oḷārikā, kusalābyākatā saṅkhārā ^170

sukhumā. Kusalākusalā saṅkhārā oḷārikā, abyākatā saṅkhārā sukhumā. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā ^171

saṅkhārā oḷārikā, sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā sukhumā. ^172

Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā oḷārikā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā ^173

saṅkhārā sukhumā. Asamāpannassa saṅkhārā oḷārikā, samāpannassa saṅkhārā sukhumā. Sāsavā ^174

saṅkhārā oḷārikā, anāsavā saṅkhārā sukhumā. Te te vā pana saṅkhāre upādāyupādāya saṅkhārā oḷārikā ^175

sukhumā daṭṭhabbā. ^176

24. Tattha katame saṅkhārā hīnā paṇītā? Akusalā saṅkhārā hīnā, kusalābyākatā saṅkhārā paṇītā. ^177

Kusalākusalā saṅkhārā hīnā, abyākatā saṅkhārā paṇītā. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā hīnā, ^178

sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā paṇītā. Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi ^179

sampayuttā saṅkhārā hīnā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā paṇītā. Asamāpannassa ^180

saṅkhārā hīnā, samāpannassa saṅkhārā paṇītā. Sāsavā saṅkhārā hīnā, anāsavā saṅkhārā paṇītā. Te te vā ^181

pana saṅkhāre upādāyupādāya saṅkhārā hīnā paṇītā daṭṭhabbā. ^182

25. Tattha katame saṅkhārā dūre? Akusalā saṅkhārā kusalābyākatehi saṅkhārehi dūre; ^183

kusalābyākatā saṅkhārā akusalehi saṅkhārehi dūre; kusalā saṅkhārā akusalābyākatehi saṅkhārehi dūre; ^184

akusalābyākatā saṅkhārā kusalehi saṅkhārehi dūre; abyākatā saṅkhārā kusalākusalehi saṅkhārehi dūre; ^185

kusalākusalā saṅkhārā abyākatehi saṅkhārehi dūre. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā sukhāya ^186

ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttehi saṅkhārehi dūre; sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca ^187

vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi saṅkhārehi dūre; sukhāya vedanāya ^188

sampayuttā saṅkhārā dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttehi saṅkhārehi dūre; ^189

dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi ^190

saṅkhārehi dūre; adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi ^191

sampayuttehi saṅkhārehi dūre; sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā saṅkhārā adukkhamasukhāya ^192

vedanāya sampayuttehi saṅkhārehi dūre. Asamāpannassa saṅkhārā samāpannassa saṅkhārehi dūre; ^193

samāpannassa saṅkhārā asamāpannassa saṅkhārehi dūre. Sāsavā saṅkhārā anāsavehi saṅkhārehi dūre; ^194

anāsavā saṅkhārā sāsavehi saṅkhārehi dūre. Ime vuccanti saṅkhārā dūre. ^195

Tattha katame saṅkhārā santike? Akusalā saṅkhārā akusalānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike; kusalā ^196

saṅkhārā kusalānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike; abyākatā saṅkhārā abyākatānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike. ^197

Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike; ^198

sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike; ^199

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā saṅkhārā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttānaṃ ^200

saṅkhārānaṃ santike. Asamāpannassa saṅkhārā asamāpannassa saṅkhārānaṃ santike; samāpannassa ^201

saṅkhārā samāpannassa saṅkhārānaṃ santike. Sāsavā saṅkhārā sāsavānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike; ^202

anāsavā saṅkhārā anāsavānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ santike. Ime vuccanti saṅkhārā santike. Te te vā pana ^203

saṅkhāre upādāyupādāya saṅkhārā dūre santike daṭṭhabbā. ^204

5. Viññāṇakkhandho ^205

26. Tattha katamo viññāṇakkhandho? Yaṃ kiñci viññāṇaṃ atītānāgatapaccuppannaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā ^206

bahiddhā vā oḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūre santike vā, tadekajjhaṃ ^207

abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati viññāṇakkhandho. ^208

27. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ atītaṃ? Yaṃ viññāṇaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vigataṃ vipariṇataṃ ^209

atthaṅgataṃ abbhatthaṅgataṃ uppajjitvā vigataṃ atītaṃ atītaṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ ^210

sotaviññāṇaṃ ghānaviññāṇaṃ jivhāviññāṇaṃ kāyaviññāṇaṃ manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ ^211

atītaṃ. ^212

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ anāgataṃ? Yaṃ viññāṇaṃ ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ asañjātaṃ anibbattaṃ ^213

anabhinibbattaṃ apātubhūtaṃ anuppannaṃ asamuppannaṃ anuṭṭhitaṃ asamuṭṭhitaṃ anāgataṃ ^214

anāgataṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ sotaviññāṇaṃ ghānaviññāṇaṃ jivhāviññāṇaṃ ^215

kāyaviññāṇaṃ manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ anāgataṃ. ^216

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ paccuppannaṃ? Yaṃ viññāṇaṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ sañjātaṃ nibbattaṃ ^217

abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ uppannaṃ samuppannaṃ uṭṭhitaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ paccuppannaṃ ^218

paccuppannaṃsena saṅgahitaṃ, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ sotaviññāṇaṃ ghānaviññāṇaṃ jivhāviññāṇaṃ ^219

kāyaviññāṇaṃ manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ paccuppannaṃ. ^220

28. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ? Yaṃ viññāṇaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhattaṃ ^221

paccattaṃ niyakaṃ pāṭipuggalikaṃ upādinnaṃ, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ sotaviññāṇaṃ ghānaviññāṇaṃ ^222

jivhāviññāṇaṃ kāyaviññāṇaṃ manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ ajjhattaṃ. ^223

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ bahiddhā? Yaṃ viññāṇaṃ tesaṃ tesaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ^224

ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ niyakaṃ pāṭipuggalikaṃ upādinnaṃ, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ sotaviññāṇaṃ ^225

ghānaviññāṇaṃ jivhāviññāṇaṃ kāyaviññāṇaṃ manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ bahiddhā. ^226

29. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ sukhumaṃ? Akusalaṃ viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ, kusalābyākatā ^227

viññāṇā sukhumā. Kusalākusalā viññāṇā oḷārikā, abyākataṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhumaṃ. Dukkhāya vedanāya ^228

sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ, sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā ^229

sukhumā. Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā oḷārikā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^230

sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhumaṃ. Asamāpannassa viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ, samāpannassa viññāṇaṃ ^231

sukhumaṃ. Sāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ, anāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhumaṃ. Taṃ taṃ vā pana viññāṇaṃ ^232

upādāyupādāya viññāṇaṃ oḷārikaṃ sukhumaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. ^233

30. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ hīnaṃ paṇītaṃ? Akusalaṃ viññāṇaṃ hīnaṃ, kusalābyākatā viññāṇā ^234

paṇītā. Kusalākusalā viññāṇā hīnā, abyākataṃ viññāṇaṃ paṇītaṃ. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ ^235

viññāṇaṃ hīnaṃ, sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā paṇītā. ^236

Sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā hīnā, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ ^237

viññāṇaṃ paṇītaṃ. Asamāpannassa viññāṇaṃ hīnaṃ, samāpannassa viññāṇaṃ paṇītaṃ. Sāsavaṃ ^238

viññāṇaṃ hīnaṃ, anāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ paṇītaṃ. Taṃ taṃ vā pana viññāṇaṃ upādāyupādāya viññāṇaṃ ^239

hīnaṃ paṇītaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. ^240

31. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ dūre? Akusalaṃ viññāṇaṃ kusalābyākatehi viññāṇehi dūre; ^241

kusalābyākatā viññāṇā akusalā viññāṇā dūre; kusalaṃ viññāṇaṃ akusalābyākatehi viññāṇehi dūre; ^242

akusalābyākatā viññāṇā kusalā viññāṇā dūre; abyākataṃ viññāṇaṃ kusalākusalehi viññāṇehi dūre; ^243

kusalākusalā viññāṇā abyākatā viññāṇā dūre. Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhāya ^244

ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttehi viññāṇehi dūre; sukhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca ^245

vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā viññāṇā dūre; sukhāya vedanāya ^246

sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttehi viññāṇehi dūre; ^247

dukkhāya ca adukkhamasukhāya ca vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā ^248

viññāṇā dūre; adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi ^249

sampayuttehi viññāṇehi dūre; sukhadukkhāhi vedanāhi sampayuttā viññāṇā adukkhamasukhāya ^250

vedanāya sampayuttā viññāṇā dūre. Asamāpannassa viññāṇaṃ samāpannassa viññāṇā dūre; ^251

samāpannassa viññāṇaṃ asamāpannassa viññāṇā dūre. Sāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ anāsavā viññāṇā dūre; ^252

anāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ sāsavā viññāṇā dūre – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ dūre. ^253

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇaṃ santike? Akusalaṃ viññāṇaṃ akusalassa viññāṇassa santike; kusalaṃ ^254

viññāṇaṃ kusalassa viññāṇassa santike; abyākataṃ viññāṇaṃ abyākatassa viññāṇassa santike. ^255

Dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttassa viññāṇassa santike; ^256

sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttassa viññāṇassa santike; ^257

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ viññāṇaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttassa ^258

viññāṇassa santike. Asamāpannassa viññāṇaṃ asamāpannassa viññāṇassa santike; samāpannassa ^259

viññāṇaṃ samāpannassa viññāṇassa santike. Sāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ sāsavassa viññāṇassa santike; ^260

anāsavaṃ viññāṇaṃ anāsavassa viññāṇassa santike – idaṃ vuccati viññāṇaṃ santike. Taṃ taṃ vā pana ^261

viññāṇaṃ upādāyupādāya viññāṇaṃ dūre santike daṭṭhabbaṃ. ^262

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^263

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^264

32. Pañcakkhandhā – rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^265

viññāṇakkhandho. ^266

1. Rūpakkhandho ^267

33. Tattha katamo rūpakkhandho? Ekavidhena rūpakkhandho – sabbaṃ rūpaṃ na hetu, ahetukaṃ, ^268

hetuvippayuttaṃ, sappaccayaṃ, saṅkhataṃ, rūpaṃ, lokiyaṃ, sāsavaṃ, saṃyojaniyaṃ, ganthaniyaṃ, ^269

oghaniyaṃ, yoganiyaṃ, nīvaraṇiyaṃ, parāmaṭṭhaṃ, upādāniyaṃ, saṃkilesikaṃ, abyākataṃ, ^270

anārammaṇaṃ, acetasikaṃ, cittavippayuttaṃ, nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ, ^271

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikaṃ, na savitakkasavicāraṃ, na avitakkavicāramattaṃ, avitakkaavicāraṃ, na ^272

pītisahagataṃ, na sukhasahagataṃ, na upekkhāsahagataṃ, neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ, ^273

neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukaṃ, nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī, nevasekkhanāsekkhaṃ, ^274

parittaṃ, kāmāvacaraṃ, na rūpāvacaraṃ, na arūpāvacaraṃ, pariyāpannaṃ, no apariyāpannaṃ, aniyataṃ, ^275

aniyyānikaṃ, uppannaṃ, chahi viññāṇehi viññeyyaṃ, aniccaṃ, jarābhibhūtaṃ. Evaṃ ekavidhena ^276

rūpakkhandho. ^277

Duvidhena rūpakkhandho – atthi rūpaṃ upādā, atthi rūpaṃ no upādā [nupādā (sī. ka.)]. Atthi rūpaṃ ^278

upādinnaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ anupādinnaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ upādinnupādāniyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ ^279

anupādinnupādāniyaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ anidassanaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, ^280

atthi rūpaṃ appaṭighaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ indriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, atthi ^281

rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ viññatti, atthi rūpaṃ na viññatti. Atthi rūpaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ, ^282

atthi rūpaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ cittasahabhu, atthi rūpaṃ na cittasahabhu. Atthi rūpaṃ ^283

cittānuparivatti, atthi rūpaṃ na cittānuparivatti. Atthi rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ. Atthi ^284

rūpaṃ oḷārikaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ. Atthi rūpaṃ dūre, atthi rūpaṃ santike…pe… atthi rūpaṃ ^285

kabaḷīkāro āhāro, atthi rūpaṃ na kabaḷīkāro āhāro. Evaṃ duvidhena rūpakkhandho. ^286

(Yathā rūpakaṇḍe vibhattaṃ, tathā idha vibhajitabbaṃ.) ^287

Tividhena rūpakkhandho – yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ taṃ upādā, yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ taṃ ^288

atthi upādā, atthi no upādā. Yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ taṃ upādinnaṃ, yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ taṃ ^289

atthi upādinnaṃ, atthi anupādinnaṃ. Yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ taṃ upādinnupādāniyaṃ, yaṃ taṃ ^290

rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ taṃ atthi upādinnupādāniyaṃ, atthi anupādinnupādāniyaṃ…pe… yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ^291

ajjhattikaṃ taṃ na kabaḷīkāro āhāro, yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ taṃ atthi kabaḷīkāro āhāro, atthi na ^292

kabaḷīkāro āhāro. Evaṃ tividhena rūpakkhandho. ^293

Catubbidhena rūpakkhandho – yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā taṃ atthi upādinnaṃ, atthi anupādinnaṃ; ^294

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ no upādā taṃ atthi upādinnaṃ, atthi anupādinnaṃ. Yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā taṃ atthi ^295

upādinnupādāniyaṃ, atthi anupādinnupādāniyaṃ; yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ no upādā taṃ atthi ^296

upādinnupādāniyaṃ, atthi anupādinnupādāniyaṃ. Yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi ^297

appaṭighaṃ; yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ no upādā taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi appaṭighaṃ. Yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā ^298

taṃ atthi oḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ; yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ no upādā taṃ atthi oḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ. Yaṃ ^299

taṃ rūpaṃ upādā taṃ atthi dūre, atthi santike; yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ no upādā taṃ atthi dūre, atthi santike… ^300

pe… diṭṭhaṃ, sutaṃ, mutaṃ, viññātaṃ rūpaṃ. Evaṃ catubbidhena rūpakkhandho. ^301

Pañcavidhena rūpakkhandho – pathavīdhātu, āpodhātu, tejodhātu, vāyodhātu, yañca rūpaṃ upādā. ^302

Evaṃ pañcavidhena rūpakkhandho. ^303

Chabbidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, sotaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, ghānaviññeyyaṃ ^304

rūpaṃ, jivhāviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, kāyaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, manoviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ. Evaṃ chabbidhena ^305

rūpakkhandho. ^306

Sattavidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, sotaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, ghānaviññeyyaṃ ^307

rūpaṃ, jivhāviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, kāyaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, manodhātuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, ^308

manoviññāṇadhātuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ. Evaṃ sattavidhena rūpakkhandho. ^309

Aṭṭhavidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, sotaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, ghānaviññeyyaṃ ^310

rūpaṃ, jivhāviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, kāyaviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ atthi sukhasamphassaṃ, atthi ^311

dukkhasamphassaṃ, manodhātuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ, manoviññāṇadhātuviññeyyaṃ rūpaṃ. Evaṃ ^312

aṭṭhavidhena rūpakkhandho. ^313

Navavidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, ^314

kāyindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, yañca rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ. Evaṃ navavidhena ^315

rūpakkhandho. ^316

Dasavidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, ^317

kāyindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, na indriyaṃ rūpaṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi ^318

appaṭighaṃ. Evaṃ dasavidhena rūpakkhandho. ^319

Ekādasavidhena rūpakkhandho – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, ^320

kāyāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, yañca ^321

rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ. Evaṃ ekādasavidhena rūpakkhandho. ^322

Ayaṃ vuccati rūpakkhandho. ^323

2. Vedanākkhandho ^324

34. Tattha katamo vedanākkhandho? Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^325

Duvidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^326

Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato. ^327

Catubbidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^328

apariyāpanno. ^329

Pañcavidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi sukhindriyaṃ, atthi dukkhindriyaṃ, atthi somanassindriyaṃ, ^330

atthi domanassindriyaṃ, atthi upekkhindriyaṃ. Evaṃ pañcavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^331

Chabbidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^332

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā ^333

vedanā. Evaṃ chabbidhena vedanākkhandho. ^334

Sattavidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^335

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^336

vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ sattavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^337

Aṭṭhavidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^338

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā atthi sukhā, atthi dukkhā, ^339

manodhātusamphassajā vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ aṭṭhavidhena ^340

vedanākkhandho. ^341

Navavidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^342

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^343

vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi abyākatā. Evaṃ ^344

navavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^345

Dasavidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^346

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā atthi sukhā, atthi dukkhā, ^347

manodhātusamphassajā vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi ^348

abyākatā. Evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^349

35. Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^350

Duvidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^351

Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, atthi ^352

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^353

anupādinnaanupādāniyo. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^354

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi avitakkaavicāro. ^355

Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. ^356

Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya ^357

pahātabbahetuko. Atthi ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Atthi sekkho, ^358

atthi asekkho, atthi nevasekkhanāsekkho. Atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo. Atthi ^359

parittārammaṇo, atthi mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi appamāṇārammaṇo. Atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi ^360

paṇīto. Atthi micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi ^361

maggahetuko, atthi maggādhipati. Atthi uppanno, atthi anuppanno, atthi uppādī. Atthi atīto, atthi ^362

anāgato, atthi paccuppanno. Atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi paccuppannārammaṇo. ^363

Atthi ajjhatto, atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho. Atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, ^364

atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^365

36. Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^366

Duvidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto…pe… atthi na ^367

hetusahetuko, atthi na hetuahetuko. Atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. Atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na ^368

viññeyyo. Atthi sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. Atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. Atthi ^369

āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavo. Atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. Atthi ^370

saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi saṃyojanavippayutto. Atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi ^371

saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. Atthi ganthaniyo, atthi aganthaniyo. Atthi ganthasampayutto, atthi ^372

ganthavippayutto. Atthi ganthavippayuttaganthaniyo, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyo. Atthi ^373

oghaniyo, atthi anoghaniyo. Atthi oghasampayutto, atthi oghavippayutto. Atthi oghavippayuttaoghaniyo, ^374

atthi oghavippayuttaanoghaniyo. Atthi yoganiyo, atthi ayoganiyo. Atthi yogasampayutto, atthi ^375

yogavippayutto. Atthi yogavippayuttayoganiyo, atthi yogavippayuttaayoganiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇiyo, atthi ^376

anīvaraṇiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇasampayutto, atthi nīvaraṇavippayutto. Atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyo, ^377

atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyo. Atthi parāmaṭṭho, atthi aparāmaṭṭho. Atthi parāmāsasampayutto, ^378

atthi parāmāsavippayutto. Atthi parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭho, atthi parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭho. ^379

Atthi upādinno, atthi anupādinno. Atthi upādāniyo, atthi anupādāniyo. Atthi upādānasampayutto, atthi ^380

upādānavippayutto. Atthi upādānavippayuttaupādāniyo, atthi upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyo. Atthi ^381

saṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkilesiko. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭho, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭho. Atthi kilesasampayutto, atthi ^382

kilesavippayutto. Atthi kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesiko, atthi kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi dassanena ^383

pahātabbo, atthi na dassanena pahātabbo. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. ^384

Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, atthi na dassanena pahātabbahetuko. Atthi bhāvanāya ^385

pahātabbahetuko, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Atthi savitakko, atthi avitakko. Atthi savicāro, ^386

atthi avicāro. Atthi sappītiko, atthi appītiko. Atthi pītisahagato, atthi na pītisahagato. Atthi kāmāvacaro, ^387

atthi na kāmāvacaro. Atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi na rūpāvacaro. Atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi na arūpāvacaro. ^388

Atthi pariyāpanno, atthi apariyāpanno. Atthi niyyāniko, atthi aniyyāniko. Atthi niyato, atthi aniyato. ^389

Atthi sauttaro, atthi anuttaro. Atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^390

Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^391

vedanākkhandho. ^392

37. Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^393

Duvidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^394

Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, atthi ^395

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Atthi upādinnupādāniyo…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi ^396

bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^397

Dukamūlakaṃ. ^398

vedanākkhandho. ^402

vedanākkhandho. ^406

āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. Atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavo… ^410

pe… atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^411

Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^412

vedanākkhandho. ^413

pahātabbahetuko. Atthi ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Atthi sekkho, ^422

Atthi ajjhatto, atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho. Atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, ^428

atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^429

42. Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^430

Duvidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto. Atthi na hetusahetuko, ^431

atthi na hetuahetuko. Atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. Atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. Atthi ^432

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^461

Ubhatovaḍḍhakaṃ. ^516

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^518

Aparopi sattavidhena vedanākkhandho – atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi ^519

bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi ^520

arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena vedanākkhandho. ^521

Catuvīsatividhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^522

akusalo, atthi abyākato; sotasamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā ^523

vedanākkhandho…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā ^524

vedanākkhandho…pe… manosamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi ^525

abyākato; cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ghānasamphassajā vedanā, ^526

jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena ^527

vedanākkhandho. ^528

Aparopi catuvīsatividhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi ^529

vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, ^530

cakkhusamphassajā vedanā…pe… manosamphassajā vedanā; sotasamphassapaccayā ^531

vedanākkhandho…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā ^532

vedanākkhandho…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… manosamphassapaccayā ^533

vedanākkhandho atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^534

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, cakkhusamphassajā vedanā…pe… manosamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ ^535

catuvīsatividhena vedanākkhandho. ^536

Tiṃsavidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, ^537

atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^538

ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^539

manosamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^540

apariyāpanno; cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ghānasamphassajā vedanā, ^541

jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ tiṃsavidhena ^542

vedanākkhandho. ^543

Bahuvidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^544

akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^545

sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^546

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, ^547

atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^548

cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā ^549

vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ bahuvidhena vedanākkhandho. ^550

Aparopi bahuvidhena vedanākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho atthi ^551

vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^552

kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; sotasamphassapaccayā ^553

vedanākkhandho…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā ^554

vedanākkhandho…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā vedanākkhandho…pe… manosamphassapaccayā ^555

vedanākkhandho atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^556

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^557

cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā ^558

vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā vedanā. Evaṃ bahuvidhena vedanākkhandho. ^559

Ayaṃ vuccati vedanākkhandho. ^560

3. Saññākkhandho ^561

62. Tattha katamo saññākkhandho? Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^562

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^563

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato. ^564

Catubbidhena saññākkhandho – atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^565

apariyāpanno. ^566

Pañcavidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhindriyasampayutto, atthi dukkhindriyasampayutto, atthi ^567

somanassindriyasampayutto, atthi domanassindriyasampayutto, atthi upekkhindriyasampayutto. Evaṃ ^568

pañcavidhena saññākkhandho. ^569

Chabbidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ^570

ghānasamphassajā saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manosamphassajā saññā. ^571

Evaṃ chabbidhena saññākkhandho. ^572

Sattavidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ^573

ghānasamphassajā saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manodhātusamphassajā ^574

saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā. Evaṃ sattavidhena saññākkhandho. ^575

Aṭṭhavidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… kāyasamphassajā saññā atthi ^576

sukhasahagatā, atthi dukkhasahagatā, manodhātusamphassajā saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā ^577

saññā. Evaṃ aṭṭhavidhena saññākkhandho. ^578

Navavidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… kāyasamphassajā saññā, ^579

manodhātusamphassajā saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi ^580

abyākatā. Evaṃ navavidhena saññākkhandho. ^581

Dasavidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… kāyasamphassajā saññā atthi ^582

sukhasahagatā, atthi dukkhasahagatā, manodhātusamphassajā saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā ^583

saññā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi abyākatā. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^584

63. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^585

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^586

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^587

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^588

64. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^589

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^590

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, atthi ^591

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^592

anupādinnaanupādāniyo. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^593

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi avitakkaavicāro. ^594

Atthi pītisahagato, atthi sukhasahagato, atthi upekkhāsahagato. Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi ^595

bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, ^596

atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Atthi ^597

ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Atthi sekkho, atthi asekkho, atthi ^598

nevasekkhanāsekkho. Atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo. Atthi parittārammaṇo, atthi ^599

mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi appamāṇārammaṇo. Atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi paṇīto. Atthi ^600

micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi maggahetuko, atthi ^601

maggādhipati. Atthi uppanno, atthi anuppanno, atthi uppādī. Atthi atīto, atthi anāgato, atthi ^602

paccuppanno. Atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi paccuppannārammaṇo. Atthi ajjhatto, ^603

atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho. Atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^604

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe… evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^605

65. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^606

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto. Atthi na hetusahetuko, ^607

atthi na hetuahetuko. Atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. Atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. Atthi ^608

sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. Atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. Atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi ^609

āsavavippayuttaanāsavo. Atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. Atthi saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi ^610

saṃyojanavippayutto. Atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. ^611

Atthi ganthaniyo, atthi aganthaniyo. Atthi ganthasampayutto, atthi ganthavippayutto. Atthi ^612

ganthavippayuttaganthaniyo, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyo. Atthi oghaniyo, atthi anoghaniyo. Atthi ^613

oghasampayutto, atthi oghavippayutto. Atthi oghavippayuttaoghaniyo, atthi oghavippayuttaanoghaniyo. ^614

Atthi yoganiyo, atthi ayoganiyo. Atthi yogasampayutto, atthi yogavippayutto. Atthi ^615

yogavippayuttayoganiyo, atthi yogavippayuttaayoganiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇiyo, atthi anīvaraṇiyo. Atthi ^616

nīvaraṇasampayutto, atthi nīvaraṇavippayutto. Atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyo, atthi ^617

nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyo. Atthi parāmaṭṭho, atthi aparāmaṭṭho. Atthi parāmāsasampayutto, atthi ^618

parāmāsavippayutto. Atthi parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭho, atthi parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭho. Atthi ^619

upādinno, atthi anupādinno. Atthi upādāniyo, atthi anupādāniyo. Atthi upādānasampayutto, atthi ^620

upādānavippayutto. Atthi upādānavippayuttaupādāniyo, atthi upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyo. Atthi ^621

saṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkilesiko. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭho, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭho. Atthi kilesasampayutto, atthi ^622

kilesavippayutto. Atthi kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesiko, atthi kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi dassanena ^623

pahātabbo, atthi na dassanena pahātabbo. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. ^624

Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, atthi na dassanena pahātabbahetuko. Atthi bhāvanāya ^625

pahātabbahetuko, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Atthi savitakko, atthi avitakko. Atthi savicāro, ^626

atthi avicāro. Atthi sappītiko, atthi appītiko. Atthi pītisahagato, atthi na pītisahagato. Atthi ^627

sukhasahagato, atthi na sukhasahagato. Atthi upekkhāsahagato, atthi na upekkhāsahagato. Atthi ^628

kāmāvacaro, atthi na kāmāvacaro. Atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi na rūpāvacaro. Atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi na ^629

arūpāvacaro. Atthi pariyāpanno, atthi apariyāpanno. Atthi niyyāniko, atthi aniyyāniko. Atthi niyato, atthi ^630

aniyato. Atthi sauttaro, atthi anuttaro. Atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^631

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^632

saññākkhandho. ^633

66. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^634

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^635

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^636

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, ^637

atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^638

(Yathā kusalattike vitthāro, evaṃ sabbepi tikā vitthāretabbā.) ^639

Dukamūlakaṃ. ^640

67. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^641

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^642

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^643

saññākkhandho. ^644

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto…pe… atthi saraṇo, atthi ^646

araṇo. ^647

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^648

saññākkhandho. ^649

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^651

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^652

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, ^653

atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^654

70. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^655

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto…pe… atthi saraṇo, atthi ^656

araṇo. ^657

Tividhena saññākkhandho…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^658

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^659

Tikamūlakaṃ. ^660

71. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^661

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^662

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^663

saññākkhandho. ^664

72. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^665

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto. ^666

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^667

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^668

73. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^669

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi na hetu sahetuko, atthi na hetu ahetuko. ^670

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, atthi ^671

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^672

74. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^673

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. ^674

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^675

anupādinnaanupādāniyo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^676

75. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^677

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. ^678

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^679

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^680

76. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^681

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. ^682

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi ^683

avitakkaavicāro…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^684

77. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^685

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. ^686

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi pītisahagato, atthi sukhasahagato, atthi upekkhāsahagato…pe…. ^687

Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^688

78. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^689

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavo. ^690

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva ^691

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^692

79. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^693

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. ^694

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, ^695

atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^696

80. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^697

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi saṃyojanavippayutto. ^698

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi ^699

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^700

81. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^701

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi ^702

saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. ^703

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi sekkho, atthi asekkho, atthi nevasekkhanāsekkho…pe…. Evaṃ ^704

dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^705

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi ganthaniyo, atthi aganthaniyo. ^707

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo…pe…. Evaṃ ^708

dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^709

83. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^710

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi ganthasampayutto, atthi ganthavippayutto. ^711

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi parittārammaṇo, atthi mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi ^712

appamāṇārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^713

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi ganthavippayuttaganthaniyo, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyo. ^715

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi paṇīto…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^716

saññākkhandho. ^717

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi oghaniyo, atthi anoghaniyo. ^719

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato…pe…. ^720

Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^721

86. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^722

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi oghasampayutto, atthi oghavippayutto. ^723

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi maggahetuko, atthi maggādhipati…pe…. ^724

87. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^726

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi oghavippayuttaoghaniyo, atthi oghavippayuttaanoghaniyo. ^727

dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^729

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi atīto, atthi anāgato, atthi paccuppanno…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^732

saññākkhandho. ^733

89. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^734

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi yogasampayutto, atthi yogavippayutto. ^735

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi ^736

paccuppannārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^737

90. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^738

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi yogavippayuttayoganiyo, atthi yogavippayuttaayoganiyo. ^739

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi ajjhatto, atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho…pe…. Evaṃ ^740

dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^741

91. Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^742

Duvidhena saññākkhandho – atthi nīvaraṇiyo, atthi anīvaraṇiyo. ^743

Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^744

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho. ^745

Ubhatovaḍḍhakaṃ. ^746

Sattavidhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^747

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena saññākkhandho. ^748

Aparopi sattavidhena saññākkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya ^749

vedanāya sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^750

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, ^751

atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^752

apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena saññākkhandho. ^753

Catuvīsatividhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^754

akusalo, atthi abyākato; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^755

jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho ^756

atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ^757

ghānasamphassajā saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manosamphassajā saññā. ^758

Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena saññākkhandho. ^759

Aparopi catuvīsatividhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi ^760

sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^761

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… manosamphassajā saññā. ^762

Sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^763

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi sukhāya vedanāya ^764

sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; ^765

cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… manosamphassajā saññā. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena saññākkhandho. ^766

Tiṃsatividhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^767

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^768

ghānasamphassapaccayā …pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^769

manosamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^770

apariyāpanno, cakkhusamphassajā saññā…pe… manosamphassajā saññā. Evaṃ tiṃsatividhena ^771

saññākkhandho. ^772

Bahuvidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^773

akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, ^774

sotasamphassapaccayā …pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^775

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi ^776

abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, cakkhusamphassajā ^777

saññā…pe… manosamphassajā saññā. Evaṃ bahuvidhena saññākkhandho. ^778

Aparopi bahuvidhena saññākkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi sukhāya ^779

vedanāya sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^780

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, ^781

sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^782

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saññākkhandho atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi ^783

bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi ^784

arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ghānasamphassajā ^785

saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manosamphassajā saññā. Evaṃ bahuvidhena ^786

saññākkhandho. ^787

Ayaṃ vuccati saññākkhandho. ^788

4. Saṅkhārakkhandho ^789

92. Tattha katamo saṅkhārakkhandho? Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^790

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. ^791

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato. ^792

Catubbidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^793

apariyāpanno. ^794

Pañcavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sukhindriyasampayutto, atthi dukkhindriyasampayutto, ^795

atthi somanassindriyasampayutto, atthi domanassindriyasampayutto, atthi upekkhindriyasampayutto. ^796

Evaṃ pañcavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^797

Chabbidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ^798

ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manosamphassajā cetanā. ^799

Evaṃ chabbidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^800

Sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ^801

ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^802

cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā. Evaṃ sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^803

Aṭṭhavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… kāyasamphassajā cetanā atthi ^804

sukhasahagatā, atthi dukkhasahagatā, manodhātusamphassajā cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā ^805

cetanā. Evaṃ aṭṭhavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^806

Navavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… manodhātusamphassajā ^807

cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi abyākatā. Evaṃ ^808

navavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^809

Dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… kāyasamphassajā cetanā atthi ^810

sukhasahagatā, atthi dukkhasahagatā, manodhātusamphassajā cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā ^811

cetanā atthi kusalā, atthi akusalā, atthi abyākatā. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^812

93. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^813

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. ^814

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^815

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, ^816

atthi nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^817

anupādinnaanupādāniyo. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^818

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi avitakkaavicāro. ^819

Atthi pītisahagato, atthi sukhasahagato, atthi upekkhāsahagato. Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi ^820

bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, ^821

atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Atthi ^822

ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Atthi sekkho, atthi asekkho, atthi ^823

nevasekkhanāsekkho. Atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo. Atthi parittārammaṇo, atthi ^824

mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi appamāṇārammaṇo. Atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi paṇīto. Atthi ^825

micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi maggahetuko, atthi ^826

maggādhipati. Atthi uppanno, atthi anuppanno, atthi uppādī. Atthi atīto, atthi anāgato, atthi ^827

paccuppanno. Atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi paccuppannārammaṇo. Atthi ajjhatto, ^828

atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho. Atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^829

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^830

94. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^831

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. Atthi hetusampayutto, atthi ^832

hetuvippayutto. Atthi hetu ceva sahetuko ca, atthi sahetuko ceva na ca hetu. Atthi hetu ceva ^833

hetusampayutto ca, atthi hetusampayutto ceva na ca hetu. Atthi na hetu sahetuko, atthi na hetu ahetuko. ^834

Atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. Atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. Atthi āsavo, atthi no ^835

āsavo. Atthi sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. Atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. Atthi āsavo ceva ^836

sāsavo ca, atthi sāsavo ceva no ca āsavo. Atthi āsavo ceva āsavasampayutto ca, atthi āsavasampayutto ^837

ceva no ca āsavo. Atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavo. Atthi saṃyojanaṃ, atthi ^838

no saṃyojanaṃ. Atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. Atthi saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi ^839

saṃyojanavippayutto. Atthi saṃyojanañceva saṃyojaniyo ca, atthi saṃyojaniyo ceva no ca ^840

saṃyojanaṃ. Atthi saṃyojanañceva saṃyojanasampayutto ca, atthi saṃyojanasampayutto ceva no ca ^841

saṃyojanaṃ. Atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. ^842

Atthi gantho, atthi no gantho. Atthi ganthaniyo, atthi aganthaniyo. Atthi ganthasampayutto, atthi ^843

ganthavippayutto. Atthi gantho ceva ganthaniyo ca, atthi ganthaniyo ceva no ca gantho. Atthi gantho ^844

ceva ganthasampayutto ca, atthi ganthasampayutto ceva no ca gantho. Atthi ^845

ganthavippayuttaganthaniyo, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyo. Atthi ogho, atthi no ogho. Atthi ^846

oghaniyo, atthi anoghaniyo. Atthi oghasampayutto, atthi oghavippayutto. Atthi ogho ceva oghaniyo ca, ^847

atthi oghaniyo ceva no ca ogho. Atthi ogho ceva oghasampayutto ca, atthi oghasampayutto ceva no ca ^848

ogho. Atthi oghavippayuttaoghaniyo, atthi oghavippayuttaanoghaniyo. Atthi yogo, atthi no yogo. Atthi ^849

yoganiyo, atthi ayoganiyo. Atthi yogasampayutto, atthi yogavippayutto. Atthi yogo ceva yoganiyo ca, ^850

atthi yoganiyo ceva no ca yogo. Atthi yogo ceva yogasampayutto ca, atthi yogasampayutto ceva no ca ^851

yogo. Atthi yogavippayuttayoganiyo, atthi yogavippayuttaayoganiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇaṃ, atthi no ^852

nīvaraṇaṃ. Atthi nīvaraṇiyo, atthi anīvaraṇiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇasampayutto, atthi nīvaraṇavippayutto. ^853

Atthi nīvaraṇañceva nīvaraṇiyo ca, atthi nīvaraṇiyo ceva no ca nīvaraṇaṃ. Atthi nīvaraṇañceva ^854

nīvaraṇasampayutto ca, atthi nīvaraṇasampayutto ceva no ca nīvaraṇaṃ. Atthi ^855

nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyo, atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyo. ^856

Atthi parāmāso, atthi no parāmāso. Atthi parāmaṭṭho, atthi aparāmaṭṭho. Atthi parāmāsasampayutto, ^857

atthi parāmāsavippayutto. Atthi parāmāso ceva parāmaṭṭho ca, atthi parāmaṭṭho ceva no ca parāmāso. ^858

Atthi parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭho, atthi parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭho. Atthi upādinno, atthi ^859

anupādinno. Atthi upādānaṃ, atthi no upādānaṃ. Atthi upādāniyo, atthi anupādāniyo. Atthi ^860

upādānasampayutto, atthi upādānavippayutto. Atthi upādānañceva upādāniyo ca, atthi upādāniyo ceva ^861

no ca upādānaṃ. Atthi upādānañceva upādānasampayutto ca, atthi upādānasampayutto ceva no ca ^862

upādānaṃ. Atthi upādānavippayuttaupādāniyo, atthi upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyo. ^863

Atthi kileso, atthi no kileso. Atthi saṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkilesiko. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭho, atthi ^864

asaṃkiliṭṭho. Atthi kilesasampayutto, atthi kilesavippayutto. Atthi kileso ceva saṃkilesiko ca, atthi ^865

saṃkilesiko ceva no ca kileso. Atthi kileso ceva saṃkiliṭṭho ca, atthi saṃkiliṭṭho ceva no ca kileso. Atthi ^866

kileso ceva kilesasampayutto ca, atthi kilesasampayutto ceva no ca kileso. Atthi ^867

kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesiko, atthi kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi na ^868

dassanena pahātabbo. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Atthi dassanena ^869

pahātabbahetuko, atthi na dassanena pahātabbahetuko. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi na ^870

bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. ^871

Atthi savitakko, atthi avitakko. Atthi savicāro, atthi avicāro. Atthi sappītiko, atthi appītiko. Atthi ^872

pītisahagato, atthi na pītisahagato. Atthi sukhasahagato, atthi na sukhasahagato. Atthi upekkhāsahagato, ^873

atthi na upekkhāsahagato. Atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi na kāmāvacaro. Atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi na rūpāvacaro. ^874

Atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi na arūpāvacaro. Atthi pariyāpanno, atthi apariyāpanno. Atthi niyyāniko, atthi ^875

aniyyāniko. Atthi niyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi sauttaro, atthi anuttaro. Atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^876

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ ^877

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^878

95. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^879

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^880

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, ^881

atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^882

Dukamūlakaṃ. ^883

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. ^885

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^887

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^889

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ ^890

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^891

98. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^892

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. ^893

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^894

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^895

99. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^896

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^897

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^898

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^899

Tikamūlakaṃ. ^900

100. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^901

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. ^902

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ ^903

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^904

101. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho cittasampayutto. ^905

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^906

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^907

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^908

saṅkhārakkhandho. ^909

102. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^910

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto. ^911

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, atthi ^912

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^913

103. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^914

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu ceva sahetuko ca, atthi sahetuko ceva na ca hetu. ^915

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^916

anupādinnaanupādāniyo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^917

104. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^918

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu ceva hetusampayutto ca, atthi hetusampayutto ceva na ca ^919

hetu. ^920

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^921

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^922

105. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^923

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi na hetu sahetuko, atthi na hetu ahetuko. ^924

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi ^925

avitakkaavicāro…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^926

106. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^927

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. ^928

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi pītisahagato, atthi sukhasahagato, atthi upekkhāsahagato… ^929

pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^930

107. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^931

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. ^932

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva ^933

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^934

108. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^935

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi āsavo, atthi no āsavo. ^936

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, ^937

atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^938

109. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^939

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. ^940

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi ^941

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^942

110. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^943

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. ^944

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sekkho, atthi asekkho, atthi nevasekkhanāsekkho…pe…. ^945

Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^946

111. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^947

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi āsavo ceva sāsavo ca, atthi sāsavo ceva no ca āsavo. ^948

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo…pe…. Evaṃ ^949

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^950

112. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^951

no ca āsavo. ^953

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi parittārammaṇo, atthi mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi ^954

appamāṇārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^955

113. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^956

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, atthi āsavippayuttaanāsavo. ^957

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi paṇīto…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^958

saṅkhārakkhandho. ^959

114. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^960

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojanaṃ, atthi no saṃyojanaṃ. ^961

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato…pe…. ^962

Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^963

115. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^964

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. ^965

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi maggahetuko, atthi maggādhipati… ^966

pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^967

116. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^968

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi saṃyojanavippayutto. ^969

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi uppanno, atthi anuppanno, atthi uppādī…pe…. Evaṃ ^970

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^971

117. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^972

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojanañceva saṃyojaniyo ca, atthi saṃyojaniyo ceva no ^973

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi atīto, atthi anāgato, atthi paccuppanno…pe…. Evaṃ ^975

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^976

118. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^977

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojanañceva saṃyojanasampayutto ca, atthi ^978

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi ^980
