119. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^982

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi ^983

saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. ^984

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi ajjhatto, atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho…pe…. Evaṃ ^985

dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^986

120. Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. ^987

Duvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi gantho, atthi no gantho. ^988

Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^989

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^990

Ubhatovaḍḍhakaṃ. ^991

Sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^992

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^993

Aparopi sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya ^994

vedanāya sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto; atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^995

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo ^996

atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^997

apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^998

Catuvīsatividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi kusalo, ^999

atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1000

jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā ^1001

saṅkhārakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… ^1002

manosamphassajā cetanā. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1003

Aparopi catuvīsatividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi ^1004

sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1005

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… manosamphassajā cetanā; ^1006

sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1007

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi sukhāya vedanāya ^1008

sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; ^1009

cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, ^1010

kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manosamphassajā cetanā. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1011

Tiṃsatividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi ^1012

kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1013

ghānasamphassapaccayā …pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1014

manosamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^1015

apariyāpanno; cakkhusamphassajā cetanā…pe… manosamphassajā cetanā. Evaṃ tiṃsatividhena ^1016

saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1017

Bahuvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^1018

akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^1019

sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1020

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, ^1021

atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^1022

cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, ^1023

kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manosamphassajā cetanā. Evaṃ bahuvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1024

Aparopi bahuvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi ^1025

sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^1026

sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1027

kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1028

ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1029

manosamphassapaccayā saṅkhārakkhandho atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto …pe… atthi ^1030

ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^1031

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ^1032

ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manosamphassajā cetanā. ^1033

Evaṃ bahuvidhena saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1034

Ayaṃ vuccati saṅkhārakkhandho. ^1035

5. Viññāṇakkhandho ^1036

121. Tattha katamo viññāṇakkhandho? Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1037

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^1038

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato. ^1039

Catubbidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^1040

apariyāpanno. ^1041

Pañcavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sukhindriyasampayutto, atthi dukkhindriyasampayutto, ^1042

atthi somanassindriyasampayutto, atthi domanassindriyasampayutto, atthi upekkhindriyasampayutto. ^1043

Evaṃ pañcavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1044

Chabbidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, ^1045

jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ chabbidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1046

Sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1047

kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu. Evaṃ sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1048

Aṭṭhavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, ^1049

jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manodhātu, ^1050

manoviññāṇadhātu. Evaṃ aṭṭhavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1051

Navavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, ^1052

jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi ^1053

abyākataṃ. Evaṃ navavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1054

Dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ…pe… kāyaviññāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, ^1055

atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ. ^1056

Evaṃ dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1057

122. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1058

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^1059

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^1060

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko, atthi vipākadhammadhammo, ^1061

atthi nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Atthi upādinnupādāniyo, atthi anupādinnupādāniyo, atthi ^1062

anupādinnaanupādāniyo. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko, atthi ^1063

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi savitakkasavicāro, atthi avitakkavicāramatto, atthi avitakkaavicāro. ^1064

Atthi pītisahagato, atthi sukhasahagato, atthi upekkhāsahagato. Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi ^1065

bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Atthi dassanena pahātabbahetuko, ^1066

atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Atthi ^1067

ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Atthi sekkho, atthi asekkho, atthi ^1068

nevasekkhanāsekkho. Atthi paritto, atthi mahaggato, atthi appamāṇo. Atthi parittārammaṇo, atthi ^1069

mahaggatārammaṇo, atthi appamāṇārammaṇo. Atthi hīno, atthi majjhimo, atthi paṇīto. Atthi ^1070

micchattaniyato, atthi sammattaniyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi maggārammaṇo, atthi maggahetuko, atthi ^1071

maggādhipati. Atthi uppanno, atthi anuppanno, atthi uppādī. Atthi atīto, atthi anāgato, atthi ^1072

paccuppanno. Atthi atītārammaṇo, atthi anāgatārammaṇo, atthi paccuppannārammaṇo. Atthi ajjhatto, ^1073

atthi bahiddho, atthi ajjhattabahiddho. Atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1074

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1075

123. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1076

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto. Atthi na hetu sahetuko, ^1077

atthi na hetu ahetuko. Atthi lokiyo, atthi lokuttaro. Atthi kenaci viññeyyo, atthi kenaci na viññeyyo. ^1078

Atthi sāsavo, atthi anāsavo. Atthi āsavasampayutto, atthi āsavavippayutto. Atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavo, ^1079

atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavo. Atthi saṃyojaniyo, atthi asaṃyojaniyo. Atthi saṃyojanasampayutto, atthi ^1080

saṃyojanavippayutto. Atthi saṃyojanavippayuttasaṃyojaniyo, atthi saṃyojanavippayuttaasaṃyojaniyo. ^1081

Atthi ganthaniyo, atthi aganthaniyo. Atthi ganthasampayutto, atthi ganthavippayutto. Atthi ^1082

ganthavippayuttaganthaniyo, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyo. Atthi oghaniyo, atthi anoghaniyo. Atthi ^1083

oghasampayutto, atthi oghavippayutto. Atthi oghavippayuttaoghaniyo, atthi oghavippayuttaanoghaniyo. ^1084

Atthi yoganiyo, atthi ayoganiyo. Atthi yogasampayutto, atthi yogavippayutto. Atthi ^1085

yogavippayuttayoganiyo, atthi yogavippayuttaayoganiyo. Atthi nīvaraṇiyo, atthi anīvaraṇiyo. Atthi ^1086

nīvaraṇasampayutto, atthi nīvaraṇavippayutto. Atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyo, atthi ^1087

nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyo. ^1088

Atthi parāmaṭṭho, atthi aparāmaṭṭho. Atthi parāmāsasampayutto, atthi parāmāsavippayutto. Atthi ^1089

parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭho, atthi parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭho. Atthi upādinno, atthi anupādinno. ^1090

Atthi upādāniyo, atthi anupādāniyo. Atthi upādānasampayutto, atthi upādānavippayutto. Atthi ^1091

upādānavippayuttaupādāniyo, atthi upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyo. Atthi saṃkilesiko, atthi ^1092

asaṃkilesiko. Atthi saṃkiliṭṭho, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭho. Atthi kilesasampayutto, atthi kilesavippayutto. Atthi ^1093

kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesiko, atthi kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesiko. Atthi dassanena pahātabbo, atthi na ^1094

dassanena pahātabbo. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbo, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Atthi dassanena ^1095

pahātabbahetuko, atthi na dassanena pahātabbahetuko. Atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko, atthi na ^1096

bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. ^1097

Atthi savitakko, atthi avitakko. Atthi savicāro, atthi avicāro. Atthi sappītiko, atthi appītiko. Atthi ^1098

pītisahagato, atthi na pītisahagato. Atthi sukhasahagato, atthi na sukhasahagato. Atthi upekkhāsahagato, ^1099

atthi na upekkhāsahagato. Atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi na kāmāvacaro. Atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi na rūpāvacaro. ^1100

Atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi na arūpāvacaro, atthi pariyāpanno, atthi apariyāpanno. Atthi niyyāniko, atthi ^1101

aniyyāniko. Atthi niyato, atthi aniyato. Atthi sauttaro, atthi anuttaro. Atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^1102

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^1103

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1104

124. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1105

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi saraṇo, atthi araṇo. ^1106

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^1107

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, ^1108

atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1109

Dukamūlakaṃ. ^1110

125. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1111

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^1112

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^1113

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1114

126. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1115

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto…pe… atthi saraṇo, atthi ^1116

araṇo. ^1117

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena ^1118

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1119

127. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1120

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. ^1121

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^1122

sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto. Atthi vipāko…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, ^1123

atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1124

128. Ekavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – phassasampayutto. ^1125

Duvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi hetusampayutto, atthi hetuvippayutto…pe… atthi saraṇo, atthi ^1126

araṇo. ^1127

Tividhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1128

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo…pe…. Evaṃ dasavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1129

Tikamūlakaṃ. ^1130

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1134

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1139

Ubhatovaḍḍhakaṃ. ^1218

Sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^1219

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1220

Aparopi sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya ^1221

vedanāya sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi ^1222

rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, ^1223

atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^1224

apariyāpanno. Evaṃ sattavidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1225

Catuvīsatividhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi kusalo, ^1226

atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1227

jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā ^1228

viññāṇakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato; cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ^1229

ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena ^1230

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1231

Aparopi catuvīsatividhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi ^1232

sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya sampayutto, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^1233

sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; ^1234

cakkhuviññāṇaṃ…pe… kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1235

ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1236

manosamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1237

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo; cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1238

kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ catuvīsatividhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1239

Tiṃsatividhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, ^1240

atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; sotasamphassapaccayā …pe… ^1241

ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1242

manosamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi ^1243

apariyāpanno; cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, ^1244

manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ tiṃsatividhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1245

Bahuvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi ^1246

akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, ^1247

cakkhuviññāṇaṃ…pe… manoviññāṇaṃ; sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1248

jivhāsamphassapaccayā …pe… kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā ^1249

viññāṇakkhandho atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, atthi abyākato, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi ^1250

arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno, cakkhuviññāṇaṃ…pe… manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ bahuvidhena ^1251

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1252

Aparopi bahuvidhena viññāṇakkhandho – cakkhusamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi ^1253

sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1254

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; ^1255

sotasamphassapaccayā…pe… ghānasamphassapaccayā…pe… jivhāsamphassapaccayā…pe… ^1256

kāyasamphassapaccayā…pe… manosamphassapaccayā viññāṇakkhandho atthi sukhāya vedanāya ^1257

sampayutto…pe… atthi ajjhattārammaṇo, atthi bahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇo, atthi ^1258

kāmāvacaro, atthi rūpāvacaro, atthi arūpāvacaro, atthi apariyāpanno; cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ^1259

ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ bahuvidhena viññāṇakkhandho. ^1260

Ayaṃ vuccati viññāṇakkhandho. ^1261

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^1262

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^1263

150. Pañcakkhandhā – rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^1264

viññāṇakkhandho. ^1265

151. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā? ^1266

1. Tikaṃ ^1267

152. Rūpakkhandho abyākato. Cattāro khandhā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Dve ^1268

khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ^1269

‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi. Tayo khandhā siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā ^1270

dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. Rūpakkhandho ^1271

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo. Cattāro khandhā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā ^1272

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā. Rūpakkhandho siyā upādinnupādāniyo, siyā ^1273

anupādinnupādāniyo. Cattāro khandhā siyā upādinnupādāniyā, siyā anupādinnupādāniyā, siyā ^1274

anupādinnaanupādāniyā. ^1275

Rūpakkhandho asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesiko. Cattāro khandhā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā, siyā ^1276

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Rūpakkhandho avitakkaavicāro. Tayo khandhā ^1277

siyā savitakkasavicārā, siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā avitakkaavicārā. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā ^1278

savitakkasavicāro, siyā avitakkavicāramatto, siyā avitakkaavicāro, siyā na vattabbo – ^1279

‘‘savitakkasavicāro’’tipi, ‘‘avitakkavicāramatto’’tipi, ‘‘avitakkaavicāro’’tipi. Rūpakkhandho na vattabbo ^1280

– ‘‘pītisahagato’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagato’’tipi, ‘‘upekkhāsahagato’’tipi. Vedanākkhandho siyā pītisahagato ^1281

na sukhasahagato na upekkhāsahagato, siyā na vattabbo – ‘‘pītisahagato’’ti. Tayo khandhā siyā ^1282

pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ^1283

‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘upekkhāsahagatā’’tipi. ^1284

Rūpakkhandho neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Cattāro khandhā siyā dassanena ^1285

pahātabbā, siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Rūpakkhandho ^1286

neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetuko. Cattāro khandhā siyā dassanena pahātabbahetukā, siyā ^1287

bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā, siyā neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Rūpakkhandho ^1288

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmī. Cattāro khandhā siyā ācayagāmino, siyā apacayagāmino, siyā ^1289

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Rūpakkhandho nevasekkhanāsekkho. Cattāro khandhā siyā sekkhā, siyā ^1290

asekkhā, siyā nevasekkhanāsekkhā. Rūpakkhandho paritto. Cattāro khandhā siyā parittā, siyā ^1291

mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā. Rūpakkhandho anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā siyā parittārammaṇā, siyā ^1292

mahaggatārammaṇā, siyā appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parittārammaṇā’’tipi, ^1293

‘‘mahaggatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘appamāṇārammaṇā’’tipi. Rūpakkhandho majjhimo. Cattāro khandhā siyā ^1294

hīnā, siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā. Rūpakkhandho aniyato. Cattāro khandhā siyā micchattaniyatā, siyā ^1295

sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. ^1296

Rūpakkhandho anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā siyā maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahetukā, siyā ^1297

maggādhipatino, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘maggārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘maggahetukā’’tipi, ‘‘maggādhipatino’’tipi; ^1298

siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino; siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. Rūpakkhandho ^1299

anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā siyā atītārammaṇā, siyā anāgatārammaṇā, siyā paccuppannārammaṇā, ^1300

siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘atītārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘anāgatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘paccuppannārammaṇā’’tipi; siyā ^1301

ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. Rūpakkhandho anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā siyā ^1302

ajjhattārammaṇā, siyā bahiddhārammaṇā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ^1303

‘‘ajjhattārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘bahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi. Cattāro khandhā ^1304

anidassanaappaṭighā. Rūpakkhandho siyā sanidassanasappaṭigho, siyā anidassanasappaṭigho, siyā ^1305

anidassanaappaṭigho. ^1306

2. Dukaṃ ^1307

153. Cattāro khandhā na hetū. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā hetu, siyā na hetu. Rūpakkhandho ahetuko. ^1308

Cattāro khandhā siyā sahetukā, siyā ahetukā. Rūpakkhandho hetuvippayutto. Cattāro khandhā siyā ^1309

hetusampayuttā, siyā hetuvippayuttā. Rūpakkhandho na vattabbo – ‘‘hetu ceva sahetuko cā’’tipi, ^1310

‘‘sahetuko ceva na ca hetū’’tipi. Tayo khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘hetū ceva sahetukā cā’’ti, siyā sahetukā ^1311

ceva na ca hetū, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘sahetukā ceva na ca hetū’’ti. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā hetu ceva ^1312

sahetuko ca, siyā sahetuko ceva na ca hetu, siyā na vattabbo – ‘‘hetu ceva sahetuko cā’’tipi, ‘‘sahetuko ^1313

ceva na ca hetū’’tipi. Rūpakkhandho na vattabbo – ‘‘hetu ceva hetusampayutto cā’’tipi, ^1314

‘‘hetusampayutto ceva na ca hetū’’tipi. Tayo khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cā’’ti, ^1315

siyā hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū’’ti. ^1316

Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā hetu ceva hetusampayutto ca, siyā hetusampayutto ceva na ca hetu, siyā na ^1317

vattabbo – ‘‘hetu ceva hetusampayutto cā’’tipi, ‘‘hetusampayutto ceva na ca hetū’’tipi. Rūpakkhandho ^1318

hetu sahetuko, siyā na hetu ahetuko, siyā na vattabbo – ‘‘na hetu sahetuko’’tipi, ‘‘na hetu ahetuko’’tipi. ^1320

Sappaccayā, saṅkhatā. ^1321

Cattāro khandhā anidassanā. Rūpakkhandho siyā sanidassano, siyā anidassano. Cattāro khandhā ^1322

Cattāro khandhā no āsavā. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā āsavo, siyā no āsavo. Rūpakkhandho sāsavo. ^1326

Cattāro khandhā siyā sāsavā, siyā anāsavā. Rūpakkhandho āsavavippayutto. Cattāro khandhā siyā ^1327

sāsavo ceva no ca āsavo, siyā na vattabbo – ‘‘āsavo ceva sāsavo cā’’tipi, ‘‘sāsavo ceva no ca āsavo’’tipi. ^1331

Rūpakkhandho na vattabbo – ‘‘āsavo ceva āsavasampayutto cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayutto ceva no ca ^1332

no ca āsavā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavā’’ti. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā āsavo ^1334

āsavasampayutto cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayutto ceva no ca āsavo’’tipi. Rūpakkhandho ^1336

Cattāro khandhā no saṃyojanā. Saṅkhārakkhandho siyā saṃyojanaṃ, siyā no saṃyojanaṃ. ^1339

Viññāṇakkhandho no cittasamuṭṭhāno. Rūpakkhandho siyā cittasamuṭṭhāno, siyā no cittasamuṭṭhāno. ^1404

Rūpakkhandho na dassanena pahātabbo. Cattāro khandhā siyā dassanena pahātabbā, siyā na ^1449

dassanena pahātabbā. Rūpakkhandho na bhāvanāya pahātabbo. Cattāro khandhā siyā bhāvanāya ^1450

2. Āyatanavibhaṅgo ^1469

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^1470

154. Dvādasāyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, ^1471

gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, ^1472

dhammāyatanaṃ. ^1473

Cakkhuṃ aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ. Rūpā aniccā dukkhā anattā ^1474

vipariṇāmadhammā. Sotaṃ aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ. Saddā aniccā dukkhā anattā ^1475

vipariṇāmadhammā. Ghānaṃ aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ. Gandhā aniccā dukkhā ^1476

anattā vipariṇāmadhammā. Jivhā aniccā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā. Rasā aniccā dukkhā anattā ^1477

vipariṇāmadhammā. Kāyo anicco dukkho anattā vipariṇāmadhammo. Phoṭṭhabbā aniccā dukkhā anattā ^1478

vipariṇāmadhammā. Mano anicco dukkho anattā vipariṇāmadhammo. Dhammā aniccā dukkhā anattā ^1479

vipariṇāmadhammā. ^1480

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^1481

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^1482

155. Dvādasāyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, ^1483

manāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, ^1484

dhammāyatanaṃ. ^1485

156. Tattha katamaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cakkhu [cakkhuṃ (sī. syā. ka.) dha. sa. 596-599] ^1486

catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo attabhāvapariyāpanno anidassano sappaṭigho, yena cakkhunā ^1487

anidassanena sappaṭighena rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ passi vā passati vā passissati vā passe ^1488

vā, cakkhumpetaṃ cakkhāyatanampetaṃ cakkhudhātupesā cakkhundriyampetaṃ lokopeso dvārāpesā ^1489

samuddopeso paṇḍarampetaṃ khettampetaṃ vatthumpetaṃ nettampetaṃ nayanampetaṃ orimaṃ ^1490

tīrampetaṃ suñño gāmopeso. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cakkhāyatanaṃ’’. ^1491

157. Tattha katamaṃ sotāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ sotaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo ^1492

attabhāvapariyāpanno anidassano sappaṭigho, yena sotena anidassanena sappaṭighena saddaṃ ^1493

anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ suṇi vā suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇe vā, sotampetaṃ sotāyatanampetaṃ ^1494

sotadhātupesā sotindriyampetaṃ lokopeso dvārāpesā samuddopeso paṇḍarampetaṃ khettampetaṃ ^1495

vatthumpetaṃ orimaṃ tīrampetaṃ suñño gāmopeso. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sotāyatanaṃ’’. ^1496

158. Tattha katamaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ ghānaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo ^1497

attabhāvapariyāpanno anidassano sappaṭigho, yena ghānena anidassanena sappaṭighena gandhaṃ ^1498

anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāye vā, ghānampetaṃ ^1499

ghānāyatanampetaṃ ghānadhātupesā ghānindriyampetaṃ lokopeso dvārāpesā samuddopeso ^1500

paṇḍarampetaṃ khettampetaṃ vatthumpetaṃ orimaṃ tīrampetaṃ suñño gāmopeso. Idaṃ vuccati ^1501

‘‘ghānāyatanaṃ’’. ^1502

159. Tattha katamaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? Yā jivhā catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo ^1503

attabhāvapariyāpanno anidassano sappaṭigho, yāya jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya rasaṃ ^1504

anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāye vā, jivhāpesā jivhāyatanampetaṃ ^1505

jivhādhātupesā jīvhindriyampetaṃ lokopeso dvārāpesā samuddopeso paṇḍarampetaṃ khettampetaṃ ^1506

vatthumpetaṃ orimaṃ tīrampetaṃ suñño gāmopeso. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘jivhāyatanaṃ’’. ^1507

160. Tattha katamaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? Yo kāyo catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo ^1508

attabhāvapariyāpanno anidassano sappaṭigho, yena kāyena anidassanena sappaṭighena phoṭṭhabbaṃ ^1509

anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā phuse vā, kāyopeso kāyāyatanampetaṃ ^1510

kāyadhātupesā kāyindriyampetaṃ lokopeso dvārāpesā samuddopeso paṇḍarampetaṃ khettampetaṃ ^1511

vatthumpetaṃ orimaṃ tīrampetaṃ suñño gāmopeso. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘kāyāyatanaṃ’’. ^1512

161. Tattha katamaṃ manāyatanaṃ? Ekavidhena manāyatanaṃ – phassasampayuttaṃ. ^1513

Duvidhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi sahetukaṃ, atthi ahetukaṃ. ^1514

Tividhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ. ^1515

Catubbidhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi kāmāvacaraṃ, atthi rūpāvacaraṃ, atthi arūpāvacaraṃ, atthi ^1516

apariyāpannaṃ. ^1517

Pañcavidhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi sukhindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi dukkhindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi ^1518

somanassindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi domanassindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi upekkhindriyasampayuttaṃ. ^1519

Chabbidhena manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1520

kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ. Evaṃ chabbidhena manāyatanaṃ. ^1521

Sattavidhena manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1522

kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu. Evaṃ sattavidhena manāyatanaṃ. ^1523

Aṭṭhavidhena manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1524

kāyaviññāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu. Evaṃ ^1525

aṭṭhavidhena manāyatanaṃ. ^1526

Navavidhena manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1527

kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ. Evaṃ ^1528

navavidhena manāyatanaṃ. ^1529

Dasavidhena manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, ^1530

kāyaviññāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu atthi ^1531

kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ. Evaṃ dasavidhena manāyatanaṃ. ^1532

Ekavidhena manāyatanaṃ – phassasampayuttaṃ. ^1533

Duvidhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi sahetukaṃ, atthi ahetukaṃ. ^1534

Tividhena manāyatanaṃ – atthi sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, atthi dukkhāya vedanāya ^1535

sampayuttaṃ, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ…pe…. Evaṃ bahuvidhena ^1536

manāyatanaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘manāyatanaṃ’’. ^1537

162. Tattha katamaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā ^1538

sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lohitakaṃ [pītaṃ lohitaṃ (sī.)] odātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañjiṭṭhakaṃ ^1539

[mañjeṭṭhakaṃ (sī. syā.)] hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ ^1540

parimaṇḍalaṃ caturassaṃ chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ soḷasaṃsaṃ ninnaṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapo āloko ^1541

andhakāro abbhā mahikā dhūmo rajo candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa [suriyamaṇḍalassa ^1542

(sī. syā. kaṃ.)] vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā ^1543

maṇisaṅkhamuttaveḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ ^1544

catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ ^1545

sappaṭighaṃ cakkhunā anidassanena sappaṭighena passi vā passati vā passissati vā passe vā, ^1546

rūpampetaṃ rūpāyatanampetaṃ rūpadhātupesā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpāyatanaṃ’’. ^1547

163. Tattha katamaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? Yo saddo catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano ^1548

sappaṭigho bherisaddo mudiṅgasaddo [mutiṅgasaddho (sī.)] saṅkhasaddo paṇavasaddo gītasaddo ^1549

vāditasaddo sammasaddo pāṇisaddo sattānaṃ nigghosasaddo dhātūnaṃ sannighātasaddo vātasaddo ^1550

udakasaddo manussasaddo amanussasaddo, yo vā panaññopi atthi saddo catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ ^1551

upādāya anidassano sappaṭigho, yaṃ saddaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sotena anidassanena ^1552

sappaṭighena suṇi vā suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇe vā, saddopeso saddāyatanampetaṃ saddadhātupesā. ^1553

Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saddāyatanaṃ’’. ^1554

164. Tattha katamaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? Yo gandho catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano ^1555

sappaṭigho mūlagandho sāragandho tacagandho pattagandho pupphagandho phalagandho āmagandho ^1556

vissagandho sugandho duggandho, yo vā panaññopi atthi gandho catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya ^1557

anidassano sappaṭigho, yaṃ gandhaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghānena anidassanena sappaṭighena ^1558

ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāye vā, gandhopeso gandhāyatanampetaṃ gandhadhātupesā. Idaṃ ^1559

vuccati ‘‘gandhāyatanaṃ’’. ^1560

165. Tattha katamaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? Yo raso catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano ^1561

sappaṭigho mūlaraso khandharaso tacaraso pattaraso puppharaso phalaraso ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ ^1562

kaṭukaṃ loṇikaṃ [lapilakaṃ (sī.), lampikaṃ (ka. sī.)] khārikaṃ lambikaṃ kasāvo sādu asādu, yo vā ^1563

panaññopi atthi raso catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano sappaṭigho, yaṃ rasaṃ anidassanaṃ ^1564

sappaṭighaṃ jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāye vā, rasopeso ^1565

rasāyatanampetaṃ rasadhātupesā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rasāyatanaṃ’’. ^1566

166. Tattha katamaṃ phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? Pathavīdhātu tejodhātu vāyodhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ ^1567

saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yaṃ phoṭṭhabbaṃ ^1568

anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ kāyena anidassanena sappaṭighena phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā ^1569

phuse vā, phoṭṭhabbopeso phoṭṭhabbāyatanampetaṃ phoṭṭhabbadhātupesā. Idaṃ vuccati ^1570

‘‘phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ’’. ^1571

167. Tattha katamaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^1572

yañca rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu. ^1573

Tattha katamo vedanākkhandho? Ekavidhena vedanākkhandho – phassasampayutto. Duvidhena ^1574

vedanākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi ^1575

akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe… evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho…pe… evaṃ bahuvidhena ^1576

vedanākkhandho. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanākkhandho’’. ^1577

Tattha katamo saññākkhandho? Ekavidhena saññākkhandho – phassasampayutto. Duvidhena ^1578

saññākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. Tividhena saññākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, ^1579

atthi abyākato…pe… evaṃ dasavidhena saññākkhandho…pe… evaṃ bahuvidhena saññākkhandho. ^1580

Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saññākkhandho’’. ^1581

Tattha katamo saṅkhārakkhandho? Ekavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho – cittasampayutto. Duvidhena ^1582

saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi hetu, atthi na hetu. Tividhena saṅkhārakkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi akusalo, ^1583

atthi abyākato…pe… evaṃ dasavidhena saṅkhārakkhandho…pe… evaṃ bahuvidhena ^1584

saṅkhārakkhandho. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārakkhandho’’. ^1585

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ? Itthindriyaṃ ^1586

purisindriyaṃ…pe… kabaḷīkāro āhāro. Idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ ‘‘anidassanaappaṭighaṃ ^1587

dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ’’. ^1588

Tattha katamā asaṅkhatā dhātu? Rāgakkhayo, dosakkhayo, mohakkhayo – ayaṃ vuccati ^1589

‘‘asaṅkhatā dhātu’’. ^1590

Idaṃ vuccati dhammāyatanaṃ. ^1591

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^1592

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^1593

168. Dvādasāyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, ^1594

gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, ^1595

dhammāyatanaṃ. ^1596

169. Dvādasannaṃ āyatanānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati ^1597

araṇā? ^1598

1. Tikaṃ ^1599

170. Dasāyatanā abyākatā. Dvāyatanā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Dasāyatanā na ^1600

vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ^1601

‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā’’tipi. Manāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, ^1602

siyā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ. ^1603

Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā ^1604

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘sukhāya vedanāya sampayutta’’ntipi, ^1605

‘‘dukkhāya vedanāya sampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayutta’’ntipi. Dasāyatanā ^1606

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā. Dvāyatanā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā ^1607

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā. ^1608

Pañcāyatanā upādinnupādāniyā. Saddāyatanaṃ anupādinnupādāniyaṃ. Cattāro āyatanā siyā ^1609

upādinnupādāniyā, siyā anupādinnupādāniyā, siyā anupādāniyā. Dvāyatanā siyā upādinnupādāniyā, siyā ^1610

anupādinnupādāniyā, siyā anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Dasāyatanā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā. Dvāyatanā siyā ^1611

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Dasāyatanā ^1612

avitakkaavicārā. Manāyatanaṃ siyā savitakkasavicāraṃ, siyā avitakkavicāramattaṃ, siyā ^1613

avitakkaavicāraṃ. Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā savitakkasavicāraṃ, siyā avitakkavicāramattaṃ, siyā ^1614

avitakkaavicāraṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘savitakkasavicāra’’ntipi, ‘‘avitakkavicāramatta’’ntipi, ^1615

‘‘avitakkaavicāra’’ntipi. Dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ^1616

‘‘upekkhāsahagatā’’tipi. Dvāyatanā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na ^1617

vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘upekkhāsahagatā’’tipi. ^1618

Dasāyatanā neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Dvāyatanā siyā dassanena pahātabbā, siyā ^1619

bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Dasāyatanā neva dassanena na ^1620

bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Dvāyatanā siyā dassanena pahātabbahetukā, siyā bhāvanāya ^1621

pahātabbahetukā, siyā neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Dasāyatanā ^1622

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Dvāyatanā siyā ācayagāmino, siyā apacayagāmino, siyā ^1623

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Dasāyatanā nevasekkhanāsekkhā. Dvāyatanā siyā sekkhā, siyā asekkhā, ^1624

siyā nevasekkhanāsekkhā. Dasāyatanā parittā. Dvāyatanā siyā parittā, siyā mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā. ^1625

Dasāyatanā anārammaṇā. Dvāyatanā siyā parittārammaṇā, siyā mahaggatārammaṇā, siyā ^1626

appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parittārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘mahaggatārammaṇā’’tipi, ^1627

‘‘appamāṇārammaṇā’’tipi. Dasāyatanā majjhimā. Dvāyatanā siyā hīnā, siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā. ^1628

Dasāyatanā aniyatā. Dvāyatanā siyā micchattaniyatā, siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. ^1629

Dasāyatanā anārammaṇā. Dvāyatanā siyā maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahetukā, siyā maggādhipatino, ^1630

siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘maggārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘maggahetukā’’tipi, ‘‘maggādhipatino’’tipi. Pañcāyatanā siyā ^1631

uppannā, siyā uppādino, na vattabbā – ‘‘anuppannā’’ti. Saddāyatanaṃ siyā uppannaṃ, siyā ^1632

anuppannaṃ, na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘uppādī’’ti. Pañcāyatanā siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino. ^1633

Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā uppannaṃ, siyā anuppannaṃ, siyā uppādi, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘uppanna’’ntipi, ^1634

‘‘anuppanna’’ntipi, ‘‘uppādī’’tipi. Ekādasāyatanā siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. ^1635

Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā atītaṃ, siyā anāgataṃ, siyā paccuppannaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘atīta’’ntipi, ^1636

kilesā’’tipi. Manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kileso ceva kilesasampayuttañcā’’ti, siyā ^1781

kilesasampayuttañceva no ca kileso, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilesasampayuttañceva no ca kileso’’ti. ^1782

Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā kileso ceva kilesasampayuttañca, siyā kilesasampayuttañceva no ca kileso, siyā ^1783

na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kileso ceva kilesasampayuttañcā’’tipi, ‘‘kilesasampayuttañceva no ca kileso’’tipi. ^1784

Dasāyatanā kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā. Dvāyatanā siyā kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā, siyā ^1785

kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā’’tipi, ^1786

‘‘kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā’’tipi. ^1787

Dasāyatanā na dassanena pahātabbā. Dvāyatanā siyā dassanena pahātabbā, siyā na dassanena ^1788

pahātabbā. Dasāyatanā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Dvāyatanā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā na ^1789

bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Dasāyatanā na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Dvāyatanā siyā dassanena ^1790

pahātabbahetukā, siyā na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Dasāyatanā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. ^1791

Dvāyatanā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Dasāyatanā avitakkā. ^1792

Dvāyatanā siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Dasāyatanā avicārā. Dvāyatanā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. ^1793

Dasāyatanā appītikā. Dvāyatanā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. Dasāyatanā na pītisahagatā. Dvāyatanā ^1794

siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Dasāyatanā na sukhasahagatā. Dvāyatanā siyā ^1795

sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Dasāyatanā na upekkhāsahagatā. Dvāyatanā siyā ^1796

upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. ^1797

Dasāyatanā kāmāvacarā. Dvāyatanā siyā kāmāvacarā, siyā na kāmāvacarā. Dasāyatanā na ^1798

rūpāvacarā. Dvāyatanā siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā. Dasāyatanā na arūpāvacarā. Dvāyatanā siyā ^1799

arūpāvacarā, siyā na arūpāvacarā. Dasāyatanā pariyāpannā. Dvāyatanā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā ^1800

apariyāpannā. Dasāyatanā aniyyānikā. Dvāyatanā siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā. Dasāyatanā aniyatā. ^1801

Dvāyatanā siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā. Dasāyatanā sauttarā. Dvāyatanā siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā. ^1802

Dasāyatanā araṇā. Dvāyatanā siyā saraṇā, siyā araṇāti. ^1803

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^1804

Āyatanavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^1805

3. Dhātuvibhaṅgo ^1806

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^1807

172. Cha dhātuyo – pathavīdhātu [paṭhavīdhātu (sī. syā.) evamuparipi], āpodhātu, tejodhātu, ^1808

vāyodhātu, ākāsadhātu, viññāṇadhātu. ^1809

173. Tattha katamā pathavīdhātu? Pathavīdhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā. Tattha ^1810

katamā ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu? Yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ ^1811

kakkhaḷabhāvo ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nhāru [nahāru ^1812

(sī.)] aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñjaṃ [aṭṭhimiñjā (sī.)] vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilomakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ ^1813

antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ ^1814

kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvo ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu’. ^1815

Tattha katamā bāhirā pathavīdhātu? Yaṃ bāhiraṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ ^1816

kakkhaḷabhāvo bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – ayo lohaṃ tipu sīsaṃ sajjhaṃ [sajjhu (syā.)] ^1817

muttā maṇi veḷuriyo saṅkho silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lohitaṅko [lohitaṅgo (syā. ka.), lohitako (?)] ^1818

masāragallaṃ tiṇaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sakkharā kaṭhalaṃ [kathalaṃ (ka.)] bhūmi pāsāṇo pabbato, yaṃ vā ^1819

panaññampi atthi bāhiraṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvo bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ ^1820

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘bāhirā pathavīdhātu’. Yā ca ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, ^1821

tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pathavīdhātu’’. ^1822

174. Tattha katamā āpodhātu? Āpodhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā. Tattha katamā ^1823

ajjhattikā āpodhātu? Yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ āpo āpogataṃ sineho sinehagataṃ [sneho snehagataṃ ^1824

(syā.)] bandhanattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ sedo ^1825

medo assu vasā kheḷo siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ āpo ^1826

āpogataṃ sineho sinehagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘ajjhattikā ^1827

āpodhātu’. ^1828

Tattha katamā bāhirā āpodhātu? Yaṃ bāhiraṃ āpo āpogataṃ sineho sinehagataṃ bandhanattaṃ ^1829

rūpassa bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – mūlaraso khandharaso tacaraso pattaraso puppharaso ^1830

phalaraso khīraṃ dadhi sappi navanītaṃ telaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ bhummāni vā udakāni antalikkhāni vā, ^1831

yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi bāhiraṃ āpo āpogataṃ sineho sinehagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā ^1832

anupādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘bāhirā āpodhātu’. Yā ca ajjhattikā āpodhātu yā ca bāhirā āpodhātu, ^1833

tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘āpodhātu’’. ^1834

175. Tattha katamā tejodhātu? Tejodhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā. Tattha katamā ^1835

ajjhattikā tejodhātu? Yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ tejo tejogataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ ^1836

ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – yena ca santappati yena ca jīrīyati yena ca pariḍayhati yena ca ^1837

asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ tejo ^1838

tejogataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘ajjhattikā ^1839

tejodhātu’. ^1840

Tattha katamā bāhirā tejodhātu? Yaṃ bāhiraṃ tejo tejogataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ ^1841

usumagataṃ bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – kaṭṭhaggi palālaggi [sakalikaggi (sabbattha)] tiṇaggi ^1842

gomayaggi thusaggi saṅkāraggi indaggi aggisantāpo sūriyasantāpo kaṭṭhasannicayasantāpo ^1843

tiṇasannicayasantāpo dhaññasannicayasantāpo bhaṇḍasannicayasantāpo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi ^1844

bāhiraṃ tejo tejogataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^1845

‘bāhirā tejodhātu’. Yā ca ajjhattikā tejodhātu yā ca bāhirā tejodhātu, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā ^1846

abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘tejodhātu’’. ^1847

176. Tattha katamā vāyodhātu? Vāyodhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā. Tattha katamā ^1848

ajjhattikā vāyodhātu? Yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ vāyo vāyogataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ ^1849

upādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – uddhaṅgamā vātā adhogamā vātā kucchisayā vātā koṭṭhāsayā [koṭṭhasayā (sī. ^1850

syā.)] vātā aṅgamaṅgānusārino vātā satthakavātā khurakavātā uppalakavātā assāso passāso iti vā, yaṃ vā ^1851

panaññampi atthi ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ vāyo vāyogataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ – ^1852

ayaṃ vuccati ‘ajjhattikā vāyodhātu’. ^1853

Tattha katamā bāhirā vāyodhātu? Yaṃ bāhiraṃ vāyo vāyogataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā ^1854

anupādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – puratthimā vātā pacchimā vātā uttarā vātā dakkhiṇā vātā sarajā vātā arajā ^1855

vātā sītā vātā uṇhā vātā parittā vātā adhimattā vātā kāḷavātā verambhavātā pakkhavātā supaṇṇavātā ^1856

tālavaṇṭavātā vidhūpanavātā, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi bāhiraṃ vāyo vāyogataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa ^1857

bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘bāhirā vāyodhātu’. Yā ca ajjhattikā vāyodhātu yā ca bāhirā ^1858

vāyodhātu, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vāyodhātu’’. ^1859

177. Tattha katamā ākāsadhātu? Ākāsadhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā. Tattha katamā ^1860

ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu? Yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ ākāso ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivaro ^1861

vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ maṃsalohitehi ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ, seyyathidaṃ – kaṇṇacchiddaṃ ^1862

nāsacchiddaṃ mukhadvāraṃ, yena ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ ajjhoharati, yattha ca ^1863

asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ santiṭṭhati, yena ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ adhobhāgaṃ nikkhamati, yaṃ vā ^1864

panaññampi atthi ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ ākāso ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivaro vivaragataṃ ^1865

asamphuṭṭhaṃ maṃsalohitehi ajjhattaṃ upādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu’. ^1866

Tattha katamā bāhirā ākāsadhātu? Yaṃ bāhiraṃ ākāso ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivaro ^1867

vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtehi bahiddhā anupādinnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘bāhirā ^1868

ākāsadhātu’. Yā ca ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu yā ca bāhirā ākāsadhātu, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā ^1869

abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ākāsadhātu’’. ^1870

178. Tattha katamā viññāṇadhātu? Cakkhuviññāṇadhātu, sotaviññāṇadhātu, ghānaviññāṇadhātu, ^1871

jivhāviññāṇadhātu, kāyaviññāṇadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇadhātu’’. ^1872

Imā cha dhātuyo. ^1873

179. Aparāpi cha dhātuyo – sukhadhātu, dukkhadhātu, somanassadhātu, domanassadhātu, ^1874

upekkhādhātu, avijjādhātu. ^1875

180. Tattha katamā sukhadhātu? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ ^1876

sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhadhātu’’. ^1877

Tattha katamā dukkhadhātu? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ ^1878

dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘dukkhadhātu’’. ^1879

Tattha katamā somanassadhātu? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ ^1880

sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘somanassadhātu’’. ^1881

Tattha katamā domanassadhātu? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^1882

asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘domanassadhātu’’. ^1883

Tattha katamā upekkhādhātu? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^1884

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^1885

‘‘upekkhādhātu’’. ^1886

Tattha katamā avijjādhātu? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayo ananubodho asambodho ^1887

appaṭivedho asaṅgāhaṇā apariyogāhaṇā asamapekkhanā apaccavekkhaṇā apaccakkhakammaṃ ^1888

dummejjhaṃ bālyaṃ asampajaññaṃ moho pamoho sammoho avijjā avijjogho avijjāyogo avijjānusayo ^1889

avijjāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjādhātu’’. ^1890

Imā cha dhātuyo. ^1891

181. Aparāpi cha dhātuyo – kāmadhātu, byāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu, nekkhammadhātu, ^1892

abyāpādadhātu, avihiṃsādhātu. ^1893

182. Tattha katamā kāmadhātu? Kāmapaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā ^1894

cetaso abhiniropanā micchāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ vuccati kāmadhātu. Heṭṭhato avīcinirayaṃ pariyantaṃ ^1895

karitvā uparito paranimmitavasavattī deve anto karitvā yaṃ etasmiṃ antare etthāvacarā ettha ^1896

pariyāpannā khandhadhātuāyatanā rūpā [rūpaṃ (syā.)] vedanā saññā saṅkhārā viññāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^1897

‘‘kāmadhātu’’. ^1898

Tattha katamā byāpādadhātu? Byāpādapaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko…pe… micchāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ ^1899

vuccati ‘‘byāpādadhātu’’. Dasasu vā āghātavatthūsu cittassa āghāto paṭighāto paṭighaṃ paṭivirodho ^1900

kopo pakopo sampakopo doso padoso sampadoso cittassa byāpatti manopadoso kodho kujjhanā ^1901

kujjhitattaṃ doso dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpajjanā virodho paṭivirodho caṇḍikkaṃ asuropo ^1902

anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘byāpādadhātu’’. ^1903

Tattha katamā vihiṃsādhātu? Vihiṃsāpaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko…pe… micchāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ ^1904

vuccati ‘‘vihiṃsādhātu’’. Idhekacco pāṇinā vā leḍḍunā vā daṇḍena vā satthena vā rajjuyā vā ^1905

aññataraññatarena satte viheṭheti, yā evarūpā heṭhanā viheṭhanā hiṃsanā vihiṃsanā rosanā virosanā ^1906

parūpaghāto – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vihiṃsādhātu’’. ^1907

Tattha katamā nekkhammadhātu? Nekkhammapaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko…pe… sammāsaṅkappo ^1908

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘nekkhammadhātu’’. Sabbepi kusalā dhammā ‘‘nekkhammadhātu’’. ^1909

Tattha katamā abyāpādadhātu? Abyāpādapaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko…pe… sammāsaṅkappo – ^1910

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abyāpādadhātu’’. Yā sattesu metti mettāyanā mettāyitattaṃ mettācetovimutti – ayaṃ ^1911

vuccati ‘‘abyāpādadhātu’’. ^1912

Tattha katamā avihiṃsādhātu? Avihiṃsāpaṭisaṃyutto takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā ^1913

cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avihiṃsādhātu’’. Yā sattesu karuṇā karuṇāyanā ^1914

karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācetovimutti – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avihiṃsādhātu’’. ^1915

Imā cha dhātuyo. ^1916

Iti imāni tīṇi chakkāni tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo honti. ^1917

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^1918

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^1919

183. Aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviññāṇadhātu, sotadhātu, saddadhātu, ^1920

sotaviññāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviññāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, ^1921

jivhāviññāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviññāṇadhātu, manodhātu, dhammadhātu, ^1922

manoviññāṇadhātu. ^1923

184. Tattha katamā cakkhudhātu? Yaṃ cakkhu catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo…pe… ^1924

suñño gāmopeso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘cakkhudhātu’’. ^1925

Tattha katamā rūpadhātu? Yaṃ rūpaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā…pe… ^1926

rūpadhātupesā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpadhātu’’. ^1927

Tattha katamā cakkhuviññāṇadhātu? Cakkhuñca paṭicca rūpe ca uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ ^1928

hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho ^1929

tajjācakkhuviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘cakkhuviññāṇadhātu’’. ^1930

Tattha katamā sotadhātu? Yaṃ sotaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo…pe… suñño ^1931

gāmopeso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sotadhātu’’. ^1932

Tattha katamā saddadhātu? Yo saddo catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano sappaṭigho… ^1933

pe… saddadhātupesā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saddadhātu’’. ^1934

Tattha katamā sotaviññāṇadhātu? Sotañca paṭicca sadde ca uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ ^1935

hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho ^1936

tajjāsotaviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sotaviññāṇadhātu’’. ^1937

Tattha katamā ghānadhātu? Yaṃ ghānaṃ catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo…pe… suñño ^1938

gāmopeso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ghānadhātu’’. ^1939

Tattha katamā gandhadhātu? Yo gandho catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano ^1940

sappaṭigho…pe… gandhadhātupesā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘gandhadhātu’’. ^1941

Tattha katamā ghānaviññāṇadhātu? Ghānañca paṭicca gandhe ca uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ ^1942

hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho ^1943

tajjāghānaviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ghānaviññāṇadhātu’’. ^1944

Tattha katamā jivhādhātu? Yā jivhā catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo…pe… suñño ^1945

gāmopeso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jivhādhātu’’. ^1946

Tattha katamā rasadhātu? Yo raso catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassano sappaṭigho…pe… ^1947

rasadhātupesā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘rasadhātu’’. ^1948

Tattha katamā jivhāviññāṇadhātu? Jivhañca paṭicca rase ca uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ ^1949

hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho ^1950

tajjājivhāviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jivhāviññāṇadhātu’’. ^1951

Tattha katamā kāyadhātu? Yo kāyo catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādo…pe… suñño ^1952

gāmopeso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘kāyadhātu’’. ^1953

‘‘phoṭṭhabbadhātu’’. ^1955

Tattha katamā kāyaviññāṇadhātu? Kāyañca paṭicca phoṭṭhabbe ca uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ ^1956

hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho ^1957

tajjākāyaviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘kāyaviññāṇadhātu’’. ^1958

Tattha katamā manodhātu? Cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā uppajjitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppajjati ^1959

cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ ^1960

viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanodhātu; sotaviññāṇadhātuyā…pe… ghānaviññāṇadhātuyā…pe… ^1961

jivhāviññāṇadhātuyā…pe… kāyaviññāṇadhātuyā uppajjitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppajjati cittaṃ mano ^1962

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘manodhātu’’. ^1966

Tattha katamā dhammadhātu? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, yañca rūpaṃ ^1967

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu. ^1968

vedanākkhandho – atthi sahetuko, atthi ahetuko. Tividhena vedanākkhandho – atthi kusalo, atthi ^1970

akusalo, atthi abyākato…pe… evaṃ dasavidhena vedanākkhandho…pe… evaṃ bahuvidhena ^1971

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ? Itthindriyaṃ…pe… ^1981

kabaḷīkāro āhāro – idaṃ vuccati rūpaṃ ‘‘anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpannaṃ’’. ^1982

Tattha katamā asaṅkhatā dhātu? Rāgakkhayo, dosakkhayo, mohakkhayo – ayaṃ vuccati ^1983

‘‘asaṅkhatā dhātu’’. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘dhammadhātu’’. ^1984

uppajjati manodhātu, manodhātuyā uppajjitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ… ^1986

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu; sotaviññāṇadhātuyā…pe… ghānaviññāṇadhātuyā …pe… ^1987

jivhāviññāṇadhātuyā…pe… kāyaviññāṇadhātuyā uppajjitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppajjati manodhātu, ^1988

manodhātuyāpi uppajjitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppajjati cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^1989
