Navindriyā kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā. Tīṇindriyā kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā. Domanassindriyaṃ ^3027

na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesika’’ntipi, ‘‘kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesika’’ntipi. ^3028

Tīṇindriyā siyā kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā, siyā kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā. Cha indriyā siyā ^3029

kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā, siyā kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā, siyā na vattabbā – ^3030

‘‘kilesavippayuttasaṃkilesikā’’tipi, ‘‘kilesavippayuttaasaṃkilesikā’’tipi. ^3031

Pannarasindriyā na dassanena pahātabbā. Sattindriyā siyā dassanena pahātabbā, siyā na dassanena ^3032

pahātabbā. Pannarasindriyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Sattindriyā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā na ^3033

bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Pannarasindriyā na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Sattindriyā siyā dassanena ^3034

pahātabbahetukā, siyā na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Pannarasindriyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. ^3035

Sattindriyā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. ^3036

Navindriyā avitakkā. Domanassindriyaṃ savitakkaṃ. Dvādasindriyā siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. ^3037

Navindriyā avicārā. Domanassindriyaṃ savicāraṃ. Dvādasindriyā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. ^3038

Ekādasindriyā appītikā. Ekādasindriyā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. Ekādasindriyā na pītisahagatā. ^3039

Ekādasindriyā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Dvādasindriyā na sukhasahagatā. Dasindriyā siyā ^3040

sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Dvādasindriyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Dasindriyā siyā ^3041

upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. ^3042

Dasindriyā kāmāvacarā. Tīṇindriyā na kāmāvacarā. Navindriyā siyā kāmāvacarā, siyā na ^3043

kāmāvacarā. Terasindriyā na rūpāvacarā. Navindriyā siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā. Cuddasindriyā ^3044

na arūpāvacarā. Aṭṭhindriyā siyā arūpāvacarā, siyā na arūpāvacarā. Dasindriyā pariyāpannā. Tīṇindriyā ^3045

apariyāpannā. Navindriyā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. Ekādasindriyā aniyyānikā. ^3046

Anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ niyyānikaṃ. Dasindriyā siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā. Dasindriyā ^3047

aniyatā. Anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ niyataṃ. Ekādasindriyā siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā. Dasindriyā ^3048

sauttarā. Tīṇindriyā anuttarā. Navindriyā siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā. Pannarasindriyā araṇā. ^3049

Domanassindriyaṃ saraṇaṃ. Cha indriyā siyā saraṇā, siyā araṇāti. ^3050

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^3051

Indriyavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^3052

6. Paṭiccasamuppādavibhaṅgo ^3053

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^3054

225. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, ^3055

nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, ^3056

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ ^3057

sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3058

samudayo hoti. ^3059

226. Tattha katamā avijjā? Dukkhe aññāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudaye aññāṇaṃ, dukkhanirodhe aññāṇaṃ, ^3060

dukkhanirodhagāminiyā paṭipadāya aññāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3061

Tattha katame avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā? Puññābhisaṅkhāro, apuññābhisaṅkhāro, āneñjābhisaṅkhāro, ^3062

kāyasaṅkhāro, vacīsaṅkhāro, cittasaṅkhāro. ^3063

Tattha katamo puññābhisaṅkhāro? Kusalā cetanā kāmāvacarā rūpāvacarā dānamayā sīlamayā ^3064

bhāvanāmayā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘puññābhisaṅkhāro’’. ^3065

Tattha katamo apuññābhisaṅkhāro? Akusalā cetanā kāmāvacarā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3066

‘‘apuññābhisaṅkhāro’’. ^3067

Tattha katamo āneñjābhisaṅkhāro? Kusalā cetanā arūpāvacarā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3068

‘‘āneñjābhisaṅkhāro’’. ^3069

Tattha katamo kāyasaṅkhāro? Kāyasañcetanā kāyasaṅkhāro, vacīsañcetanā vacīsaṅkhāro, ^3070

manosañcetanā cittasaṅkhāro. Ime vuccanti ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā’’. ^3071

227. Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ^3072

ghānaviññāṇaṃ, jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manoviññāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā ^3073

viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3074

228. Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3075

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3076

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cattāro mahābhūtā, catunnañca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^3077

‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3078

229. Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ^3079

ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā ^3080

saḷāyatanaṃ’’. ^3081

230. Tattha katamo saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso? Cakkhusamphasso sotasamphasso ghānasamphasso ^3082

jivhāsamphasso kāyasamphasso manosamphasso – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3083

231. Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^3084

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manosamphassajā ^3085

vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3086

232. Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā? Rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, ^3087

phoṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā’’. ^3088

233. Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ? Kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, ^3089

attavādupādānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ’’. ^3090

234. Tattha katamo upādānapaccayā bhavo? Bhavo duvidhena – atthi kammabhavo, atthi ^3091

upapattibhavo. Tattha katamo kammabhavo? Puññābhisaṅkhāro, apuññābhisaṅkhāro, āneñjābhisaṅkhāro ^3092

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘kammabhavo’’. Sabbampi bhavagāmikammaṃ kammabhavo. ^3093

Tattha katamo upapattibhavo? Kāmabhavo, rūpabhavo, arūpabhavo, saññābhavo, asaññābhavo, ^3094

nevasaññānāsaññābhavo, ekavokārabhavo, catuvokārabhavo, pañcavokārabhavo – ayaṃ vuccati ^3095

‘‘upapattibhavo’’. Iti ayañca kammabhavo, ayañca upapattibhavo. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upādānapaccayā ^3096

bhavo’’. ^3097

235. Tattha katamā bhavapaccayā jāti? Yā tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāye jāti ^3098

sañjāti okkanti abhinibbatti, khandhānaṃ pātubhāvo, āyatanānaṃ paṭilābho – ayaṃ vuccati ^3099

‘‘bhavapaccayā jāti’’. ^3100

236. Tattha katamaṃ jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? Atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ. Tattha katamā jarā? Yā ^3101

tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāye jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍiccaṃ pāliccaṃ valittacatā āyuno ^3102

saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripāko – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jarā’’. ^3103

Tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? Yā tesaṃ tesaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhedo ^3104

antaradhānaṃ maccu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā [kālaṃkiriyā (ka.)] khandhānaṃ bhedo kaḷevarassa nikkhepo ^3105

jīvitindriyassupacchedo – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘maraṇaṃ’’. Iti ayañca jarā, idañca maraṇaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3106

‘‘jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’. ^3107

237. Tattha katamo soko? Ñātibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, bhogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3108

rogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3109

aññataraññatarena byasanena samannāgatassa, aññataraññatarena dukkhadhammena phuṭṭhassa soko ^3110

socanā socitattaṃ antosoko antoparisoko cetaso parijjhāyanā domanassaṃ sokasallaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3111

‘‘soko’’. ^3112

238. Tattha katamo paridevo? Ñātibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, bhogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3113

rogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3114

aññataraññatarena byasanena samannāgatassa, aññataraññatarena dukkhadhammena phuṭṭhassa ādevo ^3115

paridevo ādevanā paridevanā ādevitattaṃ paridevitattaṃ vācā palāpo vippalāpo lālappo lālappanā ^3116

lālappitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati paridevo’’. ^3117

239. Tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ ^3118

asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘dukkhaṃ’’. ^3119

240. Tattha katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ, cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, ^3120

cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ, cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ^3121

‘‘domanassaṃ’’. ^3122

241. Tattha katamo upāyāso? Ñātibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, bhogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3123

rogabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanena vā phuṭṭhassa, ^3124

aññataraññatarena byasanena samannāgatassa, aññataraññatarena dukkhadhammena phuṭṭhassa āyāso ^3125

upāyāso āyāsitattaṃ upāyāsitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upāyāso’’. ^3126

242. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotīti, evametassa kevalassa ^3127

dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hoti, samāgamo hoti, samodhānaṃ hoti, pātubhāvo hoti. Tena vuccati ^3128

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3129

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^3130

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^3131

1. Paccayacatukkaṃ ^3132

243. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3133

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, ^3134

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3135

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3136

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3137

phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, ^3138

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3139

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, ^3140

nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3141

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3142

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3143

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, ^3144

nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3145

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3146

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3147

Paccayacatukkaṃ. ^3148

2. Hetucatukkaṃ ^3149

244. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ, ^3150

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahetukaṃ, ^3151

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā ^3152

taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3153

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3154

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ, ^3155

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmapaccayā phasso nāmahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā ^3156

phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; ^3157

upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3158

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3159

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ, ^3160

viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukaṃ, ^3161

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā ^3162

taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3163

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3164

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ, ^3165

viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukaṃ, ^3166

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā ^3167

taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3168

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3169

Hetucatukkaṃ. ^3170

3. Sampayuttacatukkaṃ ^3171

245. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, ^3172

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, ^3173

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, ^3174

vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā ^3175

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3176

samudayo hoti. ^3177

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, ^3178

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapaccayā phasso nāmasampayutto, phassapaccayā ^3179

vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ ^3180

taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^3181

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3182

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, ^3183

viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3184

nāmarūpasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā ^3185

phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ ^3186

taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^3187

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3188

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, ^3189

viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ ^3190

nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, ^3191

phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā ^3192

upādānaṃ upādānasampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3193

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3194

Sampayuttacatukkaṃ. ^3195

4. Aññamaññacatukkaṃ ^3196

246. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, ^3197

viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; nāmapaccayā ^3198

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi ^3199

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi ^3200

vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3201

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3202

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi ^3203

saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; nāmapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi ^3204

nāmaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; ^3205

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3206

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3207

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi ^3208

saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapaccayā ^3209

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi ^3210

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi ^3211

vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3212

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3213

Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi ^3214

saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, ^3215

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; ^3216

phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; ^3217

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3218

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3219

Aññamaññacatukkaṃ. ^3220

Mātikā ^3221

247. Saṅkhārapaccayā avijjā…pe… viññāṇapaccayā avijjā…pe… nāmapaccayā avijjā…pe… ^3222

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā avijjā…pe… phassapaccayā avijjā…pe… vedanāpaccayā avijjā…pe… ^3223

taṇhāpaccayā avijjā…pe… upādānapaccayā avijjā…pe… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā ^3224

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^3225

phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, ^3226

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3227

Mātikā. ^3228

5. Paccayacatukkaṃ ^3229

248. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3230

somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ ^3231

vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3232

samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3233

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, ^3234

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3235

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3236

249. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3237

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3238

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ [cetayitattaṃ (sī. ka.)] ^3239

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3240

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ ^3241

mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^3242

‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3243

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^3244

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^3245

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ ^3246

mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^3247

‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3248

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3249

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3250

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3251

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3252

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo anunayo anurodho nandī nandirāgo cittassa ^3253

sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā’’. ^3254

Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ? Yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantāro ^3255

diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ gāho patiṭṭhāho abhiniveso parāmāso ^3256

kummaggo micchāpatho micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāho [vipariyesaggāho (bahūsu)] – idaṃ ^3257

vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ’’. ^3258

Tattha katamo upādānapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā upādānaṃ, vedanākkhandho saññākkhandho ^3259

saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upādānapaccayā bhavo’’. ^3260

Tattha katamā bhavapaccayā jāti? Yā tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañjāti nibbatti abhinibbatti ^3261

pātubhāvo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘bhavapaccayā jāti’’. ^3262

Tattha katamaṃ jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? Atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ. Tattha katamā jarā? Yā tesaṃ ^3263

tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyuno saṃhāni – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jarā’’. Tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? Yo ^3264

tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ khayo vayo bhedo paribhedo aniccatā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^3265

‘‘maraṇaṃ’’. Iti ayañca jarā, idañca maraṇaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’. ^3266

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotīti. Evametassa kevalassa ^3267

dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hoti, samāgamo hoti, samodhānaṃ hoti, pātubhāvo hoti. Tena vuccati ^3268

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3269

250. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, ^3270

nāmapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, ^3271

upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3272

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3273

251. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3274

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3275

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3276

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3277

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3278

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3279

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^3280

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^3281

Nāmapaccayā phassoti. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Ṭhapetvā phassaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3282

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3283

Tattha katamo nāmapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3284

vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā phasso’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3285

samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3286

Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, ^3287

nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3288

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3289

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3290

253. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3291

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3292

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3293

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3294

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3295

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3296

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3297

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3298

rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, jivhāyatanassa ^3299

upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3300

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3301

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3302

Nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatananti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3303

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3304

rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, ^3305

idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3306

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3307

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3308

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3309

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^3310

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3311

254. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā ^3312

nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, ^3313

vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^3314

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3315

255. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3316

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3317

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3318

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3319

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3320

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3321

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3322

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3323

rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, jivhāyatanassa ^3324

upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3325

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3326

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3327

Nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatananti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3328

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3329

rūpaṃ? Cattāro ca mahābhūtā, yañca rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. ^3330

Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3331

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, ^3332

jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ’’. ^3333

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3334

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^3335

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3336

Paccayacatukkaṃ. ^3337

6. Hetucatukkaṃ ^3338

256. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3339

saṅkhārahetukaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3340

nāmahetukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, ^3341

vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, ^3342

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3343

257. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3344

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3345

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3346

vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko’’. ^3347

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3348

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ’’. ^3349

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^3350

saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ’’. ^3351

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3352

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahetukaṃ’’. ^3353

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā ^3354

samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko’’. ^3355

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ ^3356

cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3357

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā’’. ^3358

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ ^3359

vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā’’. ^3360

Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ? Yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… titthāyatanaṃ ^3361

vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ’’…pe… tena vuccati ^3362

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti’’ti. ^3363

258. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3364

saṅkhārahetukaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmapaccayā phasso nāmahetuko, ^3365

phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ ^3366

taṇhāhetukaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^3367

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3368

259. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3369

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3370

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3371

vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko’’. ^3372

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3373

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ. ^3374

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^3375

saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ’’. ^3376

Nāmapaccayā phasso nāmahetukoti. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Ṭhapetvā phassaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3377

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3378

Tattha katamo nāmapaccayā phasso nāmahetuko? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ ^3379

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā phasso nāmahetuko’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^3380

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3381

260. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3382

saṅkhārahetukaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3383

nāmarūpahetukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā ^3384

phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; ^3385

upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3386

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3387

261. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3388

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3389

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3390

vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko’’. ^3391

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3392

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ’’. ^3393

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha ^3394

katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3395

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, ^3396

jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3397

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3398

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ’’. ^3399

Nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukanti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3400

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3401

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca ^3402

nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3403

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano ^3404

mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3405

nāmarūpahetukaṃ’’. ^3406

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā ^3407

samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko’’…pe… tena ^3408

vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3409

262. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3410

saṅkhārahetukaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ ^3411

nāmarūpahetukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā ^3412

phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ; ^3413

upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3414

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3415

263. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3416

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3417

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3418

vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāhetuko’’. ^3419

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3420

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ’’. ^3421

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha ^3422

katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3423

Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, ^3424

jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3425

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3426

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇahetukaṃ’’. ^3427

Nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukanti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3428

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3429

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cattāro ca mahābhūtā, yañca rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ^3430

‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3431

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ^3432

ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā ^3433

saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahetukaṃ’’. ^3434

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā ^3435

samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko’’. ^3436

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ ^3437

cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3438

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā’’. ^3439

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ ^3440

vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāhetukā’’. ^3441

Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ? Yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… titthāyatanaṃ ^3442

vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhetukaṃ’’…pe… tena vuccati ^3443

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3444

Hetucatukkaṃ. ^3445

7. Sampayuttacatukkaṃ ^3446

Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3447

saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3448

nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā ^3449

phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ ^3450

taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^3451

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3452

265. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3453

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3454

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ^3455

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto’’. ^3456

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ… ^3457

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3458

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^3459

saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3460

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ… ^3461

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3462

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto? Yo phasso phusanā ^3463

samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto’’. ^3464

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ ^3465

cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3466

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā’’. ^3467

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ^3468

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā’’. ^3469

Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ? Yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… ^3470

titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ’’ …pe… ^3471

tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3472

266. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3473

saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapaccayā phasso ^3474

nāmasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, ^3475

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^3476

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3477

267. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3478

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3479

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ^3480

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto’’. ^3481

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ… ^3482

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3483

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^3484

saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3485

Nāmapaccayā phasso nāmasampayuttoti. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Ṭhapetvā phassaṃ, ^3486

vedanākkhandho saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3487

Tattha katamo nāmapaccayā phasso nāmasampayutto? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā ^3488

samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā phasso nāmasampayutto’’…pe… tena vuccati ^3489

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3490

268. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3491

saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā ^3492

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, ^3493

phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā ^3494

upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3495

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3496

269. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3497

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3498

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ^3499

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto’’. ^3500

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ … ^3501

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3502

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi ^3503

rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ^3504

‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa ^3505

upacayo, jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ ^3506

cittahetukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3507

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ’’. ^3508

Nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttanti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3509

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3510

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca ^3511

nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3512

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano ^3513

mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ ^3514

nāmasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3515

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto? Yo phasso phusanā ^3516

samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso ^3517

chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo ^3518

hotī’’ti. ^3519

270. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ^3520

saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, ^3521

nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso ^3522

chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā ^3523

vedanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā ^3524

jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3525

271. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3526

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3527

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ^3528

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro avijjāsampayutto’’. ^3529

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ… ^3530

pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’. ^3531

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi ^3532

rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ^3533

‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa ^3534

upacayo, jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ ^3535

cittahetukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3536

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ viññāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ’’. ^3537

Nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatananti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. ^3538

Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ^3539

‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cattāro ca mahābhūtā, yañca rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu ^3540

vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3541
