vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3544

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto? Yo phasso phusanā ^3545

samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso ^3546

chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo ^3547

hotī’’ti. ^3548

Sampayuttacatukkaṃ. ^3549

8. Aññamaññacatukkaṃ ^3550

272. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā ^3551

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; ^3552

nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^3553

phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā ^3554

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā ^3555

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3556

samudayo hoti. ^3557

273. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3558

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3559

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3560

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3561

Tattha katamā saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho ^3562

akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā’’. ^3563

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3564

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ. ^3565

Tattha katamo viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3566

‘‘viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro’’. ^3567

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^3568

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^3569

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3570

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3571

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3572

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3573

Tattha katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^3574

saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmaṃ’’. ^3575

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3576

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3577

Tattha katamaṃ phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3578

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3579

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3580

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3581

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayāpi phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3582

vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayāpi phasso’’. ^3583

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā taṇhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ^3584

‘‘vedanāpaccayā taṇhā’’. ^3585

Tattha katamā taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3586

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā’’. ^3587

Tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ? Yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… titthāyatanaṃ ^3588

vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ’’. ^3589

Tattha katamā upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā? Yo rāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ^3590

‘‘upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā’’. ^3591

Tattha katamo upādānapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā upādānaṃ, vedanākkhandho saññākkhandho ^3592

saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upādānapaccayā bhavo’’. ^3593

Tattha katamā bhavapaccayā jāti? Yā tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañjāti nibbatti abhinibbati ^3594

pātubhāvo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘bhavapaccayā jāti’’. ^3595

Tattha katamaṃ jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? Atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ. Tattha katamā jarā? Yā tesaṃ ^3596

tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyuno saṃhāni – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jarā’’. Tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? Yo ^3597

tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ khayo vayo bhedo paribhedo aniccatā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^3598

‘‘maraṇaṃ’’. Iti ayañca jarā, idañca maraṇaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’. ^3599

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotīti. Evametassa kevalassa ^3600

dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hoti, samāgamo hoti, samodhānaṃ hoti, pātubhāvo hoti. Tena vuccati ^3601

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3602

274. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā ^3603

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; ^3604

nāmapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi nāmaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; ^3605

vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; ^3606

upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3607

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3608

275. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3609

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3610

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3611

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3612

Tattha katamā saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho ^3613

akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā’’. ^3614

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3615

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3616

Tattha katamo viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3617

‘‘viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro’’. ^3618

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^3619

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^3620

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3621

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3622

Nāmapaccayā phassoti. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? Ṭhapetvā phassaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3623

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. ^3624

Tattha katamo nāmapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^3625

vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā phasso’’. ^3626

Tattha katamaṃ phassapaccayāpi nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^3627

viññāṇakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayāpi nāmaṃ’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa ^3628

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3629

276. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā ^3630

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; ^3631

nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^3632

phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā ^3633

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā ^3634

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3635

samudayo hoti. ^3636

277. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusamūlaṃ – ayaṃ ^3637

vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3638

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3639

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro. ^3640

Tattha katamā saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho ^3641

akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā’’. ^3642

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3643

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3644

Tattha katamo viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3645

‘‘viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro’’. ^3646

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3647

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3648

rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, jivhāyatanassa ^3649

upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3650

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3651

‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3652

Nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇanti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3653

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3654

rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, ^3655

idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3656

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3657

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3658

Nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatananti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3659

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3660

rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, ^3661

idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3662

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3663

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3664

Tattha katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3665

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3666

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, ^3667

jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3668

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3669

‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3670

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3671

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3672

Tattha katamaṃ phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3673

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’…pe… tena vuccati ^3674

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3675

278. Tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā; saṅkhārapaccayā ^3676

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; ^3677

nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^3678

phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā ^3679

taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayāpi vedanā; taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayāpi taṇhā; upādānapaccayā ^3680

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3681

samudayo hoti. ^3682

279. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3683

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3684

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3685

‘‘avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3686

Tattha katamā saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho ^3687

akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayāpi avijjā’’. ^3688

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3689

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3690

Tattha katamo viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3691

‘‘viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro’’. ^3692

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3693

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati nāmaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? ^3694

Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, jivhāyatanassa upacayo, ^3695

kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ ^3696

vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3697

Nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇanti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3698

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3699

rūpaṃ? Yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, ^3700

idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3701

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3702

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3703

Nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatananti. Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ^3704

Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha katamaṃ ^3705

rūpaṃ? Cattāro ca mahābhūtā, yañca rūpaṃ nissāya manoviññāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. ^3706

Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3707

Tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, ^3708

jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ’’. ^3709

Tattha katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ? Atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ. Tattha katamaṃ ^3710

nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’. Tattha ^3711

katamaṃ rūpaṃ? Cakkhāyatanassa upacayo, sotāyatanassa upacayo, ghānāyatanassa upacayo, ^3712

jivhāyatanassa upacayo, kāyāyatanassa upacayo, yaṃ vā panaññampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahetukaṃ ^3713

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’. Iti idañca nāmaṃ, idañca rūpaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ^3714

‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’. ^3715

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3716

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3717

Tattha katamaṃ phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3718

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3719

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3720

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā ^3721

vedanā’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3722

Aññamaññacatukkaṃ. ^3723

9. Akusalaniddeso ^3724

280. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3725

somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena…pe… somanassasahagataṃ ^3726

diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ ^3727

sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā ^3728

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā ^3729

saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3730

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā adhimokkho, ^3731

adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3732

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3733

281. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3734

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’. ^3735

Tattha katamo avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3736

avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro…pe…. ^3737

Tattha katamo taṇhāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā – ^3738

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘taṇhāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^3739

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3740

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^3741

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3742

282. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3743

upekkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā ^3744

rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3745

samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3746

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, ^3747

taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3748

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3749

283. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3750

ayaṃ vuccati avijjā…pe…. ^3751

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3752

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3753

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo ^3754

hotī’’ti. ^3755

284. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3756

upekkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena…pe… upekkhāsahagataṃ ^3757

diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… upekkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena ^3758

rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ ^3759

vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, ^3760

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3761

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā adhimokkho, ^3762

adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3763

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3764

285. Tattha katamā avijjā…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3765

samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3766

286. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3767

domanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… domanassasahagataṃ ^3768

paṭighasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā ^3769

rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3770

samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3771

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā paṭighaṃ, ^3772

paṭighapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3773

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3774

287. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3775

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3776

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ ^3777

cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3778

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3779

Tattha katamaṃ vedanāpaccayā paṭighaṃ? Yo cittassa āghāto…pe… caṇḍikkaṃ asuropo [asulopo ^3780

(syā.)] anattamanatā cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā paṭighaṃ’’. ^3781

Tattha katamo paṭighapaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^3782

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭighapaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^3783

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3784

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^3785

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3786

288. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3787

upekkhāsahagataṃ vicikicchāsampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ ^3788

vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3789

samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3790

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā vicikicchā, ^3791

vicikicchāpaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^3792

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3793

289. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3794

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3795

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3796

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ, cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3797

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3798

Tattha katamā vedanāpaccayā vicikicchā? Yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā ^3799

dveḷhakaṃ dvidhāpatho [dvedhāpatho (sī. syā.)] saṃsayo anekaṃsaggāho āsappanā parisappanā ^3800

apariyogāhaṇā [apariyogāhanā (sī. syā. ka.)] chambhitattaṃ cittassa manovilekho – ayaṃ vuccati ^3801

‘‘vedanāpaccayā vicikicchā’’. ^3802

Tattha katamo vicikicchāpaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā vicikicchaṃ, vedanākkhandho saññākkhandho ^3803

saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vicikicchāpaccayā bhavo’’…pe… tena vuccati ^3804

‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3805

290. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3806

upekkhāsahagataṃ uddhaccasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā ^3807

rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3808

samaye avijjāpaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3809

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā uddhaccaṃ, ^3810

uddhaccapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3811

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3812

291. Tattha katamā avijjā? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ^3813

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjā’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3814

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3815

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3816

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3817

Tattha katamaṃ vedanāpaccayā uddhaccaṃ? Yaṃ cittassa uddhaccaṃ avūpasamo cetaso vikkhepo ^3818

bhantattaṃ cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā uddhaccaṃ’’. ^3819

Tattha katamo uddhaccapaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā ^3820

tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘uddhaccapaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^3821

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3822

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^3823

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3824

Akusalaniddeso. ^3825

10. Kusalaniddeso ^3826

292. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3827

somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā ^3828

rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ ^3829

samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, ^3830

nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3831

pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^3832

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3833

293. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso, amoho. ^3834

Tattha katamo alobho? Yo alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ ^3835

anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘alobho’’. ^3836

Tattha katamo adoso? Yo adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ^3837

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adoso’’. ^3838

Tattha katamo amoho? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ ^3839

vuccati ‘‘amoho’’. Ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3840

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3841

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^3842

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ…pe… viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ…pe… nāmapaccayā ^3843

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ…pe… chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso…pe… phassapaccayā vedanā…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ^3844

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3845

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā pasādo? Yā saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo – ayaṃ vuccati ^3846

‘‘vedanāpaccayā pasādo’’. ^3847

Tattha katamo pasādapaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^3848

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pasādapaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^3849

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3850

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^3851

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3852

294. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3853

somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… somanassasahagataṃ ^3854

ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena ^3855

rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ ^3856

vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, ^3857

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3858

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā ^3859

adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^3860

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3861

295. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso. ^3862

Tattha katamo alobho? Yo alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ ^3863

anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘alobho’’. ^3864

Tattha katamo adoso? Yo adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ^3865

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adoso’’. Ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3866

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3867

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3868

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3869

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā ^3870

vedanā’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3871

296. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3872

upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ ^3873

sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā ^3874

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye ^3875

kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3876

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, ^3877

pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3878

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3879

297. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso, amoho – ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3880

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3881

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati – ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3882

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3883

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3884

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo ^3885

hotī’’ti. ^3886

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti ^3887

upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ ^3888

sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā ^3889

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye ^3890

kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^3891

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, ^3892

pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3893

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3894

299. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso – ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3895

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3896

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3897

samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3898

300. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi… ^3899

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ [paṭhavīkasiṇaṃ (sī. syā.)], tasmiṃ ^3900

samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, ^3901

nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3902

pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^3903

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3904

301. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso, amoho – ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3905

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3906

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3907

samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3908

302. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ^3909

ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… ^3910

catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā ^3911

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^3912

phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, ^3913

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3914

303. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso, amoho – ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3915

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3916

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3917

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3918

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā ^3919

vedanā’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3920

304. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ ^3921

apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ ^3922

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā ^3923

saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3924

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā ^3925

adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametesaṃ ^3926

dhammānaṃ samudayo hoti. ^3927

305. Tattha katame kusalamūlā? Alobho, adoso, amoho. ^3928

Tattha katamo alobho…pe… adoso…pe… amoho? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho ^3929

dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ ^3930

vuccati ‘‘amoho’’. Ime vuccanti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’. ^3931

Tattha katamo kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^3932

‘‘kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3933

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3934

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3935

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā pasādo? Yā saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo – ayaṃ vuccati ^3936

‘‘vedanāpaccayā pasādo’’. ^3937

Tattha katamo pasādapaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^3938

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pasādapaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^3939

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^3940

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^3941

pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’. ^3942

Evametesaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayo hotīti. Evametesaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅgati hoti, samāgamo hoti, ^3943

samodhānaṃ hoti, pātubhāvo hoti. Tena vuccati ‘‘evametesaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3944

Kusalaniddeso. ^3945

11. Abyākataniddeso ^3946

306. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^3947

upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^3948

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3949

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3950

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3951

307. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^3952

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3953

tajjācakkhuviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^3954

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^3955

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^3956

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^3957

tajjācakkhuviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^3958

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^3959

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3960

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^3961

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^3962

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3963

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā vedanaṃ, saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho ^3964

viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā bhavo’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^3965

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3966

308. Tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārahetukaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ ^3967

viññāṇahetukaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahetukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso ^3968

chaṭṭhāyatanahetuko, phassapaccayā vedanā phassahetukā, vedanāpaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^3969

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3970

309. Tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ ^3971

viññāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso ^3972

chaṭṭhāyatanasampayutto, phassapaccayā vedanā phassasampayuttā, vedanāpaccayā bhavo, ^3973

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3974

310. Tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayāpi saṅkhāro; viññāṇapaccayā ^3975

nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayāpi viññāṇaṃ; nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayāpi nāmaṃ; ^3976

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapaccayā vedanā, ^3977

vedanāpaccayāpi phasso; vedanāpaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^3978

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^3979

311. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^3980

upacitattā vipākaṃ sotaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pe… ^3981

ghānaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pe… jivhāviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ ^3982

hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pe… kāyaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti sukhasahagataṃ ^3983

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, ^3984

nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^3985

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^3986

samudayo hoti. ^3987

312. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’… ^3988

pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^3989

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ ^3990

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^3991

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā vedanaṃ, saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho ^3992

viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā bhavo’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^3993

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^3994

313. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^3995

upacitattā vipākā manodhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā ^3996

gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye ^3997

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^3998

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā ^3999

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^4000

samudayo hoti. ^4001

314. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^4002

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanodhātu – ^4003

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4004

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^4005

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^4006

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4007

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4008

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4009

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4010

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^4011

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4012

315. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^4013

upacitattā vipākā manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti somanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā ^4014

vā gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā ^4015

panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^4016

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, ^4017

adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^4018

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4019

316. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^4020

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4021

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ^4022

‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4023

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^4024

sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4025

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4026

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4027

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4028

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^4029

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4030

317. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^4031

upacitattā vipākā manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā ^4032

gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā ^4033

panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^4034

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, ^4035

adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa ^4036

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4037

318. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^4038

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4039

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ^4040

‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4041

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^4042

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^4043

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4044

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4045

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4046

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4047

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^4048

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4049

319. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^4050

upacitattā vipākā manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti somanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pe… ^4051

somanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… somanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pe… ^4052

somanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pe… ^4053

upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pe… ^4054

upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ ^4055

yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^4056

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, ^4057

pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^4058

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4059

320. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^4060

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4061

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ^4062

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4063

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā pasādo? Yā saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo – ayaṃ vuccati ^4064

‘‘vedanāpaccayā pasādo’’. ^4065

Tattha katamo pasādapaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4066

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pasādapaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4067

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4068

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^4069

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4070

321. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva ^4071

kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti… ^4072

pe… avikkhepo hoti – ime dhammā kusalā. ^4073

Tasseva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… ^4074

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, ^4075

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā ^4076

vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā ^4077

jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4078

322. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ^4079

ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… ^4080

catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti – ime ^4081

dhammā kusalā. ^4082

Tasseva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ ^4083

samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^4084

upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, ^4085

nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā ^4086

pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^4087

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4088

323. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ ^4089

apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ ^4090

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… ^4091

avikkhepo hoti – ime dhammā kusalā. ^4092

Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… ^4093

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^4094

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^4095

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā ^4096

adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametesaṃ ^4097

dhammānaṃ samudayo hoti. ^4098

324. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ ^4099

cakkhuviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ…pe… sotaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ ^4100

hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pe… ghānaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ^4101

gandhārammaṇaṃ…pe… jivhāviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pe… ^4102

kāyaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti dukkhasahagataṃ phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^4103

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^4104

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^4105

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4106

325. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhāro’’. ^4107

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4108

tajjākāyaviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ^4109

‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4110

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ ^4111

asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘phassapaccayā ^4112

vedanā’’. ^4113

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā vedanaṃ, saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho ^4114

viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā bhavo’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^4115

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4116

326. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā ^4117

manodhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā gandhārammaṇā vā ^4118

rasārammaṇā vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārapaccayā ^4119

viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, ^4120

phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, ^4121

jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4122

327. Tattha katamo saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati saṅkhāro. ^4123

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanodhātu – ^4124

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4125

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^4126

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^4127

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4128

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4129

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4130

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4131

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’… ^4132

pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4133

328. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā ^4134

manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ ^4135
