hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pe… kāyaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti sukhasahagataṃ ^4249

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ…pe… manodhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… ^4250

phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā…pe… manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti somanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā… ^4251

pe… dhammārammaṇā vā…pe… manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā… ^4252

pe… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, ^4253

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^4254

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā ^4255

bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa ^4256

samudayo hoti. ^4257

346. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā ^4258

upacitattā vipākā manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti somanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pe… ^4259

somanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… somanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā… ^4260

pe… somanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pe… ^4261

upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhārena…pe… upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pe… ^4262

upekkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ ^4263

vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, ^4264

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā ^4265

vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā ^4266

jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4267

347. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva ^4268

kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti… ^4269

pe… avikkhepo hoti – ime dhammā kusalā. ^4270

Tasseva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… ^4271

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, ^4272

saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, ^4273

chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā ^4274

adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa ^4275

kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4276

348. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ^4277

ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… ^4278

catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti – ime ^4279

dhammā kusalā. ^4280

Tasseva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ ^4281

samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^4282

upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, ^4283

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā ^4284

vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā ^4285

jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4286

349. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ ^4287

apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ ^4288

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… ^4289

avikkhepo hoti – ime dhammā kusalā. ^4290

Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… ^4291

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^4292

kusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^4293

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā pasādo, ^4294

pasādapaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^4295

Evametesaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayo hoti. ^4296

Kusalamūlakavipākaniddeso. ^4297

14. Akusalamūlakavipākaniddeso ^4298

350. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ ^4299

cakkhuviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^4300

akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā ^4301

chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā bhavo, ^4302

bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4303

351. Tattha katamo akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^4304

vuccati ‘‘akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘evametassa kevalassa ^4305

dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4306

352. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ ^4307

sotaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pe… ghānaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ ^4308

hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pe… jivhāviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ^4309

rasārammaṇaṃ…pe… kāyaviññāṇaṃ uppannaṃ hoti dukkhasahagataṃ phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ…pe… ^4310

manodhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… phoṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā ^4311

panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā ^4312

nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, ^4313

vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ. ^4314

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4315

353. Katame dhammā abyākatā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā ^4316

manoviññāṇadhātu uppannā hoti upekkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pe… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ ^4317

yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, ^4318

viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ, nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā ^4319

vedanā, vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho, adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā ^4320

jarāmaraṇaṃ. Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. ^4321

354. Tattha katamo akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro? Yā cetanā sañcetanā sañcetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ ^4322

vuccati ‘‘akusalamūlapaccayā saṅkhāro’’. ^4323

Tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4324

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ’’. ^4325

Tattha katamaṃ viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ^4326

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘viññāṇapaccayā nāmaṃ’’. ^4327

Tattha katamaṃ nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4328

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘nāmapaccayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’. ^4329

Tattha katamo chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso? Yo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ^4330

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapaccayā phasso’’. ^4331

Tattha katamā phassapaccayā vedanā? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^4332

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ vuccati ^4333

‘‘phassapaccayā vedanā’’. ^4334

Tattha katamo vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho? Yo cittassa adhimokkho adhimuccanā tadadhimuttatā ^4335

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vedanāpaccayā adhimokkho’’. ^4336

Tattha katamo adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo? Ṭhapetvā adhimokkhaṃ, vedanākkhandho ^4337

saññākkhandho saṅkhārakkhandho viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘adhimokkhapaccayā bhavo’’. ^4338

Tattha katamā bhavapaccayā jāti? Yā tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañjāti nibbatti abhinibbatti ^4339

pātubhāvo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘bhavapaccayā jāti’’. ^4340

Tattha katamaṃ jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? Atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ. Tattha katamā jarā? Yā tesaṃ ^4341

tesaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyuno saṃhāni – ayaṃ vuccati jarā. Tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? Yo ^4342

tesaṃ tesaṃ dhammānaṃ khayo vayo bhedo paribhedo aniccatā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^4343

‘‘maraṇaṃ’’. Iti ayañca jarā, idañca maraṇaṃ. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’. ^4344

Evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotīti. Evametassa kevalassa ^4345

dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hoti, samāgamo hoti, samodhānaṃ hoti, pātubhāvo hoti. Tena vuccati ^4346

evametassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hotī’’ti. ^4347

Akusalamūlakavipākaniddeso. ^4348

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^4349

Paṭiccasamuppādavibhaṅgo [paccayākāravibhaṅgo (sī. syā.)] niṭṭhito. ^4350

7. Satipaṭṭhānavibhaṅgo ^4351

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^4352

355. Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāye kāyānupassī viharati bahiddhā kāye ^4353

kāyānupassī viharati ajjhattabahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4354

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, ajjhattaṃ vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati bahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī ^4355

viharati ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4356

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, ajjhattaṃ citte cittānupassī viharati bahiddhā citte cittānupassī viharati ^4357

ajjhattabahiddhā citte cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ, ^4358

ajjhattaṃ dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati bahiddhā dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ^4359

ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4360

abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4361

1. Kāyānupassanāniddeso ^4362

356. Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāye kāyānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyaṃ ^4363

uddhaṃ pādatalā adho kesamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucino paccavekkhati – ^4364

‘‘atthi imasmiṃ kāye kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nhāru [nahāru (sī.)] aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñjaṃ ^4365

[aṭṭhimiñjā (sī.)] vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilomakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ ^4366

udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ sedo medo assu vasā kheḷo siṅghāṇikā lasikā ^4367

mutta’’nti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ ^4368

āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā bahiddhā kāye cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4369

Kathañca bhikkhu bahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu bahiddhā kāyaṃ uddhaṃ ^4370

pādatalā adho kesamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucino paccavekkhati – ‘‘atthissa ^4371

kāye kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñjaṃ vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ ^4372

kilomakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ ^4373

sedo medo assu vasā kheḷo siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’’nti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti ^4374

svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā ^4375

ajjhattabahiddhā kāye cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4376

Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā ^4377

kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adho kesamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucino ^4378

paccavekkhati – ‘‘atthi imasmiṃ kāye kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñjaṃ ^4379

vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilomakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ ^4380

pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ sedo medo assu vasā kheḷo siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’’nti. Evaṃ bhikkhu ^4381

ajjhattabahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4382

357. Anupassīti. Tattha katamā anupassanā? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo ^4383

sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘anupassanā’’. Imāya anupassanāya upeto hoti samupeto upāgato ^4384

samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘anupassī’’ti. ^4385

358. Viharatīti. Iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^4386

359. Ātāpīti. Tattha katamo ātāpo [katamaṃ ātāpaṃ (sabbattha)]? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… ^4387

sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ātāpo’’. Iminā ātāpena upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato ^4388

upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘ātāpī’’ti. ^4389

360. Sampajānoti. Tattha katamaṃ sampajaññaṃ? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho ^4390

dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sampajaññaṃ’’. Iminā sampajaññena upeto hoti samupeto ^4391

upāgato samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘sampajāno’’ti. ^4392

361. Satimāti. Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. Imāya ^4393

satiyā upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^4394

‘‘satimā’’ti. ^4395

362. Vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassanti. Tattha katamo loko? Sveva kāyo loko. Pañcapi ^4396

upādānakkhandhā loko. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘loko’’. Tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa ^4397

sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abhijjhā’’. Tattha katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ ^4398

dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ ^4399

vuccati ‘‘domanassaṃ’’. Iti ayañca abhijjhā idañca domanassaṃ imamhi loke vinītā honti paṭivinītā ^4400

santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena ^4401

vuccati ‘‘vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassa’’nti. ^4402

Kāyānupassanāniddeso. ^4403

2. Vedanānupassanāniddeso ^4404

363. Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4405

vedayamāno ‘‘sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘dukkhaṃ ^4406

vedanaṃ vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ ^4407

vedanaṃ vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4408

vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4409

vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4410

vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4411

vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamāno ‘‘sāmisaṃ ^4412

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4413

vedayamāno ‘‘nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayāmī’’ti pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati ^4414

bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā ^4415

svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā bahiddhā vedanāsu cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4416

Kathañca bhikkhu bahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4417

vedayamānaṃ ‘‘sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘dukkhaṃ ^4418

vedanaṃ vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ ^4419

vedanaṃ vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4420

vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4421

vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4422

vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4423

vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ ^4424

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4425

vedayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ vedayatī’’ti pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati ^4426

bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā ^4427

svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4428

Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ ^4429

vedanaṃ ‘‘sukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘dukkhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, ^4430

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘adukkhamasukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4431

‘‘sāmisā sukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘nirāmisā sukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, ^4432

sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘sāmisā dukkhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vedanaṃ ^4433

‘‘nirāmisā dukkhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘sāmisā ^4434

adukkhamasukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ‘‘nirāmisā ^4435

adukkhamasukhā vedanā’’ti pajānāti. Evaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ^4436

ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4437

364. Anupassīti …pe… viharatīti…pe… ātāpīti…pe… sampajānoti…pe… satimāti…pe… vineyya ^4438

loke abhijjhādomanassanti. Tattha katamo loko? Sāyeva vedanā loko. Pañcapi upādānakkhandhā loko. ^4439

Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘loko’’. Tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ^4440

‘‘abhijjhā’’. Tattha katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ ^4441

cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ^4442

‘‘domanassaṃ’’. Iti ayañca abhijjhā idañca domanassaṃ imamhi loke vinītā honti paṭivinītā santā samitā ^4443

vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘vineyya ^4444

loke abhijjhādomanassa’’nti. ^4445

Vedanānupassanāniddeso. ^4446

3. Cittānupassanāniddeso ^4447

365. Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ citte cittānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ^4448

‘‘sarāgaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, sadosaṃ vā ^4449

cittaṃ ‘‘sadosaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, vītadosaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītadosaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, samohaṃ ^4450

vā cittaṃ ‘‘samohaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamohaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītamohaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4451

saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vikkhittaṃ me citta’’nti ^4452

pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggataṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ^4453

‘‘amahaggataṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sauttaraṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ ^4454

vā cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaraṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4455

asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttaṃ me citta’’nti ^4456

pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttaṃ me citta’’nti pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti ^4457

bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ ^4458

vavatthapetvā bahiddhā citte cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4459

Kathañca bhikkhu bahiddhā citte cittānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ^4460

‘‘sarāgamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, sadosaṃ ^4461

vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘sadosamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vītadosaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītadosamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4462

samohaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘samohamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamohaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītamohamassa ^4463

citta’’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ^4464

‘‘vikkhittamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggatamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4465

amahaggataṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘amahaggatamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vāssa cittaṃ ^4466

‘‘sauttaramassa citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaramassa citta’’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ ^4467

vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitamassa citta’’nti ^4468

pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ^4469

‘‘avimuttamassa citta’’nti pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ ^4470

vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā ^4471

ajjhattabahiddhā citte cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4472

Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā citte cittānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ^4473

‘‘sarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, sadosaṃ vā cittaṃ ^4474

‘‘sadosaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītadosaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītadosaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, samohaṃ vā cittaṃ ^4475

‘‘samohaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamohaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītamohaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vā ^4476

cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vikkhittaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ ^4477

vā cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggataṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘amahaggataṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4478

sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sauttaraṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaraṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, ^4479

samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitaṃ citta’’nti ^4480

pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttaṃ citta’’nti ^4481

pajānāti. Evaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā citte cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4482

abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4483

366. Anupassīti…pe… viharatīti…pe… ātāpīti…pe… sampajānoti…pe… satimāti…pe… vineyya ^4484

loke abhijjhādomanassanti. Tattha katamo loko? Taṃyeva cittaṃ loko. Pañcapi upādānakkhandhā loko ^4485

– tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abhijjhā’’. Tattha ^4486

katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ ^4487

vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘domanassaṃ’’. Iti ayañca abhijjhā ^4488

idañca domanassaṃ imamhi loke vinītā honti paṭivinītā santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā ^4489

abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘vineyya loke ^4490

abhijjhādomanassa’’nti. ^4491

Cittānupassanāniddeso. ^4492

4. Dhammānupassanāniddeso ^4493

367. Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu santaṃ vā ^4494

ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ kāmacchando’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ ^4495

kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ kāmacchando’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa kāmacchandassa ^4496

uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca ^4497

pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādo hoti tañca pajānāti. Santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ…pe… ^4498

santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thinamiddhaṃ [thīnamiddhaṃ (sī. syā.)] …pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ ^4499

uddhaccakukkuccaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’’ti ^4500

pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca ^4501

anuppannāya vicikicchāya uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hoti ^4502

tañca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādo hoti tañca pajānāti. ^4503

Santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ ^4504

vā ajjhattaṃ satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi me ajjhattaṃ satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca ^4505

anuppannassa satisambojjhaṅgassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa satisambojjhaṅgassa ^4506

bhāvanāya pāripūrī hoti tañca pajānāti, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… ^4507

santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ [viriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ (sī. syā.)] …pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ ^4508

pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ …pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ ^4509

samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi me ajjhattaṃ ^4510

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi me ^4511

ajjhattaṃ upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa uppādo ^4512

hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hoti tañca ^4513

pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ ^4514

āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā bahiddhā dhammesu cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4515

Kathañca bhikkhu bahiddhā dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu santaṃ vāssa ^4516

kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthissa kāmacchando’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthissa ^4517

kāmacchando’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa kāmacchandassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca ^4518

uppannassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ ^4519

anuppādo hoti tañca pajānāti. Santaṃ vāssa byāpādaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa thinamiddhaṃ…pe… ^4520

santaṃ vāssa uddhaccakukkuccaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthissa vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, ^4521

asantaṃ vāssa vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthissa vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannāya vicikicchāya ^4522

uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca ^4523

pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādo hoti tañca pajānāti. ^4524

Santaṃ vāssa satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthissa satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa ^4525

satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthissa satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa ^4526

satisambojjhaṅgassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa satisambojjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya ^4527

pāripūrī hoti tañca pajānāti. Santaṃ vāssa dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa ^4528

vīriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa ^4529

passaddhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vāssa ^4530

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthissa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa ^4531

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthissa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa ^4532

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa ^4533

bhāvanāya pāripūrī hoti tañca pajānāti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti svāvatthitaṃ ^4534

vavatthapeti. So taṃ nimittaṃ āsevitvā bhāvetvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapetvā ^4535

ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu cittaṃ upasaṃharati. ^4536

Kathañca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu santaṃ vā ^4537

kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthi kāmacchando’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthi kāmacchando’’ti ^4538

pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa kāmacchandassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa ^4539

kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādo hoti ^4540

tañca pajānāti. Santaṃ vā byāpādaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā thinamiddhaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ^4541

uddhaccakukkuccaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthi vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ^4542

vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthi vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannāya vicikicchāya uppādo hoti tañca ^4543

pajānāti, yathā ca uppannāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya ^4544

āyatiṃ anuppādo hoti tañca pajānāti. ^4545

Santaṃ vā satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā satisambojjhaṅgaṃ ^4546

‘‘natthi satisambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppannassa satisambojjhaṅgassa uppādo hoti tañca ^4547

pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa satisambojjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hoti tañca pajānāti. Santaṃ vā ^4548

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā vīriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ^4549

pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā passaddhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā ^4550

samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ…pe… santaṃ vā upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti ^4551

pajānāti, asantaṃ vā upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca ^4552

anuppannassa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa uppādo hoti tañca pajānāti, yathā ca uppannassa ^4553

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hoti tañca pajānāti. Evaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā ^4554

dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4555

368. Anupassīti. Tattha katamā anupassanā? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo ^4556

sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuccati anupassanā. Imāya anupassanāya upeto hoti samupeto upāgato ^4557

samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘anupassī’’ti. ^4558

369. Viharatīti. Iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^4559

370. Ātāpīti. Tattha katamo ātāpo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ ^4560

vuccati ‘‘ātāpo’’. Iminā ātāpena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘ātāpī’’ti. ^4561

371. Sampajānoti. Tattha katamaṃ sampajaññaṃ? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho ^4562

dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sampajaññaṃ’’. Iminā sampajaññena upeto hoti…pe… ^4563

samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘sampajāno’’ti. ^4564

372. Satimāti. Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. Imāya ^4565

satiyā upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘satimā’’ti. ^4566

373. Vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassanti. Tattha katamo loko? Teva dhammā loko. Pañcapi ^4567

upādānakkhandhā loko. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘loko’’. Tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa ^4568

sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abhijjhā’’. Tattha katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ ^4569

dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ ^4570

vuccati ‘‘domanassaṃ’’. Iti ayañca abhijjhā idañca domanassaṃ imamhi loke vinītā honti paṭivinītā ^4571

santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena ^4572

vuccati ‘‘vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassa’’nti. ^4573

Dhammānupassanāniddeso. ^4574

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^4575

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^4576

374. Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati, vedanāsu vedanānupassī ^4577

viharati, citte cittānupassī viharati, dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati. ^4578

375. Kathañca bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4579

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4580

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ ^4581

kāye kāyānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^4582

maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4583

376. Kathañca bhikkhu vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4584

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4585

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ ^4586

vedanāsu vedanānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^4587

maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati satipaṭṭhānaṃ. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4588

377. Kathañca bhikkhu citte cittānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ ^4589

bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva ^4590

kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ citte ^4591

cittānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^4592

maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4593

378. Kathañca bhikkhu dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye ^4594

lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā ^4595

pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^4596

dandhābhiññaṃ dhammesu dhammānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati ^4597

satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^4598

satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4599

379. Tattha katamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^4600

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^4601

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ dhammesu ^4602

dhammānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^4603

maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4604

380. Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati, vedanāsu vedanānupassī ^4605

viharati, citte cittānupassī viharati, dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati. ^4606

381. Kathañca bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4607

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4608

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, ^4609

tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa ^4610

jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^4611

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ kāye kāyānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati ^4612

sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. ^4613

Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4614

382. Kathañca bhikkhu vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4615

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4616

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, ^4617

tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa ^4618

jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^4619

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ vedanāsu vedanānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati ^4620

sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. ^4621

Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4622

383. Kathañca bhikkhu citte cittānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ ^4623

bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva ^4624

kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ ^4625

samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa ^4626

katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^4627

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ citte cittānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati ^4628

sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. ^4629

Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4630

384. Kathañca bhikkhu dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye ^4631

lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā ^4632

pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^4633

dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva ^4634

lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ ^4635

upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ dhammesu dhammānupassī, yā ^4636

tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ ^4637

vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4638

385. Tattha katamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^4639

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^4640

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^4641

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^4642

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^4643

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^4644

maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā. ^4645

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^4646

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^4647

386. Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya ^4648

loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ, vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4649

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, citte cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4650

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^4651

abhijjhādomanassaṃ. ^4652

387. Catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati ^4653

araṇā? ^4654

1. Tikaṃ ^4655

388. Siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā. Siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya ^4656

vedanāya sampayuttā. Siyā vipākā siyā vipākadhammadhammā. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā. ^4657

Asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Siyā savitakkasavicārā, siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā avitakkaavicārā. Siyā ^4658

pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upekkhāsahagatā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Neva ^4659

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Siyā apacayagāmino, siyā nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. ^4660

Siyā sekkhā, siyā asekkhā. Appamāṇā. Appamāṇārammaṇā. Paṇītā. Siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. ^4661

Na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahetukā, siyā maggādhipatino, siyā na vattabbā maggahetukātipi, ^4662

maggādhipatinotipi. Siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino. Siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā ^4663

paccuppannā. Na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, paccuppannārammaṇātipi. Siyā ^4664

ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. Bahiddhārammaṇā. Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^4665

2. Dukaṃ ^4666

389. Na hetū. Sahetukā. Hetusampayuttā. Na vattabbā hetū ceva sahetukā cāti, sahetukā ceva na ca ^4667

hetū. Na vattabbā hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cāti, hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū. Na hetū sahetukā. ^4668

Sappaccayā. Saṅkhatā. Anidassanā. Appaṭighā. Arūpā. Lokuttarā. Kenaci viññeyyā, kenaci na ^4669

viññeyyā. No āsavā. Anāsavā. Āsavavippayuttā. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva sāsavācātipi, sāsavā ceva no ca ^4670

āsavātipi. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttācātipi, āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. ^4671

Āsavavippayuttā. Anāsavā. No saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no ^4672

nīvaraṇā…pe… no parāmāsā…pe… sārammaṇā. No cittā. Cetasikā. Cittasampayuttā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhā. ^4673

Cittasamuṭṭhānā. Cittasahabhuno. Cittānuparivattino. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā. ^4674

Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino. Bāhirā. No upādā. ^4675

Anupādinnā. No upādānā…pe… no kilesā…pe… na dassanena pahātabbā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Na ^4676

dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. ^4677

Siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. Siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. Siyā ^4678

pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Siyā upekkhāsahagatā, ^4679

siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Na kāmāvacarā. Na rūpāvacarā. Na arūpāvacarā. Apariyāpannā. Siyā ^4680

niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā. Siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā. Anuttarā. Araṇāti. ^4681

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^4682

Satipaṭṭhānavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^4683

8. Sammappadhānavibhaṅgo ^4684

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^4685

390. Cattāro sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4686

anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ ^4687

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4688

paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4689

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya ^4690

bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4691

paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4692

391. Kathañca bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ ^4693

janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Tattha katame anuppannā pāpakā akusalā ^4694

dhammā? Tīṇi akusalamūlāni – lobho, doso, moho. Tadekaṭṭhā ca kilesā. Taṃsampayutto ^4695

vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ ^4696

kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manokammaṃ – ime vuccanti ‘‘anuppannā pāpakā akusalā dhammā’’. Iti ^4697

imesaṃ anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4698

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4699

392. Chandaṃ janetīti. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā [chandīkatā (syā.)] ^4700

kattukamyatā kusalo dhammacchando – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chando’’. Imaṃ chandaṃ janeti sañjaneti ^4701

uṭṭhapeti samuṭṭhapeti [uṭṭhāpeti samuṭṭhāpeti (syā.) evamuparipi] nibbatteti abhinibbatteti. Tena vuccati ^4702

‘‘chandaṃ janetī’’ti. ^4703

393. Vāyamatīti. Tattha katamo vāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ ^4704

vuccati ‘‘vāyāmo’’. Iminā vāyāmena upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato upapanno sampanno ^4705

samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti. ^4706

394. Vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4707

– idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. Imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena ^4708

vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti. ^4709

395. Cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4710

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambheti ^4711

paccupatthambheti. Tena vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti. ^4712

396. Padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ^4713

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’. Iminā padhānena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^4714

‘‘padahatī’’ti. ^4715

397. Kathañca bhikkhu uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ ^4716

janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Tattha katame uppannā pāpakā akusalā ^4717

dhammā? Tīṇi akusalamūlāni – lobho, doso, moho. Tadekaṭṭhā ca kilesā. Taṃsampayutto ^4718

vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ ^4719

kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manokammaṃ – ime vuccanti ‘‘uppannā pāpakā akusalā dhammā’’. Iti ^4720

imesaṃ uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ^4721

ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4722

398. Chandaṃ janetīti. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo ^4723

dhammacchando – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chando’’. Imaṃ chandaṃ janeti sañjaneti uṭṭhapeti samuṭṭhapeti ^4724

nibbatteti abhinibbatteti. Tena vuccati ‘‘chandaṃ janetī’’ti. ^4725

399. Vāyamatīti. Tattha katamo vāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ ^4726

vuccati ‘‘vāyāmo’’. Iminā vāyāmena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti. ^4727

400. Vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4728

– idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. Imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena ^4729

vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti. ^4730

401. Cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4731

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambheti ^4732

paccupatthambheti. Tena vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti. ^4733

402. Padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ^4734

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’. Iminā padhānena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^4735

‘‘padahatī’’ti. ^4736

403. Kathañca bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4737

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Tattha katame anuppannā kusalā dhammā? Tīṇi ^4738

kusalamūlāni – alobho, adoso, amoho. Taṃsampayutto vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^4739

saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho. Taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammaṃ, vacīkammaṃ, manokammaṃ – ^4740

ime vuccanti ‘‘anuppannā kusalā dhammā’’. Iti imesaṃ anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4741

uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4742

404. Chandaṃ janetīti…pe… vāyamatīti…pe… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pe… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti… ^4743

pe… padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho …pe… sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ ^4744

vuccati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’. Iminā padhānena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘padahatī’’ti. ^4745

405. Kathañca bhikkhu uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya ^4746

vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? ^4747

Tattha katame uppannā kusalā dhammā? Tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alobho, adoso, amoho. Taṃsampayutto ^4748

vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ ^4749

kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manokammaṃ – ime vuccanti ‘‘uppannā kusalā dhammā’’. Iti imesaṃ ^4750

uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya ^4751

pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4752

406. Ṭhitiyāti. Yā ṭhiti so asammoso, yo asammoso so bhiyyobhāvo, yo bhiyyobhāvo taṃ ^4753

vepullaṃ, yaṃ vepullaṃ sā bhāvanā, yā bhāvanā sā pāripūrī. ^4754

407. Chandaṃ janetīti…pe… vāyamatīti…pe… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pe… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti… ^4755

pe… padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ ^4756

vuccati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’. Iminā padhānena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘padahatī’’ti. ^4757

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^4758

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^4759

408. Cattāro sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4760

anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ ^4761

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4762

paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4763

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya ^4764

bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4765

paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4766

409. Kathañca bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ ^4767

janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4768

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4769

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, ^4770

tasmiṃ samaye anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti ^4771

vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4772

410. Chandaṃ janetīti. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo ^4773

dhammacchando – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chando’’. Imaṃ chandaṃ janeti sañjaneti uṭṭhapeti samuṭṭhapeti ^4774

nibbatteti abhinibbatteti. Tena vuccati ‘‘chandaṃ janetī’’ti. ^4775

411. Vāyamatīti. Tattha katamo vāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4776

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vāyāmo’’. Iminā vāyāmena ^4777

upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^4778

‘‘vāyamatī’’ti. ^4779

412. Vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4780

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. Imaṃ vīriyaṃ ^4781

ārabhati samārabhati āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti. ^4782

413. Cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4783

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambheti ^4784

paccupatthambheti. Tena vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti. ^4785

414. Padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… ^4786

sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^4787

‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā. ^4788

415. Kathañca bhikkhu uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ ^4789

janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ ^4790

jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā ^4791

vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, ^4792

tasmiṃ samaye uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4793

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4794

416. Chandaṃ janetīti…pe… vāyamatīti…pe… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pe… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti… ^4795

pe… padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4796

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’. Avasesā ^4797

dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā. ^4798

417. Kathañca bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4799

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^4800

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^4801

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^4802

anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4803

paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4804

418. Chandaṃ janetīti…pe… vāyamatīti…pe… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pe… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti… ^4805

pe… padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho …pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4806

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’. Avasesā ^4807

dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā. ^4808

419. Kathañca bhikkhu uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya ^4809

vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? ^4810

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ ^4811

pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^4812

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā ^4813

asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati ^4814

cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4815

420. Ṭhitiyāti. Yā ṭhiti so asammoso, yo asammoso so bhiyyobhāvo, yo bhiyyobhāvo taṃ ^4816

vepullaṃ, yaṃ vepullaṃ sā bhāvanā, yā bhāvanā sā pāripūrī. ^4817

421. Chandaṃ janetīti. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo ^4818

dhammacchando – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chando’’. Imaṃ chandaṃ janeti sañjaneti uṭṭhapeti samuṭṭhapeti ^4819

nibbatteti abhinibbatteti. Tena vuccati ‘‘chandaṃ janetī’’ti. ^4820

422. Vāyamatīti. Tattha katamo vāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4821

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vāyāmo’’. Iminā vāyāmena ^4822

upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti. ^4823

423. Vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4824

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. Imaṃ vīriyaṃ ^4825

ārabhati samārabhati āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti. ^4826

424. Cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^4827

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambheti ^4828

paccupatthambheti. Tena vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti. ^4829

425. Padahatīti. Tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… ^4830

sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^4831

‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā. ^4832

426. Tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^4833

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^4834

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yo tasmiṃ samaye ^4835

cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ^4836

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā. ^4837

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^4838

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^4839

427. Cattāro sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4840

anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ ^4841

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pe… anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4842

uppādāya…pe… uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya ^4843

bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. ^4844

428. Catunnaṃ sammappadhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati ^4845

araṇā? ^4846

1. Tikaṃ ^4847

429. Kusalāyeva. Siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^4848

sampayuttā. Vipākadhammadhammā. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Siyā ^4849

savitakkasavicārā, siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā avitakkaavicārā. Siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, ^4850

siyā upekkhāsahagatā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya ^4851

pahātabbahetukā. Apacayagāmino. Sekkhā. Appamāṇā. Appamāṇārammaṇā. Paṇītā. Sammattaniyatā. ^4852

Na maggārammaṇā. Maggahetukā. Siyā maggādhipatino, siyā na vattabbā maggādhipatinoti. Siyā ^4853

uppannā, siyā anuppannā, na vattabbā uppādinoti. Siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. Na ^4854

vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, paccuppannārammaṇātipi. Siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, ^4855

siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. Bahiddhārammaṇā. Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^4856

2. Dukaṃ ^4857

430. Na hetū. Sahetukā. Hetusampayuttā. Na vattabbā hetū ceva sahetukā cāti, sahetukā ceva na ca ^4858

hetū. Na vattabbā hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cāti, hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū. Na hetū sahetukā. ^4859

Sappaccayā. Saṅkhatā. Anidassanā. Appaṭighā. Arūpā. Lokuttarā. Kenaci viññeyyā, kenaci na viññeyyā. ^4860

No āsavā. Anāsavā. Āsavavippayuttā. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā ceva no ca āsavātipi. ^4861

Na vattabbā āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Āsavavippayuttā. ^4862

Anāsavā. No saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no nīvaraṇā…pe… no ^4863

parāmāsā…pe… sārammaṇā. No cittā. Cetasikā. Cittasampayuttā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhā. Cittasamuṭṭhānā. ^4864

Cittasahabhuno. Cittānuparivattino. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno. ^4865

Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino. Bāhirā. No upādā. Anupādinnā. No upādānā…pe… no kilesā… ^4866

pe… na dassanena pahātabbā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Na bhāvanāya ^4867

pahātabbahetukā. Siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. Siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. ^4868

Siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Siyā ^4869

upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Na kāmāvacarā. Na rūpāvacarā. Na arūpāvacarā. ^4870

Apariyāpannā. Niyyānikā. Niyatā. Anuttarā. Araṇāti. ^4871

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^4872

Sammappadhānavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^4873

9. Iddhipādavibhaṅgo ^4874

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^4875

431. Cattāro iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ ^4876

bhāveti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, ^4877

cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, ^4878

vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^4879

1. Chandiddhipādo ^4880

432. Kathañca bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? ^4881

Chandaṃ ce bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassekaggataṃ [cittassa ekaggataṃ ^4882

(sī. syā.)] – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chandasamādhi’’. So anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^4883

anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ ^4884

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4885

paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^4886

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya ^4887

bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^4888

paggaṇhāti padahati. Ime vuccanti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’. Iti ayañca chandasamādhi, ime ca ^4889

padhānasaṅkhārā. Tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññahitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārotveva ^4890

saṅkhyaṃ [saṅkhaṃ (sī.)] gacchati. ^4891

433. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo dhammacchando – ayaṃ ^4892

vuccati ‘‘chando’’. ^4893

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā ^4894

samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^4895

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ^4896

ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo ṭhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho ^4897

vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca ^4898

chandena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato ^4899

upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^4900

434. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^4901

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^4902

‘‘Iddhipādo’’ti. Tathābhūtassa vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, ^4903

viññāṇakkhandho. ^4904

‘‘Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetī’’ti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^4905

bhāvetī’’ti. ^4906

2. Vīriyiddhipādo ^4907

435. Kathañca bhikkhu vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? ^4908

Vīriyaṃ ce bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassekaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^4909

‘‘vīriyasamādhi’’. So anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti ^4910

vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ ^4911

dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pe… anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pe… uppannānaṃ ^4912

kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ ^4913

janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Ime vuccanti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’. Iti ^4914

ayañca vīriyasamādhi, ime ca padhānasaṅkhārā; tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā ^4915

vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārotveva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati. ^4916

436. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ vuccati ^4917

‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. ^4918

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā ^4919

samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^4920

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ vuccati ^4921

‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca vīriyena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto ^4922

hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^4923

437. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^4924

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^4925

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa vedanākkhandho…pe… viññāṇakkhandho. ^4926

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^4927

bhāvetī’’ti. ^4928

3. Cittiddhipādo ^4929

438. Kathañca bhikkhu cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? Cittaṃ ^4930

ce bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassekaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^4931

‘‘cittasamādhi’’. So anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti ^4932

vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ ^4933

dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pe… anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pe… uppannānaṃ ^4934

kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ ^4935

cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārotveva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati. ^4938

439. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ ^4939

vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. ^4940

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuccati ^4941

‘‘samādhi’’. ^4942

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ vuccati ^4943

‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca cittena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti… ^4944

pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^4945

440. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^4946

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^4947

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa vedanākkhandho…pe… viññāṇakkhandho. ^4948

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^4949

bhāvetī’’ti. ^4950

4. Vīmaṃsiddhipādo ^4951

441. Kathañca bhikkhu vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? ^4952

Vīmaṃsaṃ ce bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassekaggataṃ – ayaṃ ^4953

vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhi’’. So anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya ^4954

chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ ^4955

akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pe… anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pe… ^4956

uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya ^4957

pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati. Ime vuccanti ^4958

‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’. Iti ayañca vīmaṃsāsamādhi, ime ca padhānasaṅkhārā; tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā ^4959

abhisaṅkhipitvā vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārotveva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati. ^4960

442. Tattha katamā vīmaṃsā? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ^4961

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsā’’. ^4962

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuccati ^4963

‘‘samādhi’’. ^4964

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho …pe… sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ vuccati ^4965

‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti imāya ca vīmaṃsāya, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto ^4966

hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^4967

443. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^4968

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^4969

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakhandho. ^4970

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^4971

bhāvetī’’ti. ^4972

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^4973

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^4974

444. Cattāro iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ ^4975

bhāveti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, ^4976

cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti, ^4977

vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^4978

1. Chandiddhipādo ^4979

445. Kathañca bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? Idha ^4980

bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya ^4981

paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^4982

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ ^4983

iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^4984

446. Tattha katamo chando? Yo chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo dhammacchando – ayaṃ ^4985

vuccati ‘‘chando’’. ^4986

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^4987

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^4988

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^4989

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca ^4990

chandena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena ^4991

vuccati ‘‘chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^4992

447. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^4993

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^4994

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa phasso…pe… paggāho avikkhepo. ^4995

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^4996

bhāvetī’’ti. ^4997

2. Vīriyiddhipādo ^4998

448. Kathañca bhikkhu vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? Idha ^4999

bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya ^5000

paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5001

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ ^5002

iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^5003

449. Tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5004

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’. ^5005

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5006

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^5007

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5008

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca ^5009

vīriyena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena ^5010

vuccati ‘‘vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^5011

450. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^5012

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^5013

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtasa phasso…pe… paggāho avikkhepo. ^5014

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^5015

bhāvetī’’ti. ^5016

3. Cittiddhipādo ^5017

451. Kathañca bhikkhu cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? Idha ^5018

bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya ^5019

paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5020

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ ^5021

iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^5022

452. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ ^5023

vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. ^5024

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5025

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^5026

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5027

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti iminā ca ^5028

cittena, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^5029

‘‘cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti. ^5030

453. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^5031

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^5032

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa phasso…pe… paggāho avikkhepo. ^5033

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^5034

bhāvetī’’ti. ^5035

4. Vīmaṃsiddhipādo ^5036

454. Kathañca bhikkhu vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti? Idha ^5037

bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya ^5038

paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5039

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ ^5040

iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^5041

455. Tattha katamā vīmaṃsā? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi ^5042

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsā’’. ^5043

Tattha katamo samādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5044

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhi’’. ^5045

Tattha katamo padhānasaṅkhāro? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5046

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhāro’’. Iti imāya ca ^5047

vīmaṃsāya, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhārena upeto hoti samupeto upāgato samupāgato ^5048

upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato’’ti ^5049

456. Iddhīti. Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābho paṭilābho patti ^5050

sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^5051

Iddhipādoti. Tathābhūtassa phasso…pe… paggāho avikkhepo. ^5052

Iddhipādaṃ bhāvetīti. Te dhamme āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ ^5053

bhāvetī’’ti. ^5054

457. Cattāro iddhipādā – chandiddhipādo, vīriyiddhipādo, cittiddhipādo, vīmaṃsiddhipādo. ^5055

458. Tattha katamo chandiddhipādo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5056

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5057

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yo tasmiṃ samaye ^5058

chando chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalo dhammacchando – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘chandiddhipādo’’. Avasesā ^5059

dhammā chandiddhipādasampayuttā. ^5060

459. Tattha katamo vīriyiddhipādo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5061

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5062

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yo tasmiṃ samaye ^5063

cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggariyāpannaṃ – ^5064

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyiddhipādo’’. Avasesā dhammā vīriyiddhipādasampayuttā. ^5065

460. Tattha katamo cittiddhipādo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5066

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi … ^5067

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ^5068

cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittiddhipādo’’. Avasesā ^5069

dhammā cittiddhipādasampayuttā. ^5070

461. Tattha katamo vīmaṃsiddhipādo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5071

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5072

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye ^5073

paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^5074

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīmaṃsiddhipādo’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5075

vīmaṃsiddhipādasampayuttā. ^5076

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^5077

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^5078

462. Cattāro iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ ^5079

bhāveti, vīriyasamādhi…pe… cittasamādhi…pe… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ ^5080

iddhipādaṃ bhāveti. ^5081

463. Catunnaṃ iddhipādānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā? ^5082

1. Tikaṃ ^5083

464. Kusalāyeva. Siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^5084

sampayuttā. Vipākadhammadhammā. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Siyā ^5085

savitakkasavicārā, siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā avitakkaavicārā. Siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, ^5086

siyā upekkhāsahagatā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya ^5087

pahātabbahetukā. Apacayagāmino. Sekkhā. Appamāṇā. Appamāṇārammaṇā. Paṇītā. Sammattaniyatā. ^5088

Na maggārammaṇā, maggahetukā, na maggādhipatino. Siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, na vattabbā ^5089

uppādinoti. Siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. Na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, ^5090

anāgatārammaṇātipi, paccuppannārammaṇātipi. Siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. ^5091

Bahiddhārammaṇā. Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^5092

2. Dukaṃ ^5093

465. Vīmaṃsiddhipādo hetu, tayo iddhipādā na hetū. Sahetukā. Hetusampayuttā. Vīmaṃsiddhipādo ^5094

hetu ceva sahetuko ca, tayo iddhipādā na vattabbā hetū ceva sahetukā cāti, sahetukā ceva na ca hetū. ^5095

Vīmaṃsiddhipādo hetu ceva hetusampayutto ca, tayo iddhipādā na vattabbā hetū ceva hetusampayuttā ^5096

cāti, hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū. Tayo iddhipādā na hetū sahetukā, vīmaṃsiddhipādo na vattabbo ^5097

na hetu sahetukotipi, na hetu ahetukotipi. Sappaccayā. Saṅkhatā. Anidassanā. Appaṭighā. Arūpā. ^5098

Lokuttarā. Kenaci viññeyyā, kenaci na viññeyyā. No āsavā. Anāsavā. Āsavavippayuttā. Na vattabbā ^5099

āsavā ceva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, ^5100

āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Āsavavippayuttā. Anāsavā. ^5101

No saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no nīvaraṇā…pe… no ^5102

parāmāsā…pe… sārammaṇā. Tayo iddhipādā no cittā, cittiddhipādo cittaṃ. Tayo iddhipādā cetasikā, ^5103

cittiddhipādo acetasiko. Tayo iddhipādā cittasampayuttā, cittiddhipādo na vattabbo cittena ^5104

sampayuttotipi, cittena vippayuttotipi. Tayo iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittiddhipādo na vattabbo cittena ^5105

saṃsaṭṭhotipi, cittena visaṃsaṭṭhotipi. Tayo iddhipādā cittasamuṭṭhānā, cittiddhipādo no cittasamuṭṭhāno. ^5106

Tayo iddhipādā cittasahabhuno, cittiddhipādo no cittasahabhū. Tayo iddhipādā cittānuparivattino, ^5107

cittiddhipādo no cittānuparivatti. Tayo iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittiddhipādo no ^5108

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhāno. Tayo iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno, cittiddhipādo no ^5109

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū. Tayo iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino, cittiddhipādo ^5110

no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti. ^5111

Tayo iddhipādā bāhirā, cittiddhipādo ajjhattiko. No upādā. Anupādinnā. No upādānā…pe… no ^5112

kilesā…pe… na dassanena pahātabbā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Na ^5113

bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. Siyā sappītikā, ^5114

siyā appītikā. Siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Siyā ^5115

upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Na kāmāvacarā. Na rūpāvacarā. Na arūpāvacarā. ^5116

Apariyāpannā. Niyyānikā. Niyatā. Anuttarā. Araṇāti. ^5117

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^5118

Iddhipādavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^5119

10. Bojjhaṅgavibhaṅgo ^5120

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^5121

466. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, ^5122

pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5123

467. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena ^5124

samannāgato, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā hoti anussaritā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘satisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5125

So tathā sato viharanto taṃ dhammaṃ paññāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpajjati – ayaṃ ^5126

vuccati ‘‘dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5127

Tassa taṃ dhammaṃ paññāya pavicinato pavicarato parivīmaṃsamāpajjato āraddhaṃ hoti vīriyaṃ ^5128

asallīnaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyasambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5129

Āraddhavīriyassa uppajjati pīti nirāmisā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pītisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5130

Pītimanassa kāyopi passambhati, cittampi passambhati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘passaddhisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5131

Passaddhakāyassa sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5132

So tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupekkhitā hoti – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5133

468. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, ^5134

pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5135

469. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu sati, atthi bahiddhā dhammesu ^5136

sati. Yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu sati tadapi satisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya ^5137

saṃvattati. Yadapi bahiddhā dhammesu sati tadapi satisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya ^5138

saṃvattati. ^5139

Tattha katamo dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo? Atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu pavicayo, atthi bahiddhā ^5140

dhammesu pavicayo. Yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu pavicayo tadapi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo ^5141

abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi bahiddhā dhammesu pavicayo tadapi ^5142

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5143

Tattha katamo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo? Atthi kāyikaṃ vīriyaṃ, atthi cetasikaṃ vīriyaṃ. Yadapi ^5144

kāyikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi ^5145

cetasikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5146

Tattha katamo pītisambojjhaṅgo? Atthi savitakkasavicārā pīti, atthi avitakkaavicārā pīti. Yadapi ^5147

savitakkasavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi ^5148

avitakkaavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5149

Tattha katamo passaddhisambojjhaṅgo? Atthi kāyapassaddhi [kāyappassaddhi (syā. ka.)], atthi ^5150

cittapassaddhi [cittappassaddhi (syā. ka.)]. Yadapi kāyapassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambojjhaṅgo ^5151

abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi cittapassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambojjhaṅgo ^5152

abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5153

Tattha katamo samādhisambojjhaṅgo? Atthi savitakko savicāro samādhi, atthi avitakko avicāro ^5154

samādhi. Yadapi savitakko savicāro samādhi tadapi samādhisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya sambodhāya ^5155

nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi avitakko avicāro samādhi tadapi samādhisambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya ^5156

sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5157

Tattha katamo upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo? Atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu upekkhā, atthi bahiddhā ^5158

dhammesu upekkhā. Yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammesu upekkhā tadapi upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo abhiññāya ^5159

sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. Yadapi bahiddhā dhammesu upekkhā tadapi upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo ^5160

abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati. ^5161

470. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, ^5162

pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5163

471. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu satisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ ^5164

virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti…pe… ^5165

vīriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti… pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti… passaddhisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti… ^5166

samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ ^5167

nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ. ^5168

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^5169

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^5170

472. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, ^5171

pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5172

473. Tattha katame satta bojjhaṅgā? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5173

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5174

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye satta ^5175

bojjhaṅgā honti – satisambojjhaṅgo…pe… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5176

474. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo ^5177

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘satisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5178

Tattha katamo dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo ^5179

sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^5180

‘‘dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5181

Tattha katamo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5182

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vīriyasambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5183

Tattha katamo pītisambojjhaṅgo? Yā pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ ^5184

[odaggaṃ (sī.) dha. sa. 9] attamanatā cittassa pītisambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pītisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5185

Tattha katamo passaddhisambojjhaṅgo? Yā vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa ^5186

saṅkhārakkhandhassa viññāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭippassaddhi passambhanā paṭippassambhanā ^5187

paṭippassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘passaddhisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5188

Tattha katamo samādhisambojjhaṅgo? Yā cittassa ṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi ^5189
