samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘samādhisambojjhaṅgo’’. ^5190

Tattha katamo upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo? Yā upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā cittassa ^5191

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’. Ime vuccanti satta bojjhaṅgā. Avasesā ^5192

dhammā sattahi bojjhaṅgehi sampayuttā. ^5193

475. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo…pe… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5194

476. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5195

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5196

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati ^5197

anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^5198

‘‘satisambojjhaṅgo’’. Avasesā dhammā satisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā ^5199

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā vīriyasambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… ^5200

avasesā dhammā pītisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā ^5201

passaddhisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā samādhisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā. ^5202

Tattha katamo upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5203

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5204

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye ^5205

upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā cittassa upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ^5206

‘‘upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’. Avasesā dhammā upekkhāsambojjhaṅgasampayuttā. ^5207

477. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo…pe… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5208

478. Tattha katame satta bojjhaṅgā? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5209

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi … ^5210

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yo tasmiṃ samaye ^5211

phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti – ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa ^5212

katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5213

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye satta bojjhaṅgā honti – ^5214

satisambojjhaṅgo…pe… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5215

479. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo ^5216

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘satisambojjhaṅgo’’…pe…. ^5217

Tattha katamo upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo? Yā upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā cittassa ^5218

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’. Ime vuccanti ‘‘satta bojjhaṅgā’’. ^5219

Avasesā dhammā sattahi bojjhaṅgehi sampayuttā. ^5220

480. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo…pe… upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5221

481. Tattha katamo satisambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5222

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5223

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5224

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5225

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5226

dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^5227

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘satisambojjhaṅgo’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5228

satisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgasampayuttā …pe… ^5229

avasesā dhammā vīriyasambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā ^5230

pītisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā passaddhisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā…pe… avasesā ^5231

dhammā samādhisambojjhaṅgasampayuttā. ^5232

Tattha katamo upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5233

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5234

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5235

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5236

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5237

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā cittassa ^5238

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5239

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgasampayuttā. ^5240

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^5241

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^5242

482. Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, ^5243

pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. ^5244

483. Sattannaṃ bojjhaṅgānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati ^5245

araṇā? ^5246

1. Tikaṃ ^5247

484. Siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā. Pītisambojjhaṅgo sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto; cha bojjhaṅgā ^5248

siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. Siyā vipākā, siyā ^5249

vipākadhammadhammā. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Siyā savitakkasavicārā, ^5250

siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā avitakkaavicārā. Pītisambojjhaṅgo na pītisahagato, sukhasahagato, na ^5251

upekkhāsahagato; cha bojjhaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upekkhāsahagatā. Neva ^5252

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Siyā ^5253

apacayagāmino, siyā nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Siyā sekkhā, siyā asekkhā. Appamāṇā. ^5254

Appamāṇārammaṇā. Paṇītā. Siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. Na maggārammaṇā, siyā ^5255

maggahetukā, siyā maggādhipatino; siyā na vattabbā maggahetukātipi, maggādhipatinotipi. Siyā ^5256

uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino. Siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. Na vattabbā ^5257

atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, paccuppannārammaṇātipi. Siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ^5258

ajjhattabahiddhā. Bahiddhārammaṇā. Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^5259

2. Dukaṃ ^5260

485. Dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo hetu, cha bojjhaṅgā hetusampayuttā. ^5261

Dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo hetu ceva sahetuko ca, cha bojjhaṅgā na vattabbā hetū ceva sahetukā cāti, ^5262

sahetukā ceva na ca hetū. Dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo hetu ceva hetusampayutto ca, cha bojjhaṅgā na ^5263

vattabbā hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cāti, hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū. Cha bojjhaṅgā na hetū ^5264

sahetukā, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo na vattabbo na hetusahetukotipi, na hetuahetukotipi. Sappaccayā. ^5265

Saṅkhatā. Anidassanā. Appaṭighā. Arūpā. Lokuttarā. Kenaci viññeyyā, kenaci na viññeyyā. No āsavā. ^5266

Anāsavā āsavavippayuttā. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Āsavā ceva ^5267

āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Āsavavippayuttā. Anāsavā. No ^5268

saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no nīvaraṇā…pe… no parāmāsā… ^5269

pe… sārammaṇā. No cittā. Cetasikā. Cittasampayuttā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhā. Cittasamuṭṭhānā. Cittasahabhuno. ^5270

Cittānuparivattino. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno. ^5271

Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino. Bāhirā. No upādā. Anupādinnā. No upādānā…pe… no kilesā… ^5272

pe… na dassanena pahātabbā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Na dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Na bhāvanāya ^5273

pahātabbahetukā. Siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. ^5274

Pītisambojjhaṅgo appītiko, cha bojjhaṅgā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. Pītisambojjhaṅgo na ^5275

pītisahagato, cha bojjhaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Pītisambojjhaṅgo sukhasahagato, cha ^5276

bojjhaṅgā siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Pītisambojjhaṅgo na upekkhāsahagato, cha ^5277

bojjhaṅgā siyā upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Na kāmāvacarā. Na rūpāvacarā. Na ^5278

arūpāvacarā. Apariyāpannā. Siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā. Siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā. Anuttarā. ^5279

Araṇāti. ^5280

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^5281

Bojjhaṅgavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^5282

11. Maggaṅgavibhaṅgo ^5283

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^5284

486. Ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvācā, ^5285

sammākammanto, sammāājīvo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^5286

487. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Dukkhe ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudaye ñāṇaṃ, dukkhanirodhe ñāṇaṃ, ^5287

dukkhanirodhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’. ^5288

Tattha katamo sammāsaṅkappo? Nekkhammasaṅkappo, abyāpādasaṅkappo, avihiṃsāsaṅkappo – ^5289

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappo’’. ^5290

Tattha katamā sammāvācā? Musāvādā veramaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya veramaṇī, pharusāya vācāya ^5291

veramaṇī, samphappalāpā veramaṇī – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāvācā’’. ^5292

Tattha katamo sammākammanto? Pāṇātipātā veramaṇī, adinnādānā veramaṇī, kāmesumicchācārā ^5293

veramaṇī – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammākammanto’’. ^5294

Tattha katamo sammāājīvo? Idha ariyasāvako micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvena jīvikaṃ kappeti ^5295

– ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāājīvo’’. ^5296

Tattha katamo sammāvāyāmo? Idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^5297

anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ ^5298

pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^5299

paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati ^5300

vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya ^5301

bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ ^5302

paggaṇhāti padahati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāvāyāmo’’. ^5303

Tattha katamā sammāsati? Idha bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya ^5304

loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ, vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^5305

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, citte cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^5306

abhijjhādomanassaṃ, dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke ^5307

abhijjhādomanassaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsati’’. ^5308

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Idha bhikkhu vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi ^5309

savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; vitakkavicārānaṃ ^5310

vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ ^5311

dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno ^5312

sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘‘upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ ^5313

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva ^5314

somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^5315

upasampajja viharati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’. ^5316

488. Ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvācā, ^5317

sammākammanto, sammāājīvo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^5318

489. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Idha bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ ^5319

virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ, sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāveti…pe… sammāvācaṃ ^5320

bhāveti…pe… sammākammantaṃ bhāveti…pe… sammāājīvaṃ bhāveti…pe… sammāvāyāmaṃ ^5321

bhāveti…pe… sammāsatiṃ bhāveti…pe… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ ^5322

nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇāmiṃ. ^5323

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^5324

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^5325

490. Aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvācā, sammākammanto, ^5326

sammāājīvo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^5327

491. Tattha katamo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5328

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5329

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5330

aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo hoti – sammādiṭṭhi…pe… sammāsamādhi. ^5331

492. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi ^5332

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’. ^5333

Tattha katamo sammāsaṅkappo? Yo takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā ^5334

sammāsaṅkappo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappo’’. ^5335

Tattha katamā sammāvācā? Yā catūhi vacīduccaritehi ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā ^5336

akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammāvācā maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ ^5337

vuccati ‘‘sammāvācā’’. ^5338

Tattha katamo sammākammanto? Yā tīhi kāyaduccaritehi ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā ^5339

akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammākammanto maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ^5340

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammākammanto’’. ^5341

Tattha katamo sammāājīvo? Yā micchāājīvā ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ ^5342

anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammāājīvo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^5343

‘‘sammāājīvo’’. ^5344

Tattha katamo sammāvāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5345

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāvāyāmo’’. ^5346

Tattha katamā sammāsati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^5347

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsati’’. ^5348

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5349

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgiko ^5350

maggo’’. Avasesā dhammā aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena sampayuttā. ^5351

493. Pañcaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, ^5352

sammāsamādhi. ^5353

494. Tattha katamo pañcaṅgiko maggo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5354

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5355

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5356

pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^5357

495. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi ^5358

dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’. ^5359

Tattha katamo sammāsaṅkappo? Yo takko vitakko…pe… sammāsaṅkappo maggaṅgaṃ ^5360

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappo’’. ^5361

Tattha katamo sammāvāyāmo? Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo ^5362

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāvāyāmo’’. ^5363

Tattha katamā sammāsati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^5364

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsati’’. ^5365

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Yā cittassa ṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5366

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcaṅgiko ^5367

maggo’’. Avasesā dhammā pañcaṅgikena maggena sampayuttā. ^5368

496. Pañcaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, ^5369

sammāsamādhi. ^5370

497. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5371

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5372

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye ^5373

paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ ^5374

maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’. Avasesā dhammā sammādiṭṭhiyā sampayuttā… ^5375

pe… avasesā dhammā sammāsaṅkappena sampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā sammāvāyāmena ^5376

sampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā sammāsatiyā sampayuttā. ^5377

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5378

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5379

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye ^5380

cittassa ṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ ^5381

vuccati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’. Avasesā dhammā sammāsamādhinā sampayuttā. ^5382

498. Aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi…pe… sammāsamādhi. ^5383

499. Tattha katamo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5384

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5385

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5386

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5387

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5388

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo hoti – sammādiṭṭhi…pe… sammāsamādhi. ^5389

Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo’’. Avasesā dhammā aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena sampayuttā. ^5390

500. Pañcaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, ^5391

sammāsamādhi. ^5392

501. Tattha katamo pañcaṅgiko maggo? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5393

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5394

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5395

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5396

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5397

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, ^5398

sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. Ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcaṅgiko maggo’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5399

pañcaṅgikena maggena sampayuttā. ^5400

502. Pañcaṅgiko maggo – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, ^5401

sammāsamādhi. ^5402

503. Tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5403

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5404

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5405

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5406

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5407

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo ^5408

sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^5409

‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’. Avasesā dhammā sammādiṭṭhiyā sampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā ^5410

sammāsaṅkappena sampayuttā…pe… avasesā dhammā sammāvāyāmena sampayuttā…pe… avasesā ^5411

dhammā sammāsatiyā sampayuttā. ^5412

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5413

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5414

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^5415

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^5416

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5417

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo ^5418

avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo ^5419

maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5420

sammāsamādhinā sampayuttā. ^5421

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^5422

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^5423

504. Ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvācā, ^5424

sammākammanto, sammāājīvo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^5425

505. Aṭṭhannaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati ^5426

araṇā? ^5427

1. Tikaṃ ^5428

506. Siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā. Sammāsaṅkappo sukhāya vedanāya sampayutto; satta maggaṅgā ^5429

siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. Siyā vipākā, siyā ^5430

vipākadhammadhammā. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Sammāsaṅkappo ^5431

avitakkavicāramatto; satta maggaṅgā siyā savitakkasavicārā, siyā avitakkavicāramattā, siyā ^5432

avitakkaavicārā. Sammāsaṅkappo pītisahagato, sukhasahagato, na upekkhāsahagato; satta maggaṅgā ^5433

siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upekkhāsahagatā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. ^5434

Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Siyā apacayagāmino, siyā ^5435

nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Siyā sekkhā, siyā asekkhā. Appamāṇā. Appamāṇārammaṇā. Paṇītā. Siyā ^5436

sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. Na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahetukā, siyā maggādhipatino; siyā na ^5437

vattabbā maggahetukātipi, maggādhipatinotipi. Siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino. Siyā atītā, ^5438

siyā anāgatā, siyā paccuppannā. Na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, ^5439

paccuppannārammaṇātipi. Siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. Bahiddhārammaṇā. ^5440

Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^5441

2. Dukaṃ ^5442

507. Sammādiṭṭhi hetu, satta maggaṅgā na hetū. Sahetukā. Hetusampayuttā. Sammādiṭṭhi hetu ceva ^5443

sahetukā ca, satta maggaṅgā na vattabbā hetū ceva sahetukā cāti, sahetukā ceva na ca hetū. Sammādiṭṭhi ^5444

hetu ceva hetusampayuttā ca, satta maggaṅgā na vattabbā hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cāti, ^5445

hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū. Satta maggaṅgā na hetū sahetukā, sammādiṭṭhi na vattabbā na hetu ^5446

sahetukātipi, na hetu ahetukātipi. ^5447

Sappaccayā. Saṅkhatā. Anidassanā. Appaṭighā. Arūpā. Lokuttarā. Kenaci viññeyyā, kenaci na ^5448

viññeyyā. ^5449

No āsavā. Anāsavā. Āsavavippayuttā. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā ceva no ca ^5450

āsavātipi. Na vattabbā āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. ^5451

Āsavavippayuttā. Anāsavā. ^5452

No saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no nīvaraṇā…pe… no ^5453

parāmāsā…pe… sārammaṇā. No cittā. Cetasikā. Cittasampayuttā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhā. Cittasamuṭṭhānā. ^5454

Cittasahabhuno. Cittānuparivattino. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno. ^5455

Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino. Bāhirā. No upādā. Anupādinnā. ^5456

No upādānā…pe… no kilesā…pe… na dassanena pahātabbā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Na ^5457

dassanena pahātabbahetukā. Na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Sammāsaṅkappo avitakko, satta maggaṅgā ^5458

siyā savitakkā, siyā avitakkā. Sammāsaṅkappo savicāro, satta maggaṅgā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā. ^5459

Sammāsaṅkappo sappītiko, satta maggaṅgā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā. Sammāsaṅkappo pītisahagato, ^5460

satta maggaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā. Sammāsaṅkappo sukhasahagato, satta maggaṅgā ^5461

siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā. Sammāsaṅkappo na upekkhāsahagato, satta maggaṅgā siyā ^5462

upekkhāsahagatā, siyā na upekkhāsahagatā. Na kāmāvacarā. Na rūpāvacarā. Na arūpāvacarā. ^5463

Apariyāpannā. Siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā. Siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā. Anuttarā. Araṇāti. ^5464

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^5465

Maggaṅgavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^5466

12. Jhānavibhaṅgo ^5467

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^5468

508. Idha bhikkhu pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati, ācāragocarasampanno, aṇumattesu vajjesu ^5469

bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu indriyesu guttadvāro bhojane mattaññū ^5470

pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyogamanuyutto sātaccaṃ nepakkaṃ bodhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ ^5471

bhāvanānuyogamanuyutto. So abhikkante paṭikkante sampajānakārī hoti, ālokite vilokite sampajānakārī ^5472

hoti, samiñjite [sammiñjite (sī. syā.)] pasārite sampajānakārī hoti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇe ^5473

sampajānakārī hoti, asite pīte khāyite sāyite sampajānakārī hoti, uccārapassāvakamme sampajānakārī ^5474

hoti, gate ṭhite nisinne sutte jāgarite bhāsite tuṇhībhāve sampajānakārī hoti. So vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ ^5475

bhajati araññaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhokāsaṃ ^5476

palālapuñjaṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghosaṃ vijanavātaṃ manussarāhasseyyakaṃ [manussarāhaseyyakaṃ ^5477

(sī. syā.)] paṭisallānasāruppaṃ. So araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati ^5478

pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā. So abhijjhaṃ loke pahāya ^5479

vigatābhijjhena cetasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisodheti. Byāpādapadosaṃ pahāya abyāpannacitto ^5480

viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadosā cittaṃ parisodheti. Thinamiddhaṃ pahāya ^5481

vigatathinamiddho viharati ālokasaññī sato sampajāno, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisodheti. ^5482

Uddhaccakukkuccaṃ pahāya anuddhato viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacitto, uddhaccakukkuccā cittaṃ ^5483

parisodheti. Vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikiccho viharati akathaṃkathī kusalesu dhammesu, ^5484

vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisodheti. So ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe ^5485

vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ^5486

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ^5487

ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; pītiyā ^5488

ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ^5489

ācikkhanti – ‘‘upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; sukhassa ca ^5490

pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ ^5491

upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati; sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā ^5492

paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘‘ananto ākāso’’ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ ^5493

upasampajja viharati; sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘‘anantaṃ viññāṇa’’nti ^5494

viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘‘natthi kiñcī’’ti ^5495

ākiñcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati; sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ^5496

nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. ^5497

Mātikā ^5498

509. ‘‘Idhā’’ti imissā diṭṭhiyā, imissā khantiyā, imissā ruciyā, imasmiṃ ādāye, imasmiṃ dhamme, ^5499

imasmiṃ vinaye, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye, imasmiṃ pāvacane, imasmiṃ brahmacariye, imasmiṃ ^5500

satthusāsane. Tena vuccati ‘‘idhā’’ti. ^5501

510. ‘‘Bhikkhū’’ti samaññāya bhikkhu, paṭiññāya bhikkhu, bhikkhatīti bhikkhu, bhikkhakoti ^5502

bhikkhu, bhikkhācariyaṃ ajjhupagatoti bhikkhu, bhinnapaṭadharoti bhikkhu, bhindati pāpake akusale ^5503

dhammeti bhikkhu, bhinnattā pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhikkhu, odhiso kilesānaṃ pahānā ^5504

bhikkhu, anodhiso kilesānaṃ pahānā bhikkhu, sekkho bhikkhu, asekkho bhikkhu, nevasekkhanāsekkho ^5505

bhikkhu, aggo bhikkhu, bhadro bhikkhu, maṇḍo bhikkhu, sāro bhikkhu, samaggena saṅghena ^5506

ñatticatutthena kammena akuppena ṭhānārahena upasampanno bhikkhu. ^5507

511. ‘‘Pātimokkha’’nti sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādi caraṇaṃ saṃyamo saṃvaro mokkhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ ^5508

kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Saṃvaroti. Kāyiko avītikkamo, vācasiko avītikkamo, ^5509

kāyikavācasiko avītikkamo. Saṃvutoti. Iminā pātimokkhasaṃvarena upeto hoti samupeto upāgato ^5510

samupāgato upapanno sampanno samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto’’ti. ^5511

512. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5512

513. ‘‘Ācāragocarasampanno’’ti atthi ācāro, atthi anācāro. ^5513

Tattha katamo anācāro? Kāyiko vītikkamo, vācasiko vītikkamo, kāyikavācasiko vītikkamo – ayaṃ ^5514

vuccati ‘‘anācāro’’. Sabbampi dussīlyaṃ anācāro. Idhekacco veḷudānena vā pattadānena vā ^5515

pupphadānena vā phaladānena vā sinānadānena vā dantakaṭṭhadānena vā cāṭukamyatāya vā ^5516

muggasūpyatāya [muggasuppatāya (sī.)] vā pāribhaṭayatāya vā jaṅghapesanikena vā aññataraññatarena ^5517

vā buddhapaṭikuṭṭhena micchāājīvena jīvikaṃ kappeti – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘anācāro’’. ^5518

Tattha katamo ācāro? Kāyiko avītikkamo, vācasiko avītikkamo, kāyikavācasiko avītikkamo – ayaṃ ^5519

vuccati ‘‘ācāro’’. Sabbopi sīlasaṃvaro ācāro. Idhekacco na veḷudānena na pattadānena na pupphadānena ^5520

na phaladānena na sinānadānena na dantakaṭṭhadānena na cāṭukamyatāya na muggasūpyatāya na ^5521

pāribhaṭayatāya na jaṅghapesanikena na aññataraññatarena buddhapaṭikuṭṭhena micchāājīvena jīvikaṃ ^5522

kappeti – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ācāro’’. ^5523

514. ‘‘Gocaro’’ti atthi gocaro, atthi agocaro. ^5524

Tattha katamo agocaro? Idhekacco vesiyāgocaro vā hoti vidhavāgocaro vā thullakumārigocaro vā ^5525

paṇḍakagocaro vā bhikkhunigocaro vā pānāgāragocaro vā, saṃsaṭṭho viharati rājūhi rājamahāmattehi ^5526

titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi ananulomikena saṃsaggena; yāni vā pana tāni kulāni assaddhāni appasannāni ^5527

anopānabhūtāni akkosakaparibhāsakāni anatthakāmāni ahitakāmāni aphāsukakāmāni ^5528

ayogakkhemakāmāni bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, tathārūpāni kulāni sevati ^5529

bhajati payirupāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘agocaro’’. ^5530

Tattha katamo gocaro? Idhekacco na vesiyāgocaro hoti na vidhavāgocaro na thullakumārigocaro na ^5531

paṇḍakagocaro na bhikkhunigocaro na pānāgāragocaro, asaṃsaṭṭho viharati rājūhi rājamahāmattehi ^5532

titthiyehi titthiyasāvakehi ananulomikena saṃsaggena; yāni vā pana tāni kulāni saddhāni pasannāni ^5533

opānabhūtāni kāsāvapajjotāni isivātapaṭivātāni atthakāmāni hitakāmāni phāsukakāmāni ^5534

yogakkhemakāmāni bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, tathārūpāni kulāni sevati ^5535

bhajati payirupāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘gocaro’’. Iti iminā ca ācārena iminā ca gocarena upeto hoti… ^5536

pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘ācāragocarasampanno’’ti. ^5537

515. ‘‘Aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī’’ti tattha katame aṇumattā vajjā? Yāni tāni vajjāni ^5538

appamattakāni oramattakāni lahusāni lahusammatāni saṃyamakaraṇīyāni saṃvarakaraṇīyāni ^5539

cittuppādakaraṇīyāni manasikārapaṭibaddhāni – ime vuccanti ‘‘aṇumattā vajjā’’. Iti imesu aṇumattesu ^5540

vajjesu vajjadassāvī ca hoti bhayadassāvī ca ādīnavadassāvī ca nissaraṇadassāvī ca. Tena vuccati ^5541

‘‘aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī’’ti. ^5542

516. ‘‘Samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesū’’ti tattha katamā sikkhā? Catasso sikkhā – bhikkhūnaṃ ^5543

bhikkhusikkhā, bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhunisikkhā, upāsakānaṃ upāsakasikkhā, upāsikānaṃ ^5544

upāsikasikkhā. Imā vuccanti ‘‘sikkhāyo’’. Iti imāsu sikkhāsu sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asesaṃ ^5545

nissesaṃ samādāya vattati. Tena vuccati ‘‘samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesū’’ti. ^5546

517. ‘‘Indriyesu guttadvāro’’ti atthi indriyesu guttadvāratā, atthi aguttadvāratā. ^5547

Tattha katamā indriyesu aguttadvāratā? Idhekacco cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hoti ^5548

anubyañjanaggāhī. Yatvādhikaraṇamenaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādomanassā ^5549

pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipajjati, na rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, ^5550

cakkhundriye na saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. Sotena saddaṃ sutvā…pe… ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pe… ^5551

jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pe… kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pe… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya ^5552

nimittaggāhī hoti anubyañjanaggāhī. Yatvādhikaraṇamenaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ ^5553

abhijjhādomanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipajjati, na rakkhati ^5554

manindriyaṃ, manindriye na saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. Yā imesaṃ channaṃ indriyānaṃ agutti agopanā ^5555

anārakkho asaṃvaro – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘indriyesu aguttadvāratā’’. ^5556

Tattha katamā indriyesu guttadvāratā? Idhekacco cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hoti ^5557

nānubyañjanaggāhī. Yatvādhikaraṇamenaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādomanassā ^5558

pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipajjati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, ^5559

cakkhundriye saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. Sotena saddaṃ sutvā…pe… ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pe… ^5560

jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pe… kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pe… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya na ^5561

nimittaggāhī hoti nānubyañjanaggāhī. Ñatvādhikaraṇamenaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ ^5562

abhijjhādomanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipajjati, rakkhati ^5563

manindriyaṃ, manindriye saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. Yā imesaṃ channaṃ indriyānaṃ gutti gopanā ārakkho ^5564

saṃvaro – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘indriyesu guttadvāratā’’. Imāya indriyesu guttadvāratāya upeto hoti ^5565

samupeto…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘indriyesu guttadvāro’’ti. ^5566

518. ‘‘Bhojane mattaññū’’ti atthi bhojane mattaññutā, atthi bhojane amattaññutā. ^5567

Tattha katamā bhojane amattaññutā? Idhekacco appaṭisaṅkhā ayoniso āhāraṃ āhāreti davāya ^5568

madāya maṇḍanāya vibhūsanāya. Yā tattha asantuṭṭhitā amattaññutā appaṭisaṅkhā bhojane – ayaṃ ^5569

vuccati ‘‘bhojane amattaññutā’’ti. ^5570

Tattha katamā bhojane mattaññutā? Idhekacco paṭisaṅkhā yoniso āhāraṃ āhāreti – ‘‘neva davāya ^5571

na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadeva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā ^5572

brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañca vedanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi navañca vedanaṃ na uppādessāmi yātrā ^5573

ca me bhavissati anavajjatā ca phāsu vihāro cā’’ti. Yā tattha santuṭṭhitā mattaññutā paṭisaṅkhā bhojane – ^5574

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘bhojane mattaññutā’’. Imāya bhojane mattaññutāya upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena ^5575

vuccati ‘‘bhojane mattaññū’’ti. ^5576

519. Kathañca bhikkhu pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyogamanuyutto hoti? Idha bhikkhu ^5577

divasaṃ caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇīyehi dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti, rattiyā paṭhamayāmaṃ ^5578

caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇīyehi dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti, rattiyā majjhimayāmaṃ dakkhiṇena ^5579

passena sīhaseyyaṃ kappeti pāde pādaṃ [pādena pādaṃ (syā.)] accādhāya sato sampajāno ^5580

uṭṭhānasaññaṃ manasikaritvā, rattiyā pacchimayāmaṃ paccuṭṭhāya caṅkamena nisajjāya āvaraṇīyehi ^5581

dhammehi cittaṃ parisodheti. Evaṃ bhikkhu pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyogamanuyutto. ^5582

520. ‘‘Sātacca’’nti. Yo cetasiko vīriyārambho…pe… sammāvāyāmo. ^5583

521. ‘‘Nepakka’’nti. Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi. ^5584

522. ‘‘Bodhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyogamanuyutto’’ti. Tattha katame ^5585

bodhipakkhikā dhammā? Satta bojjhaṅgā – satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, ^5586

vīriyasambojjhaṅgo, pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, ^5587

upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo. Ime vuccanti ‘‘bodhipakkhikā dhammā’’. Iti te bodhipakkhike dhamme āsevati ^5588

bhāveti bahulīkaroti. Tena vuccati ‘‘bodhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyogamanuyutto’’ti. ^5589

523. Kathañca bhikkhu abhikkante paṭikkante sampajānakārī hoti, ālokite vilokite sampajānakārī ^5590

hoti, samiñjite pasārite sampajānakārī hoti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇe sampajānakārī hoti, asite pīte ^5591

khāyite sāyite sampajānakārī hoti, uccārapassāvakamme sampajānakārī hoti; gate ṭhite nisinne sutte ^5592

jāgarite bhāsite tuṇhībhāve sampajānakārī hoti? Idha bhikkhu sato sampajāno abhikkamati, sato ^5593

sampajāno paṭikkamati, sato sampajāno āloketi, sato sampajāno viloketi, sato sampajāno samiñjeti, sato ^5594

sampajāno pasāreti, sato sampajānakārī hoti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇe sato sampajānakārī hoti, asite ^5595

pīte khāyite sāyite sato sampajānakārī hoti, uccārapassāvakamme sato sampajānakārī hoti, gate ṭhite ^5596

nisinne sutte jāgarite bhāsite tuṇhībhāve sato sampajānakārī hotīti. ^5597

524. Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammusanatā ^5598

sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. ^5599

525. ‘‘Sampajāno’’ti tattha katamaṃ sampajaññaṃ? Yā paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo ^5600

dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā ^5601

cintā upaparikkhā bhūrīmedhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ ^5602

paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho ^5603

dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sampajaññaṃ’’. Iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajaññena ^5604

upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Evaṃ bhikkhu sato sampajāno abhikkamati, sato sampajāno ^5605

paṭikkamati, sato sampajāno āloketi, sato sampajāno viloketi, sato sampajāno samiñjeti, sato sampajāno ^5606

pasāreti, sato sampajānakārī hoti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇe sato sampajānakārī hoti, asite pīte khāyite ^5607

sāyite sato sampajānakārī hoti, uccārapassāvakamme sato sampajānakārī hoti, gate ṭhite nisinne sutte ^5608

jāgarite bhāsite tuṇhībhāve sampajānakārī hoti. ^5609

526. ‘‘Vivitta’’nti santike cepi senāsanaṃ hoti, tañca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhehi pabbajitehi. Tena taṃ ^5610

vivittaṃ. Dūre cepi senāsanaṃ hoti, tañca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhehi pabbajitehi. Tena taṃ vivittaṃ. ^5611

527. ‘‘Senāsana’’nti mañcopi senāsanaṃ, pīṭhampi senāsanaṃ, bhisipi senāsanaṃ, bibbohanampi ^5612

[bimbohanampi (sī. syā.)] senāsanaṃ, vihāropi senāsanaṃ, aḍḍhayogopi senāsanaṃ, pāsādopi ^5613

senāsanaṃ, aṭṭopi senāsanaṃ, māḷopi senāsanaṃ, leṇampi senāsanaṃ, guhāpi senāsanaṃ, ^5614

rukkhamūlampi senāsanaṃ, veḷugumbopi senāsanaṃ. Yattha vā pana bhikkhū paṭikkamanti ^5615

sabbametaṃ senāsanaṃ. ^5616

528. ‘‘Vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajatī’’ti imaṃ vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajati sambhajati sevati nisevati ^5617

saṃsevati. Tena vuccati ‘‘vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajatī’’ti. ^5618

529. ‘‘Arañña’’nti nikkhamitvā bahi indakhīlā sabbametaṃ araññaṃ. ^5619

530. ‘‘Rukkhamūla’’nti rukkhamūlaṃyeva rukkhamūlaṃ. Pabbatoyeva pabbato. Kandarāyeva ^5620

kandarā. Giriguhāyeva giriguhā. Susānaṃyeva susānaṃ. Abbhokāsoyeva abbhokāso. Palālapuñjoyeva ^5621

palālapuñjo. ^5622

531. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti dūrānametaṃ senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti ^5623

vanasaṇḍānametaṃ senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti bhīsanakānametaṃ ^5624

[bhiṃsanakānametaṃ (sī. syā.)] senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti salomahaṃsānametaṃ ^5625

senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti pariyantānametaṃ senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ^5626

‘‘Vanapattha’’nti na manussūpacārānametaṃ senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ‘‘Vanapattha’’nti ^5627

durabhisambhavānametaṃ senāsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. ^5628

532. ‘‘Appasadda’’nti santike cepi senāsanaṃ hoti, tañca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhehi pabbajitehi. Tena ^5629

taṃ appasaddaṃ. Dūre cepi senāsanaṃ hoti, tañca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhehi pabbajitehi. Tena taṃ ^5630

appasaddaṃ. ^5631

533. ‘‘Appanigghosa’’nti yadeva taṃ appasaddaṃ tadeva taṃ appanigghosaṃ. Yadeva taṃ ^5632

appanigghosaṃ tadeva taṃ vijanavātaṃ. Yadeva taṃ vijanavātaṃ tadeva taṃ manussarāhasseyyakaṃ. ^5633

Yadeva taṃ manussarāhasseyyakaṃ tadeva taṃ paṭisallānasāruppaṃ. ^5634

534. ‘‘Araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā’’ti araññagato vā hoti rukkhamūlagato ^5635

vā suññāgāragato vā. ^5636

535. ‘‘Nisīdati pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā’’ti nisinno hoti pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā. ^5637

536. ‘‘Ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāyā’’ti ujuko hoti kāyo ṭhito paṇihito. ^5638

537. ‘‘Parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā’’ti tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati paṭissati…pe… ^5639

sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. Ayaṃ sati upaṭṭhitā hoti supaṭṭhitā nāsikagge vā mukhanimitte vā. ^5640

Tena vuccati ‘‘parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā’’ti. ^5641

538. ‘‘Abhijjhaṃ loke pahāyā’’ti tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa sārāgo – ^5642

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abhijjhā’’. ^5643

Tattha katamo loko? Pañcupādānakkhandhā loko – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘loko’’. Ayaṃ abhijjhā imamhi ^5644

loke santā hoti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. ^5645

Tena vuccati ‘‘abhijjhaṃ loke pahāyā’’ti. ^5646

539. ‘‘Vigatābhijjhena cetasā’’ti tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^5647

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ vigatābhijjhaṃ hoti. Tena vuccati ^5648

‘‘vigatābhijjhena cetasā’’ti. ^5649

540. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5650

541. ‘‘Abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti tattha katamā abhijjhā? Yo rāgo sārāgo…pe… cittassa ^5651

sārāgo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘abhijjhā’’. ^5652

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^5653

‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imāya abhijjhāya sodheti visodheti parisodheti moceti vimoceti parimoceti. Tena ^5654

vuccati ‘‘abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. ^5655

542. ‘‘Byāpādapadosaṃ pahāyā’’ti atthi byāpādo, atthi padoso. ^5656

Tattha katamo byāpādo? Yo cittassa āghāto paṭighāto paṭighaṃ paṭivirodho kopo pakopo ^5657

sampakopo doso padoso sampadoso cittassa byāpatti manopadoso kodho kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ doso ^5658

dussanā dussitattaṃ [dūsanā dūsitattaṃ (syā.)] byāpatti byāpajjanā byāpajjitattaṃ virodho paṭivirodho ^5659

caṇḍikkaṃ asuropo anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘byāpādo’’. ^5660

Tattha katamo padoso? Yo byāpādo so padoso, yo padoso so byāpādo. Iti ayañca byāpādo ayañca ^5661

padoso santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā ^5662

byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘byāpādapadosaṃ pahāyā’’ti. ^5663

543. ‘‘Abyāpannacitto’’ti tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^5664

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ abyāpannaṃ hoti. Tena vuccati ^5665

‘‘abyāpannacitto’’ti. ^5666

544. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5667

545. ‘‘Byāpādapadosā cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. Atthi byāpādo atthi padoso. ^5668

Tattha katamo byāpādo? Yo cittassa āghāto…pe… caṇḍikkaṃ asuropo anattamanatā cittassa – ^5669

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘byāpādo’’. ^5670

Tattha katamo padoso? Yo byāpādo so padoso, yo padoso so byāpādo. ^5671

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^5672

‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā byāpādapadosā sodheti visodheti parisodheti moceti vimoceti ^5673

parimoceti. Tena vuccati ‘‘byāpādapadosā cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. ^5674

546. ‘‘Thinamiddhaṃ pahāyā’’ti atthi thinaṃ [thīnaṃ (sī. syā.)], atthi middhaṃ. ^5675

Tattha katamaṃ thinaṃ? Yā cittassa akalyatā akammaññatā olīyanā sallīyanā līnaṃ līyanā ^5676

līyitattaṃ thinaṃ thiyanā thiyitattaṃ [thīyanā thīyitattaṃ (sī. syā.)] cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘thinaṃ’’. ^5677

Tattha katamaṃ middhaṃ? Yā kāyassa akalyatā akammaññatā onāho pariyonāho antosamorodho ^5678

middhaṃ suppaṃ pacalāyikā suppaṃ suppanā suppitattaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘middhaṃ’’. Iti idañca ^5679

thinaṃ idañca middhaṃ santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā ^5680

visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘thinamiddhaṃ pahāyā’’ti. ^5681

547. ‘‘Vigatathinamiddho’’ti. Tassa thinamiddhassa cattattā vantattā muttattā pahīnattā ^5682

paṭinissaṭṭhattā pahīnapaṭinissaṭṭhattā. Tena vuccati ‘‘vigatathinamiddho’’ti. ^5683

548. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5684

549. ‘‘Ālokasaññī’’ti. Tattha katamā saññā? Yā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^5685

‘‘saññā’’. Ayaṃ saññā ālokā hoti vivaṭā parisuddhā pariyodātā. Tena vuccati ‘‘ālokasaññī’’ti. ^5686

550. ‘‘Sato sampajāno’’ti. Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati …pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ^5687

‘‘sati’’. ^5688

Tattha katamaṃ sampajaññaṃ? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ^5689

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sampajaññaṃ’’. Iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajaññena upeto hoti…pe… ^5690

samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘sato sampajāno’’ti. ^5691

551. ‘‘Thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. Atthi thinaṃ, atthi middhaṃ. ^5692

Tattha katamaṃ thinaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘thinaṃ’’. ^5693

Tattha katamaṃ middhaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘middhaṃ’’. ^5694

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā thinamiddhā sodheti ^5695

visodheti parisodheti moceti vimoceti parimoceti. Tena vuccati ‘‘thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. ^5696

552. ‘‘Uddhaccakukkuccaṃ pahāyā’’ti atthi uddhaccaṃ, atthi kukkuccaṃ. ^5697

Tattha katamaṃ uddhaccaṃ? Yaṃ cittassa uddhaccaṃ avūpasamo cetaso vikkhepo bhantattaṃ ^5698

cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘uddhaccaṃ’’. ^5699

Tattha katamaṃ kukkuccaṃ? Akappiye kappiyasaññitā, kappiye akappiyasaññitā, avajje ^5700

vajjasaññitā, vajje avajjasaññitā, yaṃ evarūpaṃ kukkuccaṃ kukkuccāyanā kukkuccāyitattaṃ cetaso ^5701

vippaṭisāro manovilekhā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘kukkuccaṃ’’. Iti idañca uddhaccaṃ idañca kukkuccaṃ santā ^5702

honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena ^5703

vuccati ‘‘uddhaccakukkuccaṃ pahāyā’’ti. ^5704

553. ‘‘Anuddhato’’ti tassa uddhaccakukkuccassa cattattā vantattā muttattā pahīnattā paṭinissaṭṭhattā ^5705

pahīnapaṭinissaṭṭhattā. Tena vuccati ‘‘anuddhato’’ti. ^5706

554. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti …pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5707

555. ‘‘Vūpasantacitto’’ti. Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… ^5708

tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ santaṃ hoti samitaṃ ^5709

vūpasantaṃ. Tena vuccati ‘‘ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacitto’’ti. ^5710

556. ‘‘Uddhaccakukkuccā cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti atthi uddhaccaṃ, atthi kukkuccaṃ. ^5711

Tattha katamaṃ uddhaccaṃ? Yaṃ cittassa uddhaccaṃ avūpasamo cetaso vikkhepo bhantattaṃ ^5712

cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘uddhaccaṃ’’. ^5713

Tattha katamaṃ kukkuccaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘kukkuccaṃ’’. ^5714

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā uddhaccakukkuccā ^5715

sodheti visodheti parisodheti moceti vimoceti parimoceti. Tena vuccati ‘‘uddhaccakukkuccā cittaṃ ^5716

parisodhetī’’ti. ^5717

557. ‘‘Vicikicchaṃ pahāyā’’ti, tattha katamā vicikicchā? Yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ ^5718

vimati vicikicchā dveḷhakaṃ dvidhāpatho saṃsayo anekaṃsaggāho āsappanā parisappanā ^5719

apariyogāhaṇā chambhitattaṃ cittassa manovilekho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vicikicchā’’. Ayaṃ vicikicchā ^5720

santā hoti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena ^5721

vuccati ‘‘vicikicchaṃ pahāyā’’ti. ^5722

558. ‘‘Tiṇṇavicikiccho’’ti, imaṃ vicikicchaṃ tiṇṇo hoti uttiṇṇo nittiṇṇo pāraṅgato pāramanuppatto. ^5723

Tena vuccati ‘‘tiṇṇavicikiccho’’ti. ^5724

559. ‘‘Akathaṃkathī kusalesu dhammesū’’ti imāya vicikicchāya kusalesu dhammesu na kaṅkhati ^5725

na vicikicchati akathaṃkathī hoti nikkathaṃkathī vikathaṃkatho. Tena vuccati ‘‘akathaṃkathī kusalesu ^5726

dhammesū’’ti. ^5727

560. ‘‘Vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti, tattha katamā vicikicchā? Yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā ^5728

kaṅkhāyitattaṃ chambhitattaṃ cittassa manovilekho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vicikicchā’’. ^5729

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^5730

‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imāya vicikicchāya sodheti visodheti parisodheti moceti vimoceti parimoceti. ^5731

Tena vuccati ‘‘vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisodhetī’’ti. ^5732

561. ‘‘Ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāyā’’ti ime pañca nīvaraṇā santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā ^5733

abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāyā’’ti. ^5734

562. ‘‘Cetaso upakkilese’’ti ime pañca nīvaraṇā cittassa upakkilesā. ^5735

563. ‘‘Paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe’’ti imehi pañcahi nīvaraṇehi anuppannā ceva paññā na uppajjati ^5736

uppannā ca paññā nirujjhati. Tena vuccati ‘‘paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe’’ti. ^5737

564. ‘‘Vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehī’’ti tattha katame kāmā? Chando kāmo, rāgo ^5738

kāmo, chandarāgo kāmo, saṅkappo kāmo, rāgo kāmo, saṅkapparāgo kāmo – ime vuccanti ‘‘kāmā’’. ^5739

Tattha katame akusalā dhammā? Kāmacchando, byāpādo, thinaṃ, middhaṃ, uddhaccaṃ, ^5740

kukkuccaṃ, vicikicchā – ime vuccanti ‘‘akusalā dhammā’’. Iti imehi ca kāmehi imehi ca akusalehi ^5741

dhammehi vivitto hoti. Tena vuccati ‘‘vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehī’’ti. ^5742

565. ‘‘Savitakkaṃ savicāra’’nti atthi vitakko, atthi vicāro. ^5743

Tattha katamo vitakko? Yo takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā ^5744

sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vitakko’’. ^5745

Tattha katamo vicāro? Yo cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhanatā anupekkhanatā – ^5746

ayaṃ vuccati vicāro. Iti iminā ca vitakkena iminā ca vicārena upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena ^5747

vuccati ‘‘savitakkaṃ savicāra’’nti. ^5748

566. ‘‘Vivekaja’’nti vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā – te imasmiṃ viveke jātā honti ^5749

sañjātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā. Tena vuccati ‘‘vivekaja’’nti. ^5750

567. ‘‘Pītisukha’’nti atthi pīti, atthi sukhaṃ. ^5751

Tattha katamā pīti? Yā pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā ^5752

cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pīti’’. ^5753

Tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ ^5754

sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’. Idaṃ sukhaṃ imāya ^5755

pītiyā sahagataṃ hoti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ. Tena vuccati ‘‘pītisukha’’nti. ^5756

568. ‘‘Paṭhama’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā [gaṇanānupubbato (syā.) evamuparipi] paṭhamaṃ. Idaṃ ^5757

paṭhamaṃ samāpajjatīti paṭhamaṃ. ^5758

569. ‘‘Jhāna’’nti vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^5759

570. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo paṭhamassa jhānassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5760

upasampadā. ^5761

571. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti …pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5762

572. ‘‘Vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā’’ti, atthi vitakko, atthi vicāro. ^5763

Tattha katamo vitakko? Yo takko vitakko…pe… sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vitakko’’. ^5764

Tattha katamo vicāro? Yo cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhanatā anupekkhanatā – ^5765

ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vicāro’’. Iti ayañca vitakko ayañca vicāro santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā ^5766

abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘vitakkavicārānaṃ ^5767

vūpasamā’’ti. ^5768

573. ‘‘Ajjhatta’’nti yaṃ ajjhattaṃ paccattaṃ. ^5769

574. ‘‘Sampasādana’’nti yā saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo. ^5770

575. ‘‘Cetaso ekodibhāva’’nti yā cittassa ṭhiti…pe… sammāsamādhi. ^5771

576. ‘‘Avitakkaṃ avicāra’’nti atthi vitakko, atthi vicāro. ^5772

Tattha katamo vitakko? Yo takko vitakko…pe… sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vitakko’’. ^5773

Tattha katamo vicāro? Yo cāro anucāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhanatā ^5774

anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘vicāro’’. Iti ayañca vitakko ayañca vicāro santā hoti samitā vūpasantā ^5775

atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘avitakkaṃ ^5776

avicāra’’nti. ^5777

577. ‘‘Samādhija’’nti sampasādo pītisukhaṃ – te imasmiṃ samādhimhi jātā honti sañjātā nibbattā ^5778

abhinibbattā pātubhūtā. Tena vuccati ‘‘samādhija’’nti. ^5779

578. ‘‘Pītisukha’’nti atthi pīti, atthi sukhaṃ. ^5780

Tattha katamā pīti…pe… ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pīti’’. ^5781

Tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ…pe… idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’. Idaṃ sukhaṃ imāya pītiyā sahagataṃ ^5782

hoti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ. Tena vuccati ‘‘pītisukha’’nti. ^5783

579. ‘‘Dutiya’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā dutiyaṃ. Idaṃ dutiyaṃ samāpajjatīti dutiyaṃ. ^5784

580. ‘‘Jhāna’’nti sampasādo, pītisukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^5785

581. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo dutiyassa jhānassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5786

upasampadā. ^5787

582. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti …pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5788

583. ‘‘Pītiyā ca virāgā’’ti tattha katamā pīti? Yā pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso ^5789

vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘pīti’’. Ayaṃ pīti santā hoti samitā vūpasantā ^5790

atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘pītiyā ca virāgā’’ti. ^5791

584. ‘‘Upekkhako’’ti tattha katamā upekkhā? Yā upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā ^5792

cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhā’’. Imāya upekkhāya upeto hoti…pe… samannāgato. Tena vuccati ^5793

‘‘upekkhako’’ti. ^5794

585. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti …pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5795

586. ‘‘Sato ca sampajāno’’ti tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ^5796

‘‘sati’’. ^5797

Tattha katamaṃ sampajaññaṃ? Yā paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ^5798

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sampajaññaṃ’’. Iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajaññena upeto hoti…pe… ^5799

samannāgato. Tena vuccati ‘‘sato ca sampajāno’’ti. ^5800

587. ‘‘Sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedetī’’ti tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ ^5801

cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ^5802

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’. ^5803

Tattha katamo kāyo? Saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ayaṃ vuccati ^5804

‘‘kāyo’’. Idaṃ sukhaṃ iminā kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti. Tena vuccati ‘‘sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedetī’’ti. ^5805

588. ‘‘Yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhantī’’ti tattha katame ariyā? Ariyā vuccanti buddhā ca buddhasāvakā ^5806

ca. Te imaṃ ācikkhanti desenti paññapenti [paññāpenti (sī. syā.)] paṭṭhapenti vivaranti vibhajanti ^5807

uttāniṃ karonti pakāsenti. Tena vuccati ‘‘yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhantī’’ti. ^5808

589. ‘‘Upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tattha katamā upekkhā? Yā upekkhā upekkhanā ^5809

ajjhupekkhanā majjhattatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhā’’. ^5810

Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. ^5811

Tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ ^5812

sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’. Iti imāya ca ^5813

upekkhāya imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sukhena samannāgato iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati ^5814

viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’’ti. ^5815

590. ‘‘Tatiya’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā tatiyaṃ. Idaṃ tatiyaṃ samāpajjatīti tatiyaṃ. ^5816

591. ‘‘Jhāna’’nti upekkhā, sati, sampajaññaṃ, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^5817

592. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo tatiyassa jhānassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5818

upasampadā. ^5819

593. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5820

594. ‘‘Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā’’ti, atthi sukhaṃ, atthi dukkhaṃ. ^5821

Tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ ^5822

vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’. ^5823

Tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? Yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ ^5824

dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘dukkhaṃ’’. Iti idañca ^5825

sukhaṃ idañca dukkhaṃ santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā ^5826

visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā’’ti. ^5827

595. ‘‘Pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti atthi somanassaṃ, atthi domanassaṃ. ^5828

Tattha katamaṃ somanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ ^5829

sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘somanassaṃ’’. ^5830

Tattha katamaṃ domanassaṃ? Yaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^5831

asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘domanassaṃ’’. Iti ^5832

idañca somanassaṃ idañca domanassaṃ pubbeva santā honti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā ^5833

abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘pubbeva ^5834

somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti. ^5835

596. ‘‘Adukkhamasukha’’nti yaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ ^5836

adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā. Tena vuccati ^5837

‘‘adukkhamasukha’’nti. ^5838

597. ‘‘Upekkhāsatipārisuddhi’’nti, tattha katamā upekkhā? Yā upekkhā upekkhanā ajjhupekkhanā ^5839

majjhattatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘upekkhā’’. ^5840

Tattha katamā sati? Yā sati anussati…pe… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘sati’’. Ayaṃ sati imāya ^5841

upekkhāya vivaṭā hoti parisuddhā pariyodātā. Tena vuccati ‘‘upekkhāsatipārisuddhi’’nti. ^5842

598. ‘‘Catuttha’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā catutthaṃ, idaṃ catutthaṃ samāpajjatīti catutthaṃ. ^5843

599. ‘‘Jhāna’’nti upekkhā, sati, cittassekaggatā. ^5844

600. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo catutthassa jhānassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5845

upasampadā. ^5846

601. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5847

602. ‘‘Sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā’’ti tattha katamā rūpasaññā? Rūpāvacarasamāpattiṃ ^5848

samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – imā ^5849

vuccanti ‘‘rūpasaññāyo’’. Imā rūpasaññāyo atikkanto hoti vītikkanto samatikkanto. Tena vuccati ^5850

‘‘sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā’’ti. ^5851

603. ‘‘Paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti tattha katamā paṭighasaññā? Rūpasaññā saddasaññā…pe… ^5852

phoṭṭhabbasaññā – imā vuccanti paṭighasaññāyo. Imā paṭighasaññāyo santā honti samitā vūpasantā ^5853

atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sositā visositā byantīkatā. Tena vuccati ‘‘paṭighasaññānaṃ ^5854

atthaṅgamā’’ti. ^5855

604. ‘‘Nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā’’ti tattha katamā nānattasaññā? Asamāpannassa manodhātu ^5856

samaṅgissa vā manoviññāṇadhātu samaṅgissa vā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – imā vuccanti ^5857

‘‘nānattasaññāyo’’. Imā nānattasaññāyo na manasi karoti. Tena vuccati ‘‘nānattasaññānaṃ ^5858

amanasikārā’’ti. ^5859

605. ‘‘Ananto ākāso’’ti, tattha katamo ākāso? Yo ākāso ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivaro ^5860

vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtehi – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘ākāso’’. Tasmiṃ ākāse cittaṃ ṭhapeti ^5861

saṇṭhapeti anantaṃ pharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘ananto ākāso’’ti. ^5862

606. ‘‘Ākāsānañcāyatana’’nti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā ^5863

diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā. ^5864

607. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo ākāsānañcāyatanassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5865

upasampadā. ^5866

608. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5867

609. ‘‘Sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti imaṃ ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ atikkanto hoti ^5868

vītikkanto samatikkanto. Tena vuccati ‘‘sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti. ^5869

610. ‘‘Anantaṃ viññāṇa’’nti taṃyeva ākāsaṃ viññāṇena phuṭṭhaṃ manasi karoti anantaṃ pharati. ^5870

Tena vuccati ‘‘anantaṃ viññāṇa’’nti. ^5871

611. ‘‘Viññāṇañcāyatana’’nti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā ^5872

diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā. ^5873

612. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo viññāṇañcāyatanassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5874

upasampadā. ^5875

613. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti…pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5876

614. ‘‘Sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti imaṃ viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ atikkanto hoti ^5877

vītikkanto samatikkanto. Tena vuccati ‘‘sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti. ^5878

615. ‘‘Natthi kiñcī’’ti taṃyeva viññāṇaṃ bhāveti vibhāveti antarabhāveti, ‘‘natthi kiñcī’’ti passati. ^5879

Tena vuccati ‘‘natthi kiñcī’’ti. ^5880

616. ‘‘Ākiñcaññāyatana’’nti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā ^5881

diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā. ^5882

617. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo ākiñcaññāyatanassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā ^5883

upasampadā. ^5884

618. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti …pe… tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5885

619. ‘‘Sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti imaṃ ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ atikkanto hoti ^5886

vītikkanto samatikkanto. Tena vuccati ‘‘sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkammā’’ti. ^5887

‘‘Nevasaññīnāsaññī’’ti taṃyeva ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ santato manasi karoti ^5888

saṅkhārāvasesasamāpattiṃ bhāveti. Tena vuccati ‘‘nevasaññīnāsaññī’’ti [ayaṃ pāṭho mātikāyaṃ natthi, ^5889

niddese pana sabbapotthakesu dissati]. ^5890

620. ‘‘Nevasaññānāsaññāyatana’’nti nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā ^5891

diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā. ^5892

621. ‘‘Upasampajjā’’ti yo nevasaññānāsaññāyatanassa lābho paṭilābho patti sampatti phusanā ^5893

sacchikiriyā upasampadā. ^5894

622. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^5895

Suttantabhājanīyaṃ. ^5896

2. Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ ^5897

1. Rūpāvacarakusalaṃ ^5898

623. Cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ. ^5899

624. Tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ ^5900

bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ ^5901

samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ^5902

‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5903

Tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ^5904

vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ ^5905

samaye tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. ^5906

Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5907

Tattha katamaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ^5908

pītiyā ca virāgā…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5909

duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5910

jhānasampayuttā. ^5911

Tattha katamaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ^5912

sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5913

duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā ^5914

dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5915

Catukkaṃ. ^5916

625. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… ^5917

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – ^5918

vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5919

jhānasampayuttā. ^5920

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi ^5921

dhammehi avitakkaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5922

pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ ^5923

vuccati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5924

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… ^5925

tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – pīti, ^5926

sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5927

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti pītiyā ca virāgā…pe… catutthaṃ ^5928

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – sukhaṃ, ^5929

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5930

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… ^5931

pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – ^5932

upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5933

Pañcakaṃ. ^5934

2. Arūpāvacarakusalaṃ ^5935

626. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ ^5936

samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^5937

upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ^5938

‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5939

3. Lokuttarakusalaṃ ^5940

627. Cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ. ^5941

628. Tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5942

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^5943

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5944

pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ ^5945

jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5946

Tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5947

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitakkavicārānaṃ ^5948

vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ ^5949

samaye tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. ^5950

Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5951

Tattha katamaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5952

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā pītiyā ca virāgā…pe… ^5953

tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ ^5954

jhānaṃ hoti – sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5955

jhānasampayuttā. ^5956

Tattha katamaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^5957

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā sukhassa ca pahānā … ^5958

pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye ^5959

duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā ^5960

dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5961

Catukkaṃ. ^5962

629. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ ^5963

diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ ^5964

upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti ^5965

– vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^5966

jhānasampayuttā. ^5967

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ ^5968

pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi avitakkaṃ ^5969

vicāramattaṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^5970

dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti vicāro pīti sukhaṃ cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ ^5971

vuccati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5972

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ ^5973

pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja ^5974

viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – pīti, sukhaṃ, ^5975

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5976

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ ^5977

pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā pītiyā ca virāgā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5978

dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^5979

Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5980

Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ ^5981

pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja ^5982

viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, ^5983

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5984

Pañcakaṃ. ^5985

4. Rūpāvacaravipākā ^5986

630. Cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ. ^5987

631. Tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ ^5988

bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ ^5989

samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa ^5990

kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati ^5991

pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, ^5992

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^5993

Tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ^5994

vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ ^5995

samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa ^5996

kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… ^5997

tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ ^5998

upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, ^5999

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā…pe…. ^6000

5. Arūpāvacaravipākā ^6001

632. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ ^6002

samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^6003

upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva ^6004

arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ ^6005

samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^6006

upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ^6007

‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^6008

6. Lokuttaravipākā ^6009

633. Cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ. ^6010

634. Tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^6011

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi… ^6012

pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso ^6013

hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā ^6014

bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ ^6015

dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, ^6016

cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^6017

Tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^6018

niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitakkavicārānaṃ ^6019

vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ ^6020

samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti. Ime dhammā kusalā. Tasseva lokuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa ^6021

katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ …pe… tatiyaṃ ^6022

jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja ^6023

viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – ^6024

upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^6025

7. Rūpārūpāvacarakiriyā ^6026

635. Cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ. ^6027

636. Tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ ^6028

bhāveti kiriyaṃ neva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vivicceva ^6029

kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ ^6030

jhānaṃ hoti – vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. ^6031

Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttā. ^6032

Tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti ^6033

kiriyaṃ neva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vitakkavicārānaṃ ^6034

vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ ^6035

jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ ^6036

jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā ^6037

jhānasampayuttā. ^6038

637. Idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samaye arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti kiriyaṃ neva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ ^6039

na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ^6040

nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja ^6041

viharati, tasmiṃ samaye duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti – upekkhā, cittassekaggatā. Idaṃ vuccati ‘‘catutthaṃ ^6042

jhānaṃ’’. Avasesā dhammā jhānasampayuttāti. ^6043

Abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ. ^6044

3. Pañhāpucchakaṃ ^6045

638. Cattāri jhānāni – idha bhikkhu vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ ^6046

savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati…pe… vitakkavicārānaṃ ^6047

vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ ^6048

dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati…pe… pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno ^6049

sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ ^6050

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati…pe… sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva ^6051

somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ ^6052

upasampajja viharati. ^6053

639. Catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā? ^6054

1. Tikaṃ ^6055

640. Siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā. Tīṇi jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ sukhaṃ vedanaṃ ṭhapetvā sukhāya ^6056

vedanāya sampayuttā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – etthuppannaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedanaṃ ṭhapetvā ^6057

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ. Siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā ^6058

nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā. Siyā upādinnupādāniyā, siyā anupādinnupādāniyā, siyā ^6059

anupādinnaanupādāniyā. Siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā. Paṭhamaṃ ^6060

jhānaṃ – etthuppanne vitakkavicāre ṭhapetvā savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avitakkaavicārā. Dve ^6061

jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ pītiṃ ṭhapetvā pītisahagatā, tīṇi jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhapetvā ^6062

sukhasahagatā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – etthuppannaṃ upekkhaṃ ṭhapetvā upekkhāsahagataṃ. Neva ^6063

dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Siyā ācayagāmino, ^6064

siyā apacayagāmino, siyā nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino. Siyā sekkhā, siyā asekkhā, siyā ^6065

nevasekkhanāsekkhā. Siyā mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā. Tīṇi jhānāni na vattabbā parittārammaṇātipi, ^6066

mahaggatārammaṇātipi, siyā appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā appamāṇārammaṇāti; catutthaṃ ^6067

jhānaṃ siyā parittārammaṇaṃ, siyā mahaggatārammaṇaṃ, siyā appamāṇārammaṇaṃ; siyā na vattabbaṃ ^6068

parittārammaṇantipi, mahaggatārammaṇantipi, appamāṇārammaṇantipi. Siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā. Siyā ^6069

sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā. Tīṇi jhānāni na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahetukā, siyā maggādhipatino, ^6070

siyā na vattabbā maggahetukātipi, maggādhipatinotipi; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā maggārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^6071

maggahetukaṃ, siyā maggādhipati, siyā na vattabbaṃ maggārammaṇantipi, maggahetukantipi ^6072

maggādhipatītipi. Siyā uppannā, siyā anuppannā, siyā uppādino. Siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā ^6073

paccuppannā. Tīṇi jhānāni na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, ^6074

paccuppannārammaṇātipi; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā atītārammaṇaṃ, siyā anāgatārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^6075

paccuppannārammaṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ atītārammaṇantipi, anāgatārammaṇantipi, ^6076

paccuppannāramaṇantipi. Siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā. Tīṇi jhānāni ^6077

bahiddhārammaṇā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā ajjhattārammaṇaṃ, siyā bahiddhārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^6078

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ ajjhattārammaṇantipi, bahiddhārammaṇantipi, ^6079

ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇantipi. Anidassanaappaṭighā. ^6080

2. Dukaṃ ^6081

641. Na hetū, sahetukā, hetusampayuttā, na vattabbā ‘‘hetū ceva sahetukā cā’’ti, sahetukā ceva na ^6082

ca hetū, na vattabbā ‘‘hetū ceva hetusampayuttā cā’’ti, hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū, na hetū ^6083

sahetukā. ^6084

Sappaccayā, saṅkhatā, anidassanā, appaṭighā, arūpā, siyā lokiyā, siyā lokuttarā, kenaci viññeyyā, ^6085

kenaci na viññeyyā. ^6086

No āsavā, siyā sāsavā, siyā anāsavā, āsavavippayuttā, na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā ceva sāsavā cā’’ti, siyā ^6087

sāsavā ceva no ca āsavā, siyā na vattabbā ‘‘sāsavā ceva no ca āsavā’’ti. Na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā ceva ^6088

āsavasampayuttā cāti’’pi, āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavātipi. Siyā āsavavippayuttā sāsavā, siyā ^6089

āsavavippayuttā anāsavā. ^6090

No saṃyojanā…pe… no ganthā…pe… no oghā…pe… no yogā…pe… no nīvaraṇā…pe… no ^6091

parāmāsā…pe… sārammaṇā, no cittā, cetasikā, cittasampayuttā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittasamuṭṭhānā, ^6092

cittasahabhuno, cittānuparivattino, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno, ^6093

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino, bāhirā, no upādā, siyā upādinnā, siyā anupādinnā. ^6094

No upādānā…pe… no kilesā…pe… na dassanena pahātabbā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbā, na ^6095

dassanena pahātabbahetukā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā. Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ – etthuppannaṃ ^6096

vitakkaṃ ṭhapetvā savitakkaṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avitakkā. Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ – etthuppannaṃ vicāraṃ ^6097

ṭhapetvā savicāraṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avicārā. Dve jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ pītiṃ ṭhapetvā sappītikā, dve ^6098

jhānāni appītikā. Dve jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ pītiṃ ṭhapetvā pītisahagatā, dve jhānāni na pītisahagatā. ^6099

Tīṇi jhānāni – etthuppannaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhapetvā sukhasahagatā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ na sukhasahagataṃ. ^6100

Catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – etthuppannaṃ upekkhaṃ ṭhapetvā upekkhāsahagataṃ, tīṇi jhānāni ^6101

upekkhāsahagatā, na kāmāvacarā, siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā, tīṇi jhānāni na arūpāvacarā, ^6102

catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā arūpāvacaraṃ, siyā na arūpāvacaraṃ, siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā, siyā ^6103

niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā, siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā, siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā, araṇāti. ^6104

Pañhāpucchakaṃ. ^6105

Jhānavibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^6106

13. Appamaññāvibhaṅgo ^6107

1. Suttantabhājanīyaṃ ^6108

642. Catasso appamaññāyo – idha bhikkhu mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, ^6109

tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ [catutthiṃ (sī.)]. Iti uddhamadho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi ^6110

sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ mettāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena ^6111

abyāpajjena [abyāpajjhena (sī. syā.)] pharitvā viharati. Karuṇāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā ^6112

viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ. Iti uddhamadho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya ^6113

sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ karuṇāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena abyāpajjena ^6114

pharitvā viharati. Muditāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, ^6115

tathā catutthaṃ. Iti uddhamadho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ muditāsahagatena ^6116

cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena abyāpajjena pharitvā viharati. Upekkhāsahagatena ^6117

cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ. Iti uddhamadho ^6118

tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ upekkhāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena ^6119

appamāṇena averena abyāpajjena pharitvā viharati. ^6120

1. Mettā ^6121

643. Kathañca bhikkhu mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati? Seyyathāpi nāma ^6122

ekaṃ puggalaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ disvā mettāyeyya, evameva sabbe satte mettāya pharati. ^6123

Tattha katamā mettā? Yā sattesu metti mettāyanā mettāyitattaṃ mettācetovimutti – ayaṃ vuccati ^6124

‘‘mettā’’. ^6125

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ ^6126

manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ ^6127

cittaṃ imāya mettāya sahagataṃ hoti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ. Tena vuccati ^6128

‘‘mettāsahagatena cetasā’’ti. ^6129

644. ‘‘Ekaṃ disa’’nti puratthimaṃ vā disaṃ pacchimaṃ vā disaṃ uttaraṃ vā disaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ vā ^6130

disaṃ uddhaṃ vā adho vā tiriyaṃ vā vidisaṃ vā. ^6131

645. ‘‘Pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuccitvā. ^6132

646. ‘‘Viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti carati viharati. Tena vuccati ‘‘viharatī’’ti. ^6133

647. ‘‘Tathā dutiya’’nti yatheva ekaṃ disaṃ tathā dutiyaṃ disaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ disaṃ tathā ^6134

catutthaṃ disaṃ tathā uddhaṃ tathā adho tathā tiriyaṃ tathā vidisaṃ. ^6135

648. ‘‘Sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ loka’’nti sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asesaṃ ^6136

nissesaṃ. Pariyādāyavacanametaṃ – ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ loka’’nti. ^6137

649. ‘‘Mettāsahagatena cetasā’’ti tattha katamā mettā? Yā sattesu metti mettāyanā mettāyitattaṃ ^6138

mettācetovimutti – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘mettā’’. ^6139

Tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? Yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ…pe… tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ^6140

‘‘cittaṃ’’. Idaṃ cittaṃ imāya mettāya sahagataṃ hoti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ. Tena vuccati ^6141
