Tattha katamaṃ asampajaññaṃ? Yaṃ aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ …pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ ^8532

– idaṃ vuccati ‘‘asampajaññaṃ’’. ^8533

(Ga) cetaso vikkhepo ^8534

Tattha katamo cetaso vikkhepo? Yaṃ cittassa uddhaccaṃ avūpasamo cetaso vikkhepo bhantattaṃ ^8535

cittassa – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘cetaso vikkhepo’’. ^8536

(35. Ka) ayoniso manasikāro ^8537

936. Tattha katamo ayoniso manasikāro? Anicce ‘‘nicca’’nti ayoniso manasikāro, dukkhe ^8538

‘‘sukha’’nti ayoniso manasikāro, anattani ‘‘attā’’ti ayoniso manasikāro, asubhe ‘‘subha’’nti ayoniso ^8539

manasikāro, saccavippaṭikulena vā cittassa āvaṭṭanā anāvaṭṭanā ābhogo samannāhāro manasikāro – ayaṃ ^8540

vuccati ‘‘ayoniso manasikāro’’. ^8541

(Kha) kummaggasevanā ^8542

Tattha katamā kummaggasevanā? Tattha katamo kummaggo? Micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappo, ^8543

micchāvācā, micchākammanto, micchāājīvo, micchāvāyāmo, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ ^8544

vuccati ‘‘kummaggo’’. Yā imassa kummaggassa sevanā nisevanā saṃsevanā bhajanā sambhajanā bhatti ^8545

sambhatti taṃsampavaṅkatā – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘kummaggasevanā’’. ^8546

(Ga) cetaso ca līnattaṃ ^8547

Tattha katamaṃ cetaso ca līnattaṃ? Yā cittassa akalyatā akammaññatā olīyanā sallīyanā līnaṃ ^8548

līyanā līyitattaṃ thinaṃ thiyanā thiyitattaṃ cittassa – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘cetaso ca līnattaṃ’’. ^8549

Tikaṃ. ^8550

4. Catukkaniddeso ^8551

(1) Cattāro āsavā ^8552

937. Tattha katame cattāro āsavā? Kāmāsavo, bhavāsavo, diṭṭhāsavo, avijjāsavo. ^8553

(Ka) kāmāsavo ^8554

Tattha katamo kāmāsavo? Yo kāmesu kāmacchando kāmarāgo kāmanandī kāmataṇhā kāmasineho ^8555

kāmapariḷāho kāmamucchā kāmajjhosānaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘kāmāsavo’’. ^8556

(Kha) bhavāsavo ^8557

Tattha katamo bhavāsavo? Yo bhavesu bhavacchando…pe… bhavajjhosānaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ^8558

‘‘bhavāsavo’’. ^8559

(Ga) diṭṭhāsavo ^8560

Tattha katamo diṭṭhāsavo? ‘‘Sassato loko’’ti vā, ‘‘asassato loko’’ti vā, ‘‘antavā loko’’ti vā, ^8561

‘‘anantavā loko’’ti vā, ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘hoti ^8562

tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato ^8563

paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā. Yā evarūpā diṭṭhi ^8564

diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… vipariyāsaggāho – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘diṭṭhāsavo’’. Sabbāpi micchādiṭṭhi diṭṭhāsavo. ^8565

(Gha) avijjāsavo ^8566

Tattha katamo avijjāsavo? Dukkhe aññāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudaye aññāṇaṃ, dukkhanirodhe aññāṇaṃ, ^8567

dukkhanirodhagāminiyā paṭipadāya aññāṇaṃ, pubbante aññāṇaṃ, aparante aññāṇaṃ, pubbantāparante ^8568

aññāṇaṃ, idappaccayatā paṭiccasamuppannesu dhammesu aññāṇaṃ. Yaṃ evarūpaṃ aññāṇaṃ ^8569

adassanaṃ…pe… avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuccati ‘‘avijjāsavo’’. Ime cattāro āsavā. ^8570

(2-5) Cattāro ganthādī ^8571

938. Tattha katame cattāro ganthā …pe… cattāro oghā…pe… cattāro yogā…pe… cattāri ^8572

upādānāni? Kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ. ^8573

(Ka) kāmupādānaṃ ^8574

Tattha katamaṃ kāmupādānaṃ? Yo kāmesu kāmacchando…pe… kāmajjhosānaṃ – idaṃ vuccati ^8575

‘‘kāmupādānaṃ’’. ^8576

(Kha) diṭṭhupādānaṃ ^8577

Tattha katamaṃ diṭṭhupādānaṃ? ‘‘Natthi dinnaṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pe… ye imañca lokaṃ parañca ^8578

lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī’’ti. Yā evarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… vipariyāsaggāho – ^8579

idaṃ vuccati ‘‘diṭṭhupādānaṃ’’. Ṭhapetvā sīlabbatupādānañca attavādupādānañca sabbāpi micchādiṭṭhi ^8580

diṭṭhupādānaṃ. ^8581

(Ga) sīlabbatupādānaṃ ^8582

Tattha katamaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ? Ito bahiddhā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sīlena suddhi vatena suddhi ^8583

sīlabbatena suddhīti. Yā evarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ^8584

‘‘sīlabbatupādānaṃ’’. ^8585

(Gha) attavādupādānaṃ ^8586

Tattha katamaṃ attavādupādānaṃ? Idha assutavā puthujjano ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa ^8587

akovido ariyadhamme avinīto, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akovido sappurisadhamme ^8588

avinīto – rūpaṃ attato samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ. ^8589

Vedanaṃ…pe… saññaṃ…pe… saṅkhāre…pe… viññāṇaṃ attato samanupassati viññāṇavantaṃ vā ^8590

attānaṃ attani vā viññāṇaṃ viññāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ. Yā evarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pe… ^8591

vipariyāsaggāho – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘attavādupādānaṃ’’. Imāni cattāri upādānāni. ^8592

(6) Cattāro taṇhuppādā ^8593

939. Tattha katame cattāro taṇhuppādā? Cīvarahetu vā bhikkhuno taṇhā uppajjamānā uppajjati, ^8594

piṇḍapātahetu vā bhikkhuno taṇhā uppajjamānā uppajjati, senāsanahetu vā bhikkhuno taṇhā ^8595

uppajjamānā uppajjati, itibhavābhavahetu vā bhikkhuno taṇhā uppajjamānā uppajjati – ime cattāro ^8596

taṇhuppādā. ^8597

(7) Cattāri agatigamanāni ^8598

Tattha katamāni cattāri agatigamanāni? Chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dosāgatiṃ gacchati, mohāgatiṃ ^8599

gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati. Yā evarūpā agati agatigamanaṃ chandagamanaṃ vaggagamanaṃ ^8600

vārigamanaṃ – imāni cattāri agatigamanāni. ^8601

(8) Cattāro vipariyāsā ^8602

Tattha katame cattāro vipariyāsā? Anicce ‘‘nicca’’nti saññāvipariyāso cittavipariyāso ^8603

diṭṭhivipariyāso, dukkhe ‘‘sukha’’nti saññāvipariyāso cittavipariyāso diṭṭhivipariyāso, anattani ‘‘attā’’ti ^8604

saññāvipariyāso cittavipariyāso diṭṭhivipariyāso, asubhe ‘‘subha’’nti saññāvipariyāso cittavipariyāso ^8605

diṭṭhivipariyāso – ime cattāro vipariyāsā. ^8606

(9) Cattāro anariyavohārā ^8607

Tattha katame cattāro anariyavohārā? Adiṭṭhe diṭṭhavāditā, assute sutavāditā, amute mutavāditā, ^8608

aviññāte viññātavāditā – ime cattāro anariyavohārā. ^8609

(10) Aparepi cattāro anariyavohārā ^8610

Tattha katame aparepi cattāro anariyavohārā? Diṭṭhe adiṭṭhavāditā, sute assutavāditā, mute ^8611

amutavāditā, viññāte aviññātavāditā – ime cattāro anariyavohārā. ^8612

(11) Cattāri duccaritāni ^8613

Tattha katamāni cattāri duccaritāni? Pāṇātipāto, adinnādānaṃ, kāmesumicchācāro, musāvādo – ^8614

imāni cattāri duccaritāni. ^8615

(12) Aparānipi cattāri duccaritāni ^8616

Tattha katamāni aparānipi cattāri duccaritāni? Musāvādo, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, ^8617

samphappalāpo – imāni cattāri duccaritāni. ^8618

(13) Cattāri bhayāni ^8619

Tattha katamāni cattāri bhayāni? Jātibhayaṃ, jarābhayaṃ, byādhibhayaṃ, maraṇabhayaṃ – imāni ^8620

cattāri bhayāni. ^8621

(14) Aparānipi cattāri bhayāni ^8622

Tattha katamāni aparānipi cattāri bhayāni? Rājabhayaṃ, corabhayaṃ, aggibhayaṃ, udakabhayaṃ – ^8623

imāni cattāri bhayāni. ^8624

Tattha katamāni aparānipi cattāri bhayāni? Ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhīlabhayaṃ [kumbhīrabhayaṃ, ^8625

kumbhīḷabhayaṃ (?) (kumbha + īra + a = bhaya)], āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ – imāni cattāri bhayāni. ^8626

Tattha katamāni aparānipi cattāri bhayāni? Attānuvādabhayaṃ, parānuvādabhayaṃ, daṇḍabhayaṃ, ^8627

duggatibhayaṃ – imāni cattāri bhayāni. ^8628

(15) Catasso diṭṭhiyo ^8629

Tattha katamā catasso diṭṭhiyo? ‘‘Sayaṅkataṃ sukhadukkha’’nti saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ^8630

‘‘paraṅkataṃ sukhadukkha’’nti saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ‘‘sayaṅkatañca paraṅkatañca ^8631

sukhadukkha’’nti saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ‘‘asayaṅkāraṃ aparaṅkāraṃ adhiccasamuppannaṃ ^8632

sukhadukkha’’nti saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati – imā catasso diṭṭhiyo. ^8633

Catukkaṃ. ^8634

5. Pañcakaniddeso ^8635

(1) Pañcorambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni ^8636

940. Tattha katamāni pañcorambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni? Sakkāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, ^8637

sīlabbataparāmāso, kāmacchando, byāpādo – imāni pañcorambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. ^8638

(2) Pañcuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni ^8639

Tattha katamāni pañcuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni? Rūparāgo, arūparāgo, māno, uddhaccaṃ, ^8640

avijjā – imāni pañcuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni. ^8641

(3) Pañca macchariyāni ^8642

Tattha katamāni pañca macchariyāni? Āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, ^8643

vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imāni pañca macchariyāni. ^8644

(4) Pañca saṅgā ^8645

Tattha katame pañca saṅgā? Rāgasaṅgo, dosasaṅgo, mohasaṅgo, mānasaṅgo, diṭṭhisaṅgo – ime ^8646

pañca saṅgā. ^8647

(5) Pañca sallā ^8648

Tattha katame pañca sallā? Rāgasallaṃ, dosasallaṃ, mohasallaṃ, mānasallaṃ, diṭṭhisallaṃ – ime ^8649

pañca sallā. ^8650

(6) Pañca cetokhilā ^8651

941. Tattha katame pañca cetokhilā? Satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, ^8652

dhamme kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, saṅghe kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati ^8653

na sampasīdati, sikkhāya kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuccati na sampasīdati, sabrahmacārīsu kupito hoti ^8654

anattamano āhatacitto khilajāto – ime pañca cetokhilā. ^8655

(7) Pañca cetaso vinibandhā ^8656

Tattha katame pañca cetaso vinibandhā? Kāme avītarāgo hoti avigatacchando avigatapemo ^8657

avigatapipāso avigatapariḷāho avigatataṇho; kāye avītarāgo hoti… rūpe avītarāgo hoti… yāvadatthaṃ ^8658

udarāvadehakaṃ bhuñjitvā seyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyutto viharati; aññataraṃ ^8659

devanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘‘imināhaṃ sīlena vā vatena vā tapena vā ^8660

brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā’’ti – ime pañca cetasovinibandhā. ^8661

(8) Pañca nīvaraṇāni ^8662

Tattha katamāni pañca nīvaraṇāni? Kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, ^8663

thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ – imāni pañca nīvaraṇāni. ^8664

(9) Pañca kammāni ānantarikāni ^8665

Tattha katamāni pañca kammāni ānantarikāni? Mātā jīvitā voropitā hoti, pitā jīvitā voropito hoti, ^8666

arahanto jīvitā voropito hoti, duṭṭhena cittena tathāgatassa lohitaṃ uppāditaṃ hoti, saṅgho bhinno hoti – ^8667

imāni pañca kammāni ānantarikāni. ^8668

(10) Pañca diṭṭhiyo ^8669

Tattha katamā pañca diṭṭhiyo? ‘‘Saññī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti ittheke abhivadanti, ^8670

‘‘asaññī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti ittheke abhivadanti, ‘‘nevasaññīnāsaññī attā hoti arogo paraṃ ^8671

maraṇā’’ti ittheke abhivadanti, sato vā pana sattassa ucchedaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññāpenti, ^8672

diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vā paneke abhivadanti – imā pañca diṭṭhiyo. ^8673

(11) Pañca verā ^8674

942. Tattha katame pañca verā? Pāṇātipāto, adinnādānaṃ, kāmesumicchācāro, musāvādo, ^8675

surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā – ime pañca verā. ^8676

(12) Pañca byasanā ^8677

Tattha katame pañca byasanā? Ñātibyasanaṃ, bhogabyasanaṃ, rogabyasanaṃ, sīlabyasanaṃ, ^8678

diṭṭhibyasanaṃ – ime pañca byasanā. ^8679

(13) Pañca akkhantiyā ādīnavā ^8680

Tattha katame pañca akkhantiyā ādīnavā? Bahuno janassa appiyo hoti amanāpo, verabahulo ca hoti, ^8681

vajjabahulo ca, sammūḷho kālaṅkaroti, kāyassa bhedā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ ^8682

nirayaṃ upapajjati – ime pañca akkhantiyā ādīnavā. ^8683

(14) Pañca bhayāni ^8684

Tattha katamāni pañca bhayāni? Ājīvakabhayaṃ, asilokabhayaṃ, parisasārajjabhayaṃ, ^8685

maraṇabhayaṃ, duggatibhayaṃ – imāni pañca bhayāni. ^8686

(15) Pañca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā ^8687

943. Tattha katame pañca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā? ^8688

(Ka) idhekacco samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evaṃvādī hoti evaṃdiṭṭhi [evaṃdiṭṭhi (syā.)] – ‘‘yato kho, ^8689

bho, ayaṃ attā pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappito samaṅgībhūto paricāreti, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ^8690

paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ ^8691

paññapenti. ^8692

(Kha) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi neso natthīti vadāmi. No ca ^8693

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Kāmā hi, bho, ^8694

aniccā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā. Tesaṃ vipariṇāmaññathābhāvā uppajjanti ^8695

sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā. Yato kho, bho, ayaṃ attā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ ^8696

jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto ^8697

hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ paññapenti. ^8698

(Ga) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. Neso natthīti vadāmi. No ca ^8699

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Yadeva tattha ^8700

vitakkitaṃ vicāritaṃ etena etaṃ oḷārikaṃ akkhāyati. Yato kho, bho, ayaṃ attā vitakkavicārānaṃ ^8701

vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ^8702

paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ ^8703

paññapenti. ^8704

(Gha) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. Neso natthīti vadāmi. No ca ^8705

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Yadeva tattha ^8706

pītigataṃ cetaso uppilāvitaṃ, etena etaṃ oḷārikaṃ akkhāyati. Yato kho, bho, ayaṃ attā pītiyā ca ^8707

virāgā…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ^8708

paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ ^8709

paññapenti. ^8710

(Ṅa) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. Neso natthīti vadāmi. No ca ^8711

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Yadeva tattha ^8712

sukhapīti cetaso ābhogo, etena etaṃ oḷārikaṃ akkhāyati. Yato kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sukhassa ca pahānā… ^8713

pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ^8714

paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānappatto hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ ^8715

paññapenti. Ime pañca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā. ^8716

Pañcakaṃ. ^8717

6. Chakkaniddeso ^8718

(1) Cha vivādamūlāni ^8719

944. Tattha katamāni cha vivādamūlāni? Kodho, makkho, issā, sāṭheyyaṃ, pāpicchatā, ^8720

sandiṭṭhiparāmāsitā – imāni cha vivādamūlāni. ^8721

(2) Cha chandarāgā ^8722

Tattha katame cha chandarāgā? Chandarāgā gehasitā dhammā. Manāpiyesu rūpesu gehasito rāgo ^8723

sārāgo cittassa sārāgo, manāpiyesu saddesu…pe… manāpiyesu gandhesu…pe… manāpiyesu rasesu… ^8724

pe… manāpiyesu phoṭṭhabbesu…pe… manāpiyesu dhammesu gehasito rāgo sārāgo cittassa sārāgo – ^8725

ime cha chandarāgā. ^8726

(3) Cha virodhavatthūni ^8727

Tattha katamāni cha virodhavatthūni? Amanāpiyesu rūpesu cittassa āghāto paṭighāto caṇḍikkaṃ ^8728

asuropo anattamanatā cittassa, amanāpiyesu saddesu…pe… amanāpiyesu gandhesu…pe… amanāpiyesu ^8729

rasesu…pe… amanāpiyesu phoṭṭhabbesu…pe… amanāpiyesu dhammesu cittassa āghāto paṭighāto ^8730

caṇḍikkaṃ asuropo anattamanatā cittassa – imāni cha virodhavatthūni. ^8731

(4) Cha taṇhākāyā ^8732

Tattha katame cha taṇhākāyā? Rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phoṭṭhabbataṇhā, ^8733

dhammataṇhā – ime cha taṇhākāyā. ^8734

(5) Cha agāravā ^8735

945. Tattha katame cha agāravā? Satthari agāravo viharati appatisso, dhamme agāravo viharati ^8736

appatisso, saṅghe agāravo viharati appatisso, sikkhāya agāravo viharati appatisso, appamāde agāravo ^8737

viharati appatisso, paṭisanthāre agāravo viharati appatisso – ime cha agāravā. ^8738

(6) Cha parihāniyā dhammā ^8739

Tattha katame cha parihāniyā dhammā? Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, ^8740

saṅgaṇikārāmatā, saṃsaggārāmatā, papañcārāmatā – ime cha parihāniyā dhammā. ^8741

(7) Aparepi cha parihāniyā dhammā ^8742

946. Tattha katame aparepi cha parihāniyā dhammā? Kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, ^8743

saṅgaṇikārāmatā, dovacassatā, pāpamittatā – ime cha parihāniyā dhammā. ^8744

(8) Cha somanassupavicārā ^8745

Tattha katame cha somanassupavicārā? Cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā somanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ ^8746

upavicarati, sotena saddaṃ sutvā…pe… ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pe… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… ^8747

pe… kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pe… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya somanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ ^8748

upavicarati – ime cha somanassupavicārā. ^8749

(9) Cha domanassupavicārā ^8750

Tattha katame cha domanassupavicārā? Cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā domanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ ^8751

upavicarati, sotena saddaṃ sutvā…pe… ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pe… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā … ^8752

pe… kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pe… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya domanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ ^8753

upavicarati – ime cha domanassupavicārā. ^8754

(10) Cha upekkhupavicārā ^8755

Tattha katame cha upekkhupavicārā? Cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā upekkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati, ^8756

sotena saddaṃ sutvā…pe… ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pe… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pe… kāyena ^8757

phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pe… manasā dhammaṃ viññāya upekkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati – ^8758

ime cha upekkhupavicārā. ^8759

(11) Cha gehasitāni somanassāni ^8760

947. Tattha katamāni cha gehasitāni somanassāni? Manāpiyesu rūpesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ ^8761

cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā, ^8762

manāpiyesu saddesu…pe… manāpiyesu gandhesu…pe… manāpiyesu rasesu…pe… manāpiyesu ^8763

phoṭṭhabbesu…pe… manāpiyesu dhammesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ ^8764

cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – imāni cha gehasitāni ^8765

somanassāni. ^8766

(12) Cha gehasitāni domanassāni ^8767

Tattha katamāni cha gehasitāni domanassāni? Amanāpiyesu rūpesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ ^8768

cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā ^8769

vedanā, amanāpiyesu saddesu…pe… amanāpiyesu gandhesu…pe… amanāpiyesu rasesu…pe… ^8770

amanāpiyesu phoṭṭhabbesu…pe… amanāpiyesu dhammesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ asātaṃ cetasikaṃ ^8771

dukkhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā asātā dukkhā vedanā – imāni ^8772

cha gehasitāni domanassāni. ^8773

(13) Cha gehasitā upekkhā ^8774

Tattha katamā cha gehasitā upekkhā? Upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu rūpesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ ^8775

nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā, ^8776

upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu saddesu…pe… upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu gandhesu…pe… upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu rasesu… ^8777

pe… upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu phoṭṭhabbesu…pe… upekkhāṭṭhāniyesu dhammesu gehasitaṃ cetasikaṃ neva ^8778

sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā ^8779

vedanā – imā cha gehasitā upekkhā. ^8780

(14) Cha diṭṭhiyo ^8781

948. Tattha katamā cha diṭṭhiyo? ‘‘Atthi me attā’’ti vā assa saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ‘‘natthi ^8782

me attā’’ti vā assa saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ‘‘attanā vā attānaṃ sañjānāmī’’ti vā assa saccato ^8783

thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ‘‘attanā vā anattānaṃ sañjānāmī’’ti vā assa saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati, ^8784

‘‘anattanā vā attānaṃ sañjānāmī’’ti vā assa saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati; atha vā panassa evaṃdiṭṭhi ^8785

hoti – ‘‘so me ayaṃ attā vado vedeyyo tatra tatra dīgharattaṃ kalyāṇapāpakānaṃ kammānaṃ vipākaṃ ^8786

paccanubhoti. Na so jāto nāhosi, na so jāto na bhavissati, nicco dhuvo sassato avipariṇāmadhammo’’ti ^8787

vā panassa saccato thetato diṭṭhi uppajjati. Imā cha diṭṭhiyo. ^8788

Chakkaṃ. ^8789

7. Sattakaniddeso ^8790

(1) Sattānusayā ^8791

949. Tattha katame sattānusayā? Kāmarāgānusayo, paṭighānusayo, mānānusayo, diṭṭhānusayo, ^8792

vicikicchānusayo, bhavarāgānusayo, avijjānusayo – ime satta anusayā. ^8793

(2) Satta saṃyojanāni ^8794

Tattha katamāni satta saṃyojanāni? Kāmarāgasaṃyojanaṃ, paṭighasaṃyojanaṃ, ^8795

mānasaṃyojanaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ, vicikicchāsaṃyojanaṃ, bhavarāgasaṃyojanaṃ, avijjāsaṃyojanaṃ ^8796

– imāni satta saṃyojanāni. ^8797

(3) Satta pariyuṭṭhānāni ^8798

Tattha katamāni satta pariyuṭṭhānāni? Kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhānaṃ, paṭighapariyuṭṭhānaṃ, ^8799

mānapariyuṭṭhānaṃ, diṭṭhipariyuṭṭhānaṃ, vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ, bhavarāgapariyuṭṭhānaṃ, ^8800

avijjāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ – imāni satta pariyuṭṭhānāni. ^8801

(4) Satta asaddhammā ^8802

950. Tattha katame satta asaddhammā? Assaddho hoti, ahiriko hoti, anottappī hoti, appassuto hoti, ^8803

kusīto hoti, muṭṭhassatī hoti, duppañño hoti – ime satta asaddhammā. ^8804

(5) Satta duccaritāni ^8805

Tattha katamāni satta duccaritāni? Pāṇātipāto, adinnādānaṃ, kāmesumicchācāro, musāvādo, pisuṇā ^8806

vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpo – imāni satta duccaritāni. ^8807

(6) Satta mānā ^8808

Tattha katame satta mānā? Māno, atimāno, mānātimāno, omāno, adhimāno, asmimāno, ^8809

micchāmāno – ime satta mānā. ^8810

(7) Satta diṭṭhiyo ^8811

951. (Ka) tattha katamā satta diṭṭhiyo? Idhekacco samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evaṃvādī hoti ^8812

evaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘‘yato kho, bho, ayaṃ attā rūpī cātumahābhūtiko [cātummahābhūtiko (sī. syā.)] ^8813

mātāpettikasambhavo kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ maraṇā, ettāvatā kho bho, ayaṃ ^8814

attā sammā samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8815

(Kha) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8816

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho bho añño attā dibbo rūpī kāmāvacaro ^8817

kabaḷīkārabhakkho. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi. Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, attā yato ^8818

kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ^8819

sammāsamucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8820

(Ga) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8821

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho, bho, añño attā dibbo rūpī manomayo ^8822

sabbaṅgapaccaṅgī ahīnindriyo. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi. Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, ^8823

attā yato kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sammā ^8824

samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8825

(Gha) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8826

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho, bho, añño attā sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ ^8827

samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ananto ākāsoti ^8828

ākāsānañcāyatanūpago. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi. Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, attā yato ^8829

kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sammā ^8830

samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8831

(Ṅa) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8832

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho, bho, añño attā sabbaso ^8833

ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma anantaṃ viññāṇanti viññāṇañcāyatanūpago. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na ^8834

passasi. Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, attā yato kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti ^8835

paraṃ maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ ^8836

vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8837

(Ca) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho, bho, eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8838

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho, bho, añño attā sabbaso ^8839

viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma natthi kiñcīti ākiñcaññāyatanūpago. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi. ^8840

Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, attā yato kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ ^8841

maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ ^8842

vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. ^8843

(Cha) tamañño evamāha – ‘‘atthi kho pana eso attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi. ‘Neso natthī’ti vadāmi. No ca ^8844

kho, bho, ayaṃ attā ettāvatā sammā samucchinno hoti. Atthi kho, bho, añño attā sabbaso ^8845

ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpago. Taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi. ^8846

Tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi. So kho, bho, attā yato kāyassa bhedā ucchijjati vinassati na hoti paraṃ ^8847

maraṇā, ettāvatā kho, bho, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchinno hotī’’ti. Ittheke sato sattassa ucchedaṃ ^8848

vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ paññapenti. Imā satta diṭṭhiyo. ^8849

Sattakaṃ. ^8850

8. Aṭṭhakaniddeso ^8851

(1) Aṭṭha kilesavatthūni ^8852

952. Tattha katamāni aṭṭha kilesavatthūni? Lobho, doso, moho, māno, diṭṭhi, vicikicchā, thinaṃ, ^8853

uddhaccaṃ – imāni aṭṭha kilesavatthūni. ^8854

(2) Aṭṭha kusītavatthūni ^8855

953. Tattha katamāni aṭṭha kusītavatthūni? ^8856

(Ka) idha bhikkhunā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘kammaṃ kho me kātabbaṃ ^8857

bhavissati. Kammaṃ kho pana me karontassa kāyo kilamissati. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na ^8858

vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ ^8859

paṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8860

(Kha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘ahaṃ kho kammaṃ ^8861

akāsiṃ. Kammaṃ kho pana me karontassa kāyo kilanto. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na ^8862

vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ ^8863

dutiyaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8864

(Ga) puna caparaṃ bhikkhunā maggo gantabbo hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘maggo kho me gantabbo ^8865

bhavissati. Maggaṃ kho pana me gacchantassa kāyo kilamissati. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; ^8866

na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ ^8867

tatiyaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8868

(Gha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhunā maggo gato hoti. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘ahaṃ kho maggaṃ ^8869

agamāsiṃ. Maggaṃ kho pana me gacchantassa kāyo kilanto. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na ^8870

vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ ^8871

catutthaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8872

(Ṅa) puna caparaṃ bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto na labhati lūkhassa vā ^8873

paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ^8874

piṇḍāya caranto nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa me kāyo ^8875

kilanto akammañño. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, ^8876

anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ pañcamaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8877

(Ca) puna caparaṃ bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya caranto labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa ^8878

vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘ahaṃ kho gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya ^8879

caranto alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhojanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ. Tassa me kāyo kilanto ^8880

akammañño māsācitaṃ maññe. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa ^8881

pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8882

(Cha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhuno uppanno hoti appamattako ābādho. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘uppanno ^8883

kho me ayaṃ appamattako ābādho. Atthi kappo nipajjituṃ. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na ^8884

vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ ^8885

sattamaṃ kusītavatthu. ^8886

(Ja) puna caparaṃ bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhito [gilānavuṭṭhito (saddanībhi) a. ni. 6.16 pāḷiyā ṭīkā ^8887

passitabbā] hoti aciravuṭṭhito gelaññā. Tassa evaṃ hoti – ‘‘ahaṃ kho gilānā vuṭṭhito aciravuṭṭhito ^8888

gelaññā. Tassa me kāyo dubbalo akammañño. Handāhaṃ nipajjāmī’’ti. So nipajjati; na vīriyaṃ ārabhati ^8889

appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya. Idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ ^8890

kusītavatthu. Imāni aṭṭha kusītavatthūni. ^8891

(3) Aṭṭhasu lokadhammesu cittassa paṭighāto ^8892

954. Tattha katamesu aṭṭhasu lokadhammesu cittassa paṭighāto? Lābhe sārāgo, alābhe paṭivirodho, ^8893

yase sārāgo, ayase paṭivirodho, pasaṃsāya sārāgo, nindāya paṭivirodho, sukhe sārāgo, dukkhe ^8894

paṭivirodho – imesu aṭṭhasu lokadhammesu cittassa paṭighāto. ^8895

(4) Aṭṭhaanariyavohārā ^8896

955. Tattha katame aṭṭha anariyavohārā? Adiṭṭhe diṭṭhavāditā, assute sutavāditā, amute mutavāditā, ^8897

aviññāte viññātavāditā, diṭṭhe adiṭṭhavāditā, sute assutavāditā, mute amutavāditā, viññāte aviññātavāditā ^8898

– ime aṭṭha anariyavohārā. ^8899

(5) Aṭṭha micchattā ^8900

956. Tattha katame aṭṭha micchattā? Micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappo, micchāvācā, ^8901

micchākammanto, micchāājīvo, micchāvāyāmo, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi – ime aṭṭha micchattā. ^8902

(6) Aṭṭha purisadosā ^8903

957. Tattha katame aṭṭha purisadosā? (Ka) idha bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu ^8904

bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno [codiyamāno (sī. syā.) a. ni. 8.14] ‘‘na sarāmi na sarāmī’’ti assatiyāva ^8905

nibbeṭheti. Ayaṃ paṭhamo purisadoso. ^8906

(Kha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā ^8907

codiyamāno codakaṃyeva paṭippharati – ‘‘kiṃ nu kho tuyhaṃ bālassa abyattassa bhaṇitena! Tvampi ^8908

nāma maṃ bhaṇitabbaṃ maññasī’’ti! Ayaṃ dutiyo purisadoso. ^8909

(Ga) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā ^8910

codiyamāno codakaṃyeva [codakasseva (syā.) a. ni. 8.14] paccāropeti – ‘‘tvampi khosi itthannāmaṃ ^8911

āpattiṃ āpanno. Tvaṃ tāva paṭhamaṃ paṭikarohī’’ti. Ayaṃ tatiyo purisadoso. ^8912

(Gha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā ^8913

codiyamāno aññenāññaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmeti, kopañca dosañca appaccayañca ^8914

pātukaroti. Ayaṃ catuttho purisadoso. ^8915

(Ṅa) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā ^8916

codiyamāno saṅghamajjhe bāhāvikkhepakaṃ bhaṇati. Ayaṃ pañcamo purisadoso. ^8917

(Ca) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno ^8918

anādiyitvā saṅghaṃ, anādiyitvā codakaṃ, sāpattikova [āpattikova (ka.) a. ni. 8.14] yenakāmaṃ ^8919

pakkamati. Ayaṃ chaṭṭho purisadoso. ^8920

(Cha) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā ^8921

codiyamāno ‘‘nevāhaṃ āpannomhi, na panāhaṃ anāpannomhī’’ti tuṇhībhūto saṅghaṃ viheseti. Ayaṃ ^8922

sattamo purisadoso. ^8923

(Ja) puna caparaṃ bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā codenti. So bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā codiyamāno ^8924

evamāha – ‘‘kiṃ nu kho tumhe āyasmanto atibāḷhaṃ mayi byāvaṭā. Idānāhaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya ^8925

hīnāyāvattissāmī’’ti. So sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā evamāha – ‘‘idāni kho tumhe āyasmanto ^8926

attamanā hothā’’ti. Ayaṃ aṭṭhamo purisadoso. Ime aṭṭha purisadosā. ^8927

(7) Aṭṭha asaññīvādā ^8928

958. Tattha katame aṭṭha asaññīvādā? ‘‘Rūpī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – asaññīti naṃ ^8929

paññapenti; arūpī attā…pe… rūpī ca arūpī ca…pe… nevarūpīnārūpī…pe… ‘‘antavā attā hoti arogo ^8930

paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – asaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘anantavā attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – asaññīti naṃ ^8931

paññapenti; ‘‘antavā ca anantavā ca attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – asaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ^8932

‘‘nevantavā nānantavā attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – asaññīti naṃ paññapenti. Ime aṭṭha ^8933

asaññīvādā. ^8934

(8) Aṭṭha nevasaññīnāsaññīvādā ^8935

959. Tattha katame aṭṭha nevasaññīnāsaññīvādā? ‘‘Rūpī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – ^8936

nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘arūpī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ ^8937

paññapenti; ‘‘rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ^8938

‘‘nevarūpīnārūpī attā hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘antavā attā ^8939

hoti arogo paraṃ maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘anantavā attā hoti arogo paraṃ ^8940

maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘antavā ca anantavā ca attā hoti arogo paraṃ ^8941

maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti; ‘‘nevantavā nānantavā attā hoti arogo paraṃ ^8942

maraṇā’’ti – nevasaññīnāsaññīti naṃ paññapenti. Ime aṭṭha nevasaññīnāsaññīvādā. ^8943

Aṭṭhakaṃ. ^8944

9. Navakaniddeso ^8945

(1) Nava āghātavatthūni ^8946

960. Tattha katamāni nava āghātavatthūni? ‘‘Anatthaṃ me acarī’’ti āghāto jāyati; ‘‘anatthaṃ me ^8947

caratī’’ti āghāto jāyati; ‘‘anatthaṃ me carissatī’’ti āghāto jāyati; ‘‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ ^8948

acari’’…pe… anatthaṃ carati…pe… anatthaṃ carissatīti āghāto jāyati; appiyassa me amanāpassa ^8949

atthaṃ acari…pe… atthaṃ carati…pe… atthaṃ carissatīti āghāto jāyati. Imāni nava āghātavatthūni. ^8950

(2) Nava purisamalāni ^8951

961. Tattha katamāni nava purisamalāni? Kodho, makkho, issā, macchariyaṃ, māyā, sāṭheyyaṃ, ^8952

musāvādo, pāpicchatā, micchādiṭṭhi – imāni nava purisamalāni. ^8953

(3) Navavidhā mānā ^8954

962. Tattha katame navavidhā mānā? ‘‘Seyyassa seyyohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘seyyassa ^8955

sadisohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘seyyassa hīnohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘sadisassa seyyohamasmī’’ti māno, ^8956

‘‘sadisassa sadisohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘sadisassa hīnohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘hīnassa seyyohamasmī’’ti ^8957

māno, ‘‘hīnassa sadisohamasmī’’ti māno, ‘‘hīnassa hīnohamasmī’’ti māno – ime navavidhā mānā. ^8958

(4) Nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā ^8959

963. Tattha katame nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā? Taṇhaṃ paṭicca pariyesanā, pariyesanaṃ paṭicca ^8960

lābho, lābhaṃ paṭicca vinicchayo, vinicchayaṃ paṭicca chandarāgo, chandarāgaṃ paṭicca ajjhosānaṃ, ^8961

ajjhosānaṃ paṭicca pariggaho, pariggahaṃ paṭicca macchariyaṃ, macchariyaṃ paṭicca ārakkho, ^8962

ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ daṇḍādāna-satthādāna-kalaha-viggahavivāda-tuvaṃtuvaṃ-pesuñña-musāvādā aneke ^8963

pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti – ime nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā. ^8964

(5) Nava iñjitāni ^8965

964. Tattha katamāni nava iñjitāni? ‘‘Asmī’’ti iñjitametaṃ, ‘‘ahamasmī’’ti iñjitametaṃ, ^8966

‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti iñjitametaṃ ‘‘bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ, ‘‘rūpī bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ, ‘‘arūpī ^8967

bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ, ‘‘saññī bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ, ‘‘asaññī bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ, ^8968

‘‘nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissa’’nti iñjitametaṃ – imāni nava iñjitāni. ^8969

(6-9) Nava maññitādīni ^8970

965. Tattha katamāni nava maññitāni… nava phanditāni… nava papañcitāni… nava saṅkhatāni? ^8971

‘‘Asmī’’ti saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘ahamasmī’’ti saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti saṅkhatametaṃ, ^8972

‘‘bhavissa’’nti saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘rūpī bhavissa’’nti saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘arūpī bhavissa’’nti ^8973

saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘saññī bhavissa’’nti saṅkhatametaṃ, ‘‘asaññī bhavissa’’nti saṅkhatametaṃ, ^8974

‘‘nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissa’’nti saṅkhatametaṃ – imāni nava saṅkhatāni. ^8975

Navakaṃ. ^8976

10. Dasakaniddeso ^8977

(1) Dasa kilesavatthūni ^8978

966. Tattha katamāni dasa kilesavatthūni? Lobho, doso, moho, māno, diṭṭhi, vicikicchā, thinaṃ, ^8979

uddhaccaṃ, ahirikaṃ, anottappaṃ – imāni dasa kilesavatthūni. ^8980

(2) Dasa āghātavatthūni ^8981

967. Tattha katamāni dasa āghātavatthūni? ‘‘Anatthaṃ me acarī’’ti āghāto jāyati, ‘‘anatthaṃ me ^8982

caratī’’ti āghāto jāyati, ‘‘anatthaṃ me carissatī’’ti āghāto jāyati, ‘‘piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ ^8983

acari’’…pe… anatthaṃ carati…pe… anatthaṃ carissatīti āghāto jāyati, appiyassa me amanāpassa ^8984

atthaṃ acari…pe… atthaṃ carati…pe… atthaṃ carissatīti āghāto jāyati, aṭṭhāne vā pana āghāto jāyati – ^8985

imāni dasa āghātavatthūni. ^8986

(3) Dasa akusalakammapathā ^8987

968. Tattha katame dasa akusalakammapathā? Pāṇātipāto, adinnādānaṃ, kāmesumicchācāro, ^8988

musāvādo, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpo, abhijjhā, byāpādo, micchādiṭṭhi – ime dasa ^8989

akusalakammapathā. ^8990

(4) Dasa saṃyojanāni ^8991

969. Tattha katamāni dasa saṃyojanāni? Kāmarāgasaṃyojanaṃ, paṭighasaṃyojanaṃ, ^8992

mānasaṃyojanaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ, vicikicchāsaṃyojanaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsasaṃyojanaṃ, ^8993

bhavarāgasaṃyojanaṃ, issāsaṃyojanaṃ, macchariyasaṃyojanaṃ, avijjāsaṃyojanaṃ – imāni dasa ^8994

saṃyojanāni. ^8995

(5) Dasa micchattā ^8996

970. Tattha katame dasa micchattā? Micchādiṭṭhi, micchāsaṅkappo, micchāvācā, micchākammanto, ^8997

micchāājīvo, micchāvāyāmo, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇaṃ, micchāvimutti – ime dasa ^8998

micchattā. ^8999

(6) Dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhi ^9000

971. Tattha katamā dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhi? Natthi dinnaṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi ^9001

sukatadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko, natthi paro loko, natthi mātā, natthi ^9002

pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā [samaggatā (ka.)] sammā ^9003

paṭipannā ye imañca lokaṃ parañca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentīti – ayaṃ dasavatthukā ^9004

micchādiṭṭhi. ^9005

(7) Dasavatthukā antaggāhikā diṭṭhi ^9006

972. Tattha katamā dasavatthukā antaggāhikā diṭṭhi? Sassato lokoti vā, asassato lokoti vā, antavā ^9007

lokoti vā, anantavā lokoti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīranti vā, hoti tathāgato ^9008

paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇāti vā, ^9009

neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇāti vā – ayaṃ dasavatthukā antaggāhikā diṭṭhi. ^9010

Dasakaṃ. ^9011

11. Taṇhāvicaritaniddeso ^9012

(1) Ajjhattikassa upādāya ^9013

973. Tattha katamāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya? Asmīti hoti, itthasmīti hoti, ^9014

evasmīti hoti, aññathāsmīti hoti, bhavissanti hoti, itthaṃ bhavissanti hoti, evaṃ bhavissanti hoti, aññathā ^9015

bhavissanti hoti, asasmīti hoti, sātasmīti hoti, siyanti hoti, itthaṃ siyanti hoti, evaṃ siyanti hoti, aññathā ^9016

siyanti hoti, apāhaṃ siyanti hoti, apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti, apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti hoti, apāhaṃ aññathā ^9017

siyanti hoti. ^9018

974. Kathañca asmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ [anavakārī (sī. ka.)] karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… ^9019

vedanaṃ… saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ asmīti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, asmīti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, ^9020

asmīti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – itthasmīti vā evasmīti vā aññathāsmīti ^9021

vā. ^9022

(2) Kathañca itthasmīti hoti? Khattiyosmīti vā, brāhmaṇosmīti vā, vessosmīti vā, suddosmīti vā, ^9023

gahaṭṭhosmīti vā, pabbajitosmīti vā, devosmīti vā, manussosmīti vā, rūpīsmīti vā, arūpīsmīti vā, ^9024

saññīsmīti vā, asaññīsmīti vā, nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ itthasmīti hoti. ^9025

(3) Kathañca evasmīti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ [paraṃ puggalaṃ (syā.)] upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo ^9026

tathāhaṃ khattiyosmīti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇosmīti vā, yathā so vesso tathāhaṃ ^9027

vessosmīti vā yathā so suddo tathāhaṃ suddosmīti vā, yathā so gahaṭṭho tathāhaṃ gahaṭṭhosmīti vā, ^9028

yathā so pabbajito tathāhaṃ pabbajitosmīti vā, yathā so devo tathāhaṃ devosmīti vā, yathā so manusso ^9029

tathāhaṃ manussosmīti vā, yathā so rūpī tathāhaṃ rūpīsmīti vā, yathā so arūpī tathāhaṃ arūpīsmīti vā, ^9030

yathā so saññī tathāhaṃ saññīsmīti vā, yathā so asaññī tathāhaṃ asaññīsmīti vā, yathā so ^9031

nevasaññīnāsaññī tathāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ evasmīti hoti. ^9032

(4) Kathañca aññathāsmīti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo nāhaṃ tathā ^9033

khattiyosmīti vā yathā so brāhmaṇo nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇosmīti vā, yathā so vesso nāhaṃ tathā ^9034

vessosmīti vā, yathā so suddo nāhaṃ tathā suddosmīti vā, yathā so gahaṭṭho nāhaṃ tathā gahaṭṭhosmīti ^9035

vā, yathā so pabbajito nāhaṃ tathā pabbajitosmīti vā, yathā so devo nāhaṃ tathā devosmīti vā, yathā so ^9036

manusso nāhaṃ tathā manussosmīti vā, yathā so rūpī nāhaṃ tathā rūpīsmīti vā, yathā so arūpī nāhaṃ ^9037

tathā arūpīsmīti vā, yathā so saññī nāhaṃ tathā saññīsmīti vā, yathā so asaññī nāhaṃ tathā asaññīsmīti ^9038

vā, yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ aññathāsmīti hoti. ^9039

(5) Kathañca bhavissanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9040

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ bhavissanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, bhavissanti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, ^9041

bhavissanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – itthaṃ bhavissanti vā, evaṃ ^9042

bhavissanti vā, aññathā bhavissanti vā. ^9043

(6) Kathañca itthaṃ bhavissanti hoti? Khattiyo bhavissanti vā, brāhmaṇo bhavissanti vā, vesso ^9044

bhavissanti vā, suddo bhavissanti vā, gahaṭṭho bhavissanti vā, pabbajito bhavissanti vā, devo bhavissanti ^9045

vā, manusso bhavissanti vā, rūpī bhavissanti vā, arūpī bhavissanti vā, saññī bhavissanti vā, asaññī ^9046

bhavissanti vā, nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissanti vā – evaṃ itthaṃ bhavissanti hoti. ^9047

(7) Kathañca evaṃ bhavissanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo tathāhaṃ khattiyo ^9048

bhavissanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇo bhavissanti vā…pe… yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī ^9049

tathāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissanti vā – evaṃ bhavissanti hoti. ^9050

(8) Kathañca aññathā bhavissanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo nāhaṃ tathā ^9051

khattiyo bhavissanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo bhavissanti vā…pe… yathā so ^9052

nevasaññīnāsaññī nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissanti vā – evaṃ aññathā bhavissanti hoti. ^9053

(9) Kathañca asasmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9054

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ niccosmi dhuvosmi sassatosmi avipariṇāmadhammosmīti – evaṃ ^9055

asasmīti hoti. ^9056

(10) Kathañca sātasmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9057

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ ucchijjissāmi vinassissāmi na bhavissāmīti – evaṃ sātasmīti hoti. ^9058

(11) Kathañca siyanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9059

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ siyanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, siyanti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, siyanti diṭṭhiṃ ^9060

paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – itthaṃ siyanti vā, evaṃ siyanti vā, aññathā siyanti vā. ^9061

(12) Kathañca itthaṃ siyanti hoti? Khattiyo siyanti vā, brāhmaṇo siyanti vā, vesso siyanti vā, suddo ^9062

siyanti vā, gahaṭṭho siyanti vā, pabbajito siyanti vā, devo siyanti vā, manusso siyanti vā, rūpī siyanti vā, ^9063

arūpī siyanti vā, saññī siyanti vā, asaññī siyanti vā, nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ itthaṃ siyanti ^9064

hoti. ^9065

(13) Kathañca evaṃ siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo tathāhaṃ khattiyo ^9066

siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī tathāhaṃ ^9067

nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ evaṃ siyanti hoti. ^9068

(14) Kathañca aññathā siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo nāhaṃ tathā ^9069

khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… yathā so ^9070

nevasaññīnāsaññī nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti. ^9071

(15) Kathañca apāhaṃ siyanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ anavakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ… vedanaṃ… ^9072

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ apāhaṃ siyanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, apāhaṃ siyanti mānaṃ ^9073

paṭilabhati, apāhaṃ siyanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – apāhaṃ itthaṃ ^9074

siyanti vā, apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti vā, apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti vā. ^9075

(16) Kathañca apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti? Apāhaṃ khattiyo siyanti vā, apāhaṃ brāhmaṇo siyanti ^9076

vā, apāhaṃ vesso siyanti vā, apāhaṃ suddo siyanti vā, apāhaṃ gahaṭṭho siyanti vā, apāhaṃ pabbajito ^9077

siyanti vā, apāhaṃ devo siyanti vā, apāhaṃ manusso siyanti vā, apāhaṃ rūpī siyanti vā, apāhaṃ arūpī ^9078

siyanti vā, apāhaṃ saññī siyanti vā, apāhaṃ asaññī siyanti vā, apāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – ^9079

evaṃ apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti. (17) Kathañca apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya ^9080

yathā so khattiyo apāhaṃ tathā khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo apāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti ^9081

vā…pe… yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī apāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ apāhaṃ evaṃ ^9082

siyanti hoti. ^9083

(18) Kathañca apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo apāhaṃ ^9084

na tathā khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo apāhaṃ na tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… yathā so ^9085

nevasaññīnāsaññī apāhaṃ na tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti. ^9086

Imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya. ^9087

(2) Bāhirassa upādāya ^9088

975. Tattha katamāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya? Iminā asmīti hoti, iminā ^9089

itthasmīti hoti, iminā evasmīti hoti, iminā aññathāsmīti hoti, iminā bhavissanti hoti, iminā itthaṃ ^9090

bhavissanti hoti, iminā evaṃ bhavissanti hoti, iminā aññathā bhavissanti hoti, iminā asasmīti hoti, iminā ^9091

sātasmīti hoti, iminā siyanti hoti, iminā itthaṃ siyanti hoti, iminā evaṃ siyanti hoti, iminā aññathā ^9092

siyanti hoti, iminā apāhaṃ siyanti hoti, iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti, iminā apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti ^9093

hoti, iminā apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti. ^9094

976. (1) Kathañca iminā asmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ… vedanaṃ… ^9095

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā asmīti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā asmīti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, ^9096

iminā asmīti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – iminā itthasmīti vā, iminā ^9097

evasmīti vā, iminā aññathāsmīti vā. ^9098

(2) Kathañca iminā itthasmīti hoti? Iminā khattiyosmīti vā, iminā brāhmaṇosmīti vā, iminā ^9099

vessosmīti vā, iminā suddosmīti vā, iminā gahaṭṭhosmīti vā, iminā pabbajitosmīti vā, iminā devosmīti ^9100

vā, iminā manussosmīti vā, iminā rūpīsmīti vā, iminā arūpīsmīti vā, iminā saññīsmīti vā, iminā ^9101

asaññīsmīti vā, iminā nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ iminā itthasmīti hoti. ^9102

(3) Kathañca iminā evasmīti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā tathāhaṃ ^9103

khattiyosmīti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇosmīti vā…pe… yathā so ^9104

nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā tathāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ iminā evasmīti hoti. ^9105

(4) Kathañca iminā aññathāsmīti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā nāhaṃ ^9106

tathā khattiyosmīti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇosmīti vā…pe… yathā so ^9107

nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññīsmīti vā – evaṃ iminā aññathāsmīti hoti. ^9108

(5) Kathañca iminā bhavissanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ… vedanaṃ… ^9109

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā bhavissanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā bhavissanti mānaṃ ^9110

paṭilabhati, iminā bhavissanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – iminā itthaṃ ^9111

bhavissanti vā, iminā evaṃ bhavissanti vā, iminā aññathā bhavissanti vā. ^9112

(6) Kathañca iminā itthaṃ bhavissanti hoti? Iminā khattiyo bhavissanti vā…pe… iminā arūpī ^9113

bhavissanti vā, iminā saññī bhavissanti vā, iminā asaññī bhavissanti vā, iminā nevasaññīnāsaññī ^9114

bhavissanti vā – evaṃ iminā itthaṃ bhavissanti hoti. ^9115

(7) Kathañca iminā evaṃ bhavissanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā ^9116

tathāhaṃ khattiyo bhavissanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇo bhavissanti vā…pe… ^9117

yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā tathāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissanti vā – evaṃ iminā evaṃ ^9118

bhavissanti hoti. ^9119

(8) Kathañca iminā aññathā bhavissanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā ^9120

nāhaṃ tathā khattiyo bhavissanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo bhavissanti vā… ^9121

pe… yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissanti vā – evaṃ iminā ^9122

aññathā bhavissanti hoti. ^9123

(9) Kathañca iminā asasmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ [avakārī (sī.)] karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… ^9124

vedanaṃ… saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā niccosmi dhuvosmi sassatosmi ^9125

avipariṇāmadhammosmīti – evaṃ iminā asasmīti hoti. ^9126

(10) Kathañca iminā sātasmīti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9127

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā ucchijjissāmi vinassissāmi na bhavissāmīti – evaṃ iminā ^9128

sātasmīti hoti. ^9129

(11) Kathañca iminā siyanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… vedanaṃ… ^9130

saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā siyanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā siyanti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, ^9131

iminā siyanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni honti – iminā itthaṃ siyanti vā, iminā ^9132

evaṃ siyanti vā, iminā aññathā siyanti vā. ^9133

(12) Kathañca iminā itthaṃ siyanti hoti? Iminā khattiyo siyanti vā, iminā brāhmaṇo siyanti vā, ^9134

iminā vesso siyanti vā, iminā suddo siyanti vā, iminā gahaṭṭho siyanti vā, iminā pabbajito siyanti vā, ^9135

iminā devo siyanti vā, iminā manusso siyanti vā, iminā rūpī siyanti vā, iminā arūpī siyanti vā, iminā ^9136

saññī siyanti vā, iminā asaññī siyanti vā, iminā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā itthaṃ siyanti ^9137

hoti. ^9138

(13) Kathañca iminā evaṃ siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā ^9139

tathāhaṃ khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā tathāhaṃ brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… yathā so ^9140

nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā tathāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā evaṃ siyanti hoti. ^9141

(14) Kathañca iminā aññathā siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā ^9142

nāhaṃ tathā khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā nāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… yathā ^9143

so nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā nāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā aññathā siyanti hoti. ^9144

(15) Kathañca iminā apāhaṃ siyanti hoti? Kañci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pe… ^9145

vedanaṃ… saññaṃ… saṅkhāre… viññāṇaṃ iminā apāhaṃ siyanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā apāhaṃ ^9146

siyanti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā apāhaṃ siyanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati. Tasmiṃ sati imāni papañcitāni ^9147

honti – iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ aññathā ^9148

siyanti vā. ^9149

(16) Kathañca iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti? Iminā apāhaṃ khattiyo siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ ^9150

brāhmaṇo siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ vesso siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ suddo siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ ^9151

gahaṭṭho siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ pabbajito siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ devo siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ ^9152

manusso siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ rūpī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ arūpī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ saññī ^9153

siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ asaññī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā ^9154

apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hoti. ^9155

(17) Kathañca iminā apāhaṃ evaṃ siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo iminā ^9156

apāhaṃ tathā khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā apāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti vā…pe… ^9157

yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā apāhaṃ tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā apāhaṃ evaṃ ^9158

siyanti hoti. ^9159

(18) Kathañca iminā apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti? Parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā so khattiyo ^9160

iminā apāhaṃ na tathā khattiyo siyanti vā, yathā so brāhmaṇo iminā apāhaṃ na tathā brāhmaṇo siyanti ^9161

vā…pe… yathā so nevasaññīnāsaññī iminā apāhaṃ na tathā nevasaññīnāsaññī siyanti vā – evaṃ iminā ^9162

apāhaṃ aññathā siyanti hoti. ^9163

Imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya. ^9164

Iti imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya, imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa ^9165

upādāya, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni honti. Iti evarūpāni ^9166

atītāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, anāgatāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, paccuppannāni chattiṃsa ^9167

taṇhāvicaritāni, tadekajjhaṃ abhisaññūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā aṭṭhataṇhāvicaritasataṃ hoti. ^9168

977. Tattha katamāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajāle veyyākaraṇe vuttāni bhagavatā? Cattāro ^9169

sassatavādā, cattāro ekaccasassatikā, cattāro antānantikā, cattāro amarāvikkhepikā, dve ^9170

adhiccasamuppannikā, soḷasa saññīvādā, aṭṭha asaññīvādā, aṭṭha nevasaññīnāsaññīvādā, satta ^9171

ucchedavādā, pañca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā – imāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajāle veyyākaraṇe ^9172

vuttāni bhagavatāti. ^9173

Khuddakavatthuvibhaṅgo niṭṭhito. ^9174

18. Dhammahadayavibhaṅgo ^9175

1. Sabbasaṅgāhikavāro ^9176

978. Kati khandhā, kati āyatanāni, kati dhātuyo, kati saccāni, kati indriyāni, kati hetū, kati āhārā, ^9177

kati phassā, kati vedanā, kati saññā, kati cetanā, kati cittāni? ^9178

Pañcakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo, cattāri saccāni, bāvīsatindriyāni, nava hetū, ^9179

cattāro āhārā, satta phassā, satta vedanā, satta saññā, satta cetanā, satta cittāni. ^9180

979. Tattha katame pañcakkhandhā? Rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^9181

saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime vuccanti ‘‘pañcakkhandhā’’. ^9182

980. Tattha katamāni dvādasāyatanāni? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ^9183

saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, ^9184

phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘dvādasāyatanāni’’. ^9185

981. Tattha katamā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo? Cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviññāṇadhātu, sotadhātu, ^9186

saddadhātu, sotaviññāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviññāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, ^9187

jivhāviññāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviññāṇadhātu, manodhātu, dhammadhātu, ^9188

manoviññāṇadhātu – imā vuccanti ‘‘aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo’’. ^9189

982. Tattha katamāni cattāri saccāni? Dukkhasaccaṃ, samudayasaccaṃ, nirodhasaccaṃ, ^9190

maggasaccaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘cattāri saccāni’’. ^9191

983. Tattha katamāni bāvīsatindriyāni? Cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, ^9192

kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, ^9193

somanassindriyaṃ, domanassindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, ^9194

samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ, aññindriyaṃ, aññātāvindriyaṃ – imāni ^9195

vuccanti ‘‘bāvīsatindriyāni’’. ^9196

984. Tattha katame nava hetū? Tayo kusalahetū, tayo akusalahetū, tayo abyākatahetū. ^9197

Tattha katame tayo kusalahetū? Alobho kusalahetu, adoso kusalahetu, amoho kusalahetu – ime tayo ^9198

kusalahetū. ^9199

Tattha katame tayo akusalahetū? Lobho akusalahetu, doso akusalahetu, moho akusalahetu – ime ^9200

tayo akusalahetū. ^9201

Tattha katame tayo abyākatahetū? Kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ vipākato kiriyābyākatesu vā ^9202

dhammesu alobho, adoso, amoho – ime tayo abyākatahetū. Ime vuccanti ‘‘nava hetū’’. ^9203

985. Tattha katame cattāro āhārā? Kabaḷīkārāhāro [kabaḷiṃkāro āhāro (sī. syā.)], phassāhāro, ^9204

manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime vuccanti ‘‘cattāro āhārā’’. ^9205

986. Tattha katame satta phassā? Cakkhusamphasso, sotasamphasso, ghānasamphasso, ^9206

jivhāsamphasso, kāyasamphasso, manodhātusamphasso, manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ime vuccanti ^9207

‘‘satta phassā’’. ^9208

987. Tattha katamā satta vedanā? Cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^9209

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9210

vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā – imā vuccanti ‘‘satta vedanā’’. ^9211

988. Tattha katamā satta saññā? Cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ^9212

ghānasamphassajā saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9213

saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā – imā vuccanti ‘‘satta saññā’’. ^9214

989. Tattha katamā satta cetanā? Cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ^9215

ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9216

cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā – imā vuccanti ‘‘satta cetanā’’. ^9217

990. Tattha katamāni satta cittāni? Cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, ^9218

jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – imāni vuccanti ‘‘satta cittāni’’. ^9219

2. Uppattānuppattivāro ^9220

1. Kāmadhātu ^9221

991. Kāmadhātuyā kati khandhā, kati āyatanāni, kati dhātuyo, kati saccāni, kati indriyāni, kati hetū, ^9222

kati āhārā, kati phassā, kati vedanā, kati saññā, kati cetanā, kati cittāni? ^9223

Kāmadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo, tīṇi saccāni, bāvīsatindriyāni, ^9224

nava hetū, cattāro āhārā, satta phassā, satta vedanā, satta saññā, satta cetanā, satta cittāni. ^9225

992. Tattha katame kāmadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā? Rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, ^9226

saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā’’. ^9227

Tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā dvādasāyatanāni? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ^9228

saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, ^9229

phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā ^9230

dvādasāyatanāni’’. ^9231

Tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo? Cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviññāṇadhātu, ^9232

sotadhātu, saddadhātu, sotaviññāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviññāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, ^9233

rasadhātu, jivhāviññāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviññāṇadhātu, manodhātu, ^9234

dhammadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – imā vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo’’. ^9235

Tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni? Dukkhasaccaṃ, samudayasaccaṃ, maggasaccaṃ – ^9236

imāni vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni’’. ^9237

Tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā bāvīsatindriyāni? Cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ…pe… ^9238

aññātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā bāvīsatindriyāni’’. ^9239

Tattha katame kāmadhātuyā nava hetū? Tayo kusalahetū, tayo akusalahetū, tayo abyākatahetū… ^9240

pe… ime vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā nava hetū’’. ^9241

Tattha katame kāmadhātuyā cattāro āhārā? Kabaḷīkārāhāro, phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, ^9242

viññāṇāhāro – ime vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā cattāro āhārā’’. ^9243

Tattha katame kāmadhātuyā satta phassā? Cakkhusamphasso sotasamphasso, ghānasamphasso, ^9244

jivhāsamphasso, kāyasamphasso, manodhātusamphasso, manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ime vuccanti ^9245

‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta phassā’’. ^9246

Tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā satta vedanā? Cakkhusamphassajā vedanā, sotasamphassajā vedanā, ^9247

ghānasamphassajā vedanā, jivhāsamphassajā vedanā, kāyasamphassajā vedanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9248

vedanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā vedanā – imā vuccatti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta vedanā’’. ^9249

Tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā satta saññā? Cakkhusamphassajā saññā, sotasamphassajā saññā, ^9250

ghānasamphassajā saññā, jivhāsamphassajā saññā, kāyasamphassajā saññā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9251

saññā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā – imā vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta saññā’’. ^9252

Tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā satta cetanā? Cakkhusamphassajā cetanā, sotasamphassajā cetanā, ^9253

ghānasamphassajā cetanā, jivhāsamphassajā cetanā, kāyasamphassajā cetanā, manodhātusamphassajā ^9254

cetanā, manoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā – imā vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta cetanā’’. ^9255

Tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā satta cittāni? Cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, ghānaviññāṇaṃ, ^9256

jivhāviññāṇaṃ, kāyaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – imāni vuccanti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā ^9257

satta cittāni’’. ^9258

2. Rūpadhātu ^9259

993. Rūpadhātuyā kati khandhā, kati āyatanā, kati dhātuyo, kati saccāni, kati indriyāni…pe… kati ^9260

cittāni? ^9261

Rūpadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā, cha āyatanāni, nava dhātuyo, tīṇi saccāni, cuddasindriyāni, aṭṭha ^9262

hetū, tayo āhārā, cattāro phassā, catasso vedanā, catasso saññā, catasso cetanā, cattāri cittāni. ^9263

994. Tattha katame rūpadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā? Rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, ^9264

saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā pañcakkhandhā’’. ^9265

Tattha katamāni rūpadhātuyā cha āyatanāni? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ^9266

saddāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cha āyatanāni’’. ^9267

Tattha katamā rūpadhātuyā nava dhātuyo? Cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviññāṇadhātu, ^9268

sotadhātu, saddadhātu, sotaviññāṇadhātu, manodhātu, dhammadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – imā vuccanti ^9269

‘‘rūpadhātuyā nava dhātuyo’’. ^9270

Tattha katamāni rūpadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni? Dukkhasaccaṃ, samudayasaccaṃ, maggasaccaṃ – ^9271

imāni vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni’’. ^9272

Tattha katamāni rūpadhātuyā cuddasindriyāni? Cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, ^9273

jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, ^9274

samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ, aññindriyaṃ, aññātāvindriyaṃ – imāni ^9275

vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cuddasindriyāni’’. ^9276

Tattha katame rūpadhātuyā aṭṭha hetū? Tayo kusalahetū, dve akusalahetū, tayo abyākatahetū. ^9277

Tattha katame tayo kusalahetū? Alobho kusalahetu, adoso kusalahetu, amoho kusalahetu – ime tayo ^9278

kusalahetū. ^9279

Tattha katame dve akusalahetū? Lobho akusalahetu, moho akusalahetu – ime dve akusalahetū. ^9280

Tattha katame tayo abyākatahetū? Kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ vipākato kiriyābyākatesu vā ^9281

dhammesu alobho, adoso, amoho – ime tayo abyākatahetū. Ime vuccanti rūpadhātuyā aṭṭha hetū. ^9282

Tattha katame rūpadhātuyā tayo āhārā? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime ^9283

vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā tayo āhārā’’. ^9284

Tattha katame rūpadhātuyā cattāro phassā? Cakkhusamphasso, sotasamphasso, ^9285

manodhātusamphasso, manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ime vuccanti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cattāro phassā’’. ^9286

Tattha katamā rūpadhātuyā catasso vedanā…pe… catasso saññā…pe… catasso cetanā…pe… ^9287

cattāri cittāni? Cakkhuviññāṇaṃ, sotaviññāṇaṃ, manodhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu – imāni vuccanti ^9288

‘‘rūpadhātuyā cattāri cittāni’’. ^9289

3. Arūpadhātu ^9290

995. Arūpadhātuyā kati khandhā…pe… kati cittāni? ^9291

Arūpadhātuyā cattāro khandhā, dve āyatanāni, dve dhātuyo, tīṇi saccāni, ekādasindriyāni, aṭṭha ^9292

hetū, tayo āhārā, eko phasso, ekā vedanā, ekā saññā, ekā cetanā, ekaṃ cittaṃ. ^9293

996. Tattha katame arūpadhātuyā cattāro khandhā? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^9294

saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime vuccanti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā cattāro khandhā’’. ^9295

Tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā dve āyatanāni? Manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuccanti ^9296

‘‘arūpadhātuyā dve āyatanāni’’. ^9297

Tattha katamā arūpadhātuyā dve dhātuyo? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā vuccanti ^9298

‘‘arūpadhātuyā dve dhātuyo’’. ^9299

Tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni? Dukkhasaccaṃ, samudayasaccaṃ, maggasaccaṃ – ^9300

imāni vuccanti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā tīṇi saccāni’’. ^9301

Tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā ekādasindriyāni? Manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, ^9302

upekkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, ^9303

aññindriyaṃ, aññātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā ekādasindriyāni’’. ^9304

Tattha katame arūpadhātuyā aṭṭha hetū? Tayo kusalahetū, dve akusalahetū, tayo abyākatahetū… ^9305

pe… ime vuccanti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā aṭṭha hetū’’. ^9306

Tattha katame arūpadhātuyā tayo āhārā? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime ^9307

vuccanti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā tayo āhārā’’. ^9308

Tattha katamo arūpadhātuyā eko phasso? Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ayaṃ vuccati ^9309

‘‘arūpadhātuyā eko phasso’’. ^9310

Tattha katamā arūpadhātuyā ekā vedanā…pe… ekā saññā…pe… ekā cetanā…pe… ekaṃ cittaṃ? ^9311

Manoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘arūpadhātuyā ekaṃ cittaṃ’’. ^9312

4. Apariyāpannaṃ ^9313

997. Apariyāpanne kati khandhā…pe… kati cittāni? ^9314

Apariyāpanne cattāro khandhā, dve āyatanāni, dve dhātuyo, dve saccāni, dvādasindriyāni, cha hetū, ^9315

tayo āhārā, eko phasso, ekā vedanā, ekā saññā, ekā cetanā, ekaṃ cittaṃ. ^9316

998. Tattha katame apariyāpanne cattāro khandhā? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, ^9317

saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime vuccanti ‘‘apariyāpanne cattāro khandhā’’. ^9318

Tattha katamāni apariyāpanne dve āyatanāni? Manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuccanti ^9319

‘‘apariyāpanne dve āyatanāni’’. ^9320

Tattha katamā apariyāpanne dve dhātuyo? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā vuccanti ^9321

‘‘apariyāpanne dve dhātuyo’’. ^9322

Tattha katamāni apariyāpanne dve saccāni? Maggasaccaṃ, nirodhasaccaṃ – imāni vuccanti ^9323

‘‘apariyāpanne dve saccāni’’. ^9324

Tattha katamāni apariyāpanne dvādasindriyāni? Manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, ^9325

upekkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, ^9326

anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ, aññindriyaṃ, aññātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuccanti ‘‘apariyāpanne ^9327

dvādasindriyāni’’. ^9328

Tattha katame apariyāpanne cha hetū? Tayo kusalahetū, tayo abyākatahetū. ^9329

Tattha katame tayo kusalahetū? Alobho kusalahetu, adoso kusalahetu, amoho kusalahetu – ime tayo ^9330

kusalahetū. ^9331

Tattha katame tayo abyākatahetū? Kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vipākato alobho, adoso, amoho – ime ^9332

tayo abyākatahetū. Ime vuccanti ‘‘apariyāpanne cha hetū’’. ^9333

Tattha katame apariyāpanne tayo āhārā? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime ^9334

vuccanti ‘‘apariyāpanne tayo āhārā’’. ^9335

Tattha katamo apariyāpanne eko phasso? Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ayaṃ vuccati ^9336

‘‘apariyāpanne eko phasso’’. ^9337

Tattha katamā apariyāpanne ekā vedanā…pe… ekā saññā…pe… ekā cetanā…pe… ekaṃ cittaṃ? ^9338

Manoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuccati ‘‘apariyāpanne ekaṃ cittaṃ’’. ^9339

3. Pariyāpannāpariyāpannavāro ^9340

1. Kāmadhātu ^9341

999. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati kāmadhātupariyāpannā, kati na kāmadhātupariyāpannā…pe… ^9342

sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati kāmadhātupariyāpannā, kati na kāmadhātupariyāpannā? ^9343

1000. Rūpakkhandho kāmadhātupariyāpanno; cattāro khandhā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9344

kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9345

Dasāyatanā kāmadhātupariyāpannā; dve āyatanā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9346

kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9347

Soḷasa dhātuyo kāmadhātupariyāpannā; dve dhātuyo siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9348

kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9349

Samudayasaccaṃ kāmadhātupariyāpannaṃ; dve saccā na kāmadhātupariyāpannā; dukkhasaccaṃ ^9350

siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannaṃ, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpannaṃ. ^9351

Dasindriyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā; tīṇindriyā na kāmadhātupariyāpannā; navindriyā siyā ^9352

kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9353

Tayo akusalahetū kāmadhātupariyāpannā; cha hetū siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9354

kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9355

Kabaḷīkāro āhāro kāmadhātupariyāpanno; tayo āhārā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9356

kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9357

Cha phassā kāmadhātupariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā kāmadhātu pariyāpanno, ^9358

siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpanno. ^9359

Cha vedanā…pe… cha saññā… cha cetanā… cha cittā kāmadhātupariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātu ^9360

siyā kāmadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpannā. ^9361

2. Rūpadhātu ^9362

1001. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati rūpadhātupariyāpannā, kati na rūpadhātupariyāpannā …pe… ^9363

sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati rūpadhātupariyāpannā, kati na rūpadhātupariyāpannā? ^9364

1002. Rūpakkhandho na rūpadhātupariyāpanno; cattāro khandhā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā ^9365

na rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9366

Dasāyatanā na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; dve āyatanā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9367

rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9368

Soḷasa dhātuyo na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; dve dhātuyo siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9369

rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9370

Tīṇi saccāni na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; dukkhasaccaṃ siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannaṃ, siyā na ^9371

rūpadhātupariyāpannaṃ. ^9372

Terasindriyā na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; navindriyā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9373

rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9374

Tayo akusalahetū na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; cha hetū siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9375

rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9376

Kabaḷīkāro āhāro na rūpadhātupariyāpanno; tayo āhārā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9377

rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9378

Cha phassā na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā rūpadhātupariyāpanno, ^9379

siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpanno. ^9380

Cha vedanā…pe… cha saññā… cha cetanā… cha cittā na rūpadhātupariyāpannā; ^9381

manoviññāṇadhātu siyā rūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9382

3. Arūpadhātu ^9383

1003. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati arūpadhātupariyāpannā, kati na arūpadhātupariyāpannā…pe… ^9384

sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati arūpadhātupariyāpannā, kati na arūpadhātupariyāpannā? ^9385

1004. Rūpakkhandho na arūpadhātupariyāpanno; cattāro khandhā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā ^9386

na arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9387

Dasāyatanā na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; dve āyatanā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9388

arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9389

Soḷasa dhātuyo na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; dve dhātuyo siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9390

arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9391

Tīṇi saccāni na arūpadhātupariyāpannāni. ^9392

Dukkhasaccaṃ siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannaṃ, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpannaṃ. ^9393

Cuddasindriyā na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; aṭṭhindriyā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9394

arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9395

Tayo akusalahetū na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; cha hetū siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9396

arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9397

Kabaḷīkāro āhāro na arūpadhātupariyāpanno; tayo āhārā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na ^9398

arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9399

Cha phassā na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā arūpadhātupariyāpanno, ^9400

siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpanno. ^9401

Cha vedanā…pe… cha saññā … cha cetanā… cha cittā na arūpadhātupariyāpannā; ^9402

manoviññāṇadhātu siyā arūpadhātupariyāpannā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpannā. ^9403

4. Pariyāpannāpariyāpannaṃ ^9404

1005. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati pariyāpannā, kati apariyāpannā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati ^9405

pariyāpannā, kati apariyāpannā? ^9406

1006. Rūpakkhandho pariyāpanno; cattāro khandhā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9407

Dasāyatanā pariyāpannā; dve āyatanā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9408

Soḷasa dhātuyo pariyāpannā; dve dhātuyo siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9409

Dve saccā pariyāpannā; dve saccā apariyāpannā. ^9410

Dasindriyā pariyāpannā, tīṇindriyā apariyāpannā; navindriyā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9411

Tayo akusalahetū pariyāpannā; cha hetū siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9412

Kabaḷīkāro āhāro pariyāpanno; tayo āhārā siyā pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9413

Cha phassā pariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā pariyāpanno, siyā apariyāpanno. ^9414

Cha vedanā…pe… cha saññā… cha cetanā… cha cittā pariyāpannā; manoviññāṇadhātu siyā ^9415

pariyāpannā, siyā apariyāpannā. ^9416

4. Dhammadassanavāro ^9417

1. Kāmadhātu ^9418

1007. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pe… kati cittāni pātubhavanti? ^9419

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ pañcakkhandhā pātubhavanti; kassaci ekādasāyatanāni ^9420

pātubhavanti; kassaci dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci ^9421

navāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci ekādasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti; ^9422

kassaci dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti; kassaci aparā dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti; kassaci nava dhātuyo ^9423

pātubhavanti; kassaci satta dhātuyo pātubhavanti; sabbesaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati; kassaci ^9424

cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci terasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni terasindriyāni ^9425

pātubhavanti; kassaci dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci dasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci ^9426

navindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aṭṭhindriyāni ^9427

pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci sattindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci ^9428

pañcindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci tayo hetū pātubhavanti; ^9429

kassaci dve hetū pātubhavanti; kassaci [keci (syā.)] ahetukā pātubhavanti; sabbesaṃ cattāro āhārā ^9430

pātubhavanti; sabbesaṃ eko phasso pātubhavati; sabbesaṃ ekā vedanā… ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ^9431

ekaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati. ^9432

1008. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ katame pañcakkhandhā pātubhavanti? ^9433

Rūpakkhandho…pe… viññāṇakkhandho – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ ime pañcakkhandhā ^9434

pātubhavanti. ^9435

1009. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa ekādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ ^9436

devānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, ^9437

opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ, nerayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe ekādasāyatanāni ^9438

pātubhavanti – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, ^9439

jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ. ^9440

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni ekādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9441

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9442

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe ^9443

dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, sotāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, ^9444

rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā ^9445

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9446

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aparāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9447

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccabadhirānaṃ ^9448

upapattikkhaṇe dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, ^9449

gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, ^9450

dhammāyatanaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9451

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa navāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9452

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhabadhirānaṃ ^9453

upapattikkhaṇe navāyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, ^9454

jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ. ^9455

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni navāyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9456

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9457

upapattikkhaṇe sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, ^9458

phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni ^9459

sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9460

1010. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa ekādasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ ^9461

devānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, ^9462

opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe ekādasa dhātuyo ^9463

pātubhavanti – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, sotadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, ^9464

kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ^9465

imā ekādasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9466

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9467

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe ^9468

dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, sotadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, ^9469

kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ^9470

imā dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9471

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aparā dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9472

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccabadhirānaṃ ^9473

upapattikkhaṇe dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ^9474

jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. Kāmadhātuyā ^9475

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imā dasa dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9476

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa nava dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9477

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhabadhirānaṃ ^9478

upapattikkhaṇe nava dhātuyo pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, ^9479

rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phoṭṭhabbadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. Kāmadhātuyā ^9480

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imā nava dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9481

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa satta dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9482

upapattikkhaṇe satta dhātuyo pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, gandhadhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, ^9483

phoṭṭhabbadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imā satta ^9484

dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9485

1011. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ katamaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati? Dukkhasaccaṃ ^9486

– kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ idaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati. ^9487

1012. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ ^9488

devānaṃ, sahetukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti – ^9489

cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā ^9490

purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, ^9491

vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni ^9492

cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9493

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa terasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ devānaṃ ^9494

sahetukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe terasindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, ^9495

sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, ^9496

jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, ^9497

samādhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni terasindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9498

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aparāni terasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Paṭhamakappikānaṃ ^9499

manussānaṃ sahetukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe terasindriyāni pātubhavanti – ^9500

cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, ^9501

somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, ^9502

paññindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni terasindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9503

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Paṭhamakappikānaṃ ^9504

manussānaṃ sahetukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti – ^9505

cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, ^9506

somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ. ^9507

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9508

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa dasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9509

sahetukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe dasindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, ^9510

manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ ^9511

vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā ^9512

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni dasindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9513

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa navindriyāni pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9514

sahetukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe navindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, ^9515

manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ ^9516

vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ^9517

imāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9518

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aparāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9519

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ ^9520

upapattikkhaṇe navindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, ^9521

kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. ^9522

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9523

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9524

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe ^9525

aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti – sotindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, ^9526

itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ^9527

imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9528

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa aparāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9529

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccabadhirānaṃ ^9530

upapattikkhaṇe aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ ^9531

manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā ^9532

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9533

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa sattindriyāni pātubhavanti? Opapātikānaṃ petānaṃ, ^9534

opapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, opapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nerayikānaṃ, jaccandhabadhirānaṃ ^9535

upapattikkhaṇe sattindriyāni pātubhavanti – ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, ^9536

itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ^9537

imāni sattindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9538
