Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa pañcindriyāni pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9539

ahetukānaṃ, ṭhapetvā napuṃsakānaṃ, upapattikkhaṇe pañcindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, ^9540

manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā ^9541

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni pañcindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9542

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti? Gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ^9543

ahetukānaṃ, napuṃsakānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, ^9544

jīvitindriyaṃ, upekkhindriyaṃ. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ imāni cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9545

1013. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa tayo hetū pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ devānaṃ, ^9546

paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahetukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ ^9547

upapattikkhaṇe tayo hetū pātubhavanti – alobho vipākahetu, adoso vipākahetu, amoho vipākahetu. ^9548

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ime tayo hetū pātubhavanti. ^9549

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kassa dve hetū pātubhavanti? Kāmāvacarānaṃ devānaṃ, ^9550

paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, gabbhaseyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahetukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ ^9551

upapattikkhaṇe dve hetū pātubhavanti – alobho vipākahetu, adoso vipākahetu. Kāmadhātuyā ^9552

upapattikkhaṇe etesaṃ ime dve hetū pātubhavanti. Avasesānaṃ sattānaṃ [avasesā sattā (?)] ahetukā ^9553

pātubhavanti. ^9554

1014. Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ katame cattāro āhārā pātubhavanti. Kabaḷīkāro ^9555

āhāro, phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ ime ^9556

cattāro āhārā pātubhavanti. ^9557

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ katamo eko phasso pātubhavati? ^9558

Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ ayaṃ eko phasso pātubhavati. ^9559

Kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ katamā ekā vedanā … ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ekaṃ ^9560

cittaṃ pātubhavati? Manoviññāṇadhātu – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe sabbesaṃ idaṃ ekaṃ cittaṃ ^9561

pātubhavati. ^9562

2. Rūpadhātu ^9563

1015. Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pe… kati cittāni pātubhavanti? ^9564

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe, ṭhapetvā asaññasattānaṃ devānaṃ, pañcakkhandhā pātubhavanti, ^9565

pañcāyatanāni pātubhavanti, pañca dhātuyo pātubhavanti, ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati, dasindriyāni ^9566

pātubhavanti, tayo hetū pātubhavanti, tayo āhārā pātubhavanti, eko phasso pātubhavati, ekā vedanā… ^9567

ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ekaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati. ^9568

1016. Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame pañcakkhandhā pātubhavanti? Rūpakkhandho, ^9569

vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ^9570

ime pañcakkhandhā pātubhavanti. ^9571

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamāni pañcāyatanāni pātubhavanti? Cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, ^9572

sotāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imāni pañcāyatanāni ^9573

pātubhavanti. ^9574

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamā pañca dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, ^9575

sotadhātu, manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imā pañca dhātuyo ^9576

pātubhavanti. ^9577

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati? Dukkhasaccaṃ – rūpadhātuyā ^9578

upapattikkhaṇe idaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati. ^9579

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamāni dasindriyāni pātubhavanti? Cakkhundriyaṃ, sotindriyaṃ, ^9580

manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ vā upekkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, ^9581

satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imāni dasindriyāni ^9582

pātubhavanti. ^9583

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame tayo hetū pātubhavanti? Alobho vipākahetu, adoso ^9584

vipākahetu, amoho vipākahetu – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ime tayo hetū pātubhavanti. ^9585

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame tayo āhārā pātubhavanti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, ^9586

viññāṇāhāro – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ime tayo āhārā pātubhavanti. ^9587

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamo eko phasso pātubhavati? Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ^9588

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ayaṃ eko phasso pātubhavati. ^9589

Rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamā ekā vedanā… ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ekaṃ cittaṃ ^9590

pātubhavati? Manoviññāṇadhātu – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe idaṃ ekaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati. ^9591

3. Asaññasattā ^9592

1017. Asaññasattānaṃ devānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pe… kati cittāni ^9593

pātubhavanti? ^9594

Asaññasattānaṃ devānaṃ upapattikkhaṇe eko khandho pātubhavati – rūpakkhandho; dve āyatanāni ^9595

pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ; dve dhātuyo pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, dhammadhātu; ^9596

ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati – dukkhasaccaṃ; ekindriyaṃ pātubhavati – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ. Asaññasattā ^9597

devā ahetukā anāhārā aphassakā avedanakā asaññakā acetanakā acittakā pātubhavanti. ^9598

4. Arūpadhātu ^9599

1018. Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pe… kati cittāni pātubhavanti? ^9600

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe cattāro khandhā pātubhavanti, dve āyatanāni pātubhavanti, dve ^9601

dhātuyo pātubhavanti, ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati, aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti, tayo hetū pātubhavanti, ^9602

tayo āhārā pātubhavanti, eko phasso pātubhavati, ekā vedanā… ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ekaṃ cittaṃ ^9603

pātubhavati. ^9604

1019. Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame cattāro khandhā pātubhavanti? Vedanākkhandho, ^9605

saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ime cattāro ^9606

khandhā pātubhavanti. ^9607

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamāni dve āyatanāni pātubhavanti? Manāyatanaṃ, ^9608

dhammāyatanaṃ – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imāni dve āyatanāni pātubhavanti. ^9609

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamā dve dhātuyo pātubhavanti? Manoviññāṇadhātu, ^9610

dhammadhātu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imā dve dhātuyo pātubhavanti. ^9611

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati? Dukkhasaccaṃ – arūpadhātuyā ^9612

upapattikkhaṇe idaṃ ekaṃ saccaṃ pātubhavati. ^9613

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? Manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, ^9614

upekkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ – ^9615

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti. ^9616

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame tayo hetū pātubhavanti? Alobho vipākahetu, adoso ^9617

vipākahetu, amoho vipākahetu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ime tayo hetū pātubhavanti. ^9618

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katame tayo āhārā pātubhavanti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, ^9619

viññāṇāhāro – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ime tayo āhārā pātubhavanti. ^9620

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamo eko phasso pātubhavati? Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso – ^9621

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe ayaṃ eko phasso pātubhavati. ^9622

Arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe katamā ekā vedanā…pe… ekā saññā… ekā cetanā… ekaṃ cittaṃ ^9623

pātubhavati? Manoviññāṇadhātu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇe idaṃ ekaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati. ^9624

5. Bhūmantaradassanavāro ^9625

1020. Kāmāvacarā dhammā, na kāmāvacarā dhammā, rūpāvacarā dhammā, na rūpāvacarā dhammā, ^9626

arūpāvacarā dhammā, na arūpāvacarā dhammā, pariyāpannā dhammā, apariyāpannā dhammā. ^9627

Katame dhammā kāmāvacarā? Heṭṭhato avīcinirayaṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparito ^9628

paranimmitavasavattī deve antokaritvā, yaṃ etasmiṃ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā ^9629

khandhadhātuāyatanā; rūpaṃ, vedanā, saññā, saṅkhārā, viññāṇaṃ – ime dhammā kāmāvacarā. ^9630

Katame dhammā na kāmāvacarā? Rūpāvacarā, arūpāvacarā, apariyāpannā – ime dhammā na ^9631

kāmāvacarā. ^9632

Katame dhammā rūpāvacarā? Heṭṭhato brahmalokaṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparito akaniṭṭhe deve ^9633

antokaritvā, yaṃ etasmiṃ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā ^9634

diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā – ime dhammā rūpāvacarā. ^9635

Katame dhammā na rūpāvacarā? Kāmāvacarā, arūpāvacarā, apariyāpannā – ime dhammā na ^9636

rūpāvacarā. ^9637

Katame dhammā arūpāvacarā? Heṭṭhato ākāsānañcāyatanūpage deve pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparito ^9638

nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpage deve antokaritvā, yaṃ etasmiṃ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā ^9639

samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā – ime dhammā ^9640

arūpāvacarā. ^9641

Katame dhammā na arūpāvacarā? Kāmāvacarā, rūpāvacarā, apariyāpannā – ime dhammā na ^9642

arūpāvacarā. ^9643

Katame dhammā pariyāpannā? Sāsavā kusalākusalabyākatā dhammā kāmāvacarā, rūpāvacarā, ^9644

arūpāvacarā, rūpakkhandho, vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ^9645

ime dhammā pariyāpannā. ^9646

Katame dhammā apariyāpannā? Maggā ca, maggaphalāni ca, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu – ime dhammā ^9647

apariyāpannā. ^9648

6. Uppādakakammaāyuppamāṇavāro ^9649

1. Uppādakakammaṃ ^9650

1021. Devāti. Tayo devā – sammutidevā [sammatidevā (syā.)], upapattidevā, visuddhidevā. ^9651

Sammutidevā nāma – rājāno, deviyo, kumārā. ^9652

Upapattidevā nāma – cātumahārājike [cātummahārājike (sī. syā.)] deve upādāya tadupari devā. ^9653

Visuddhidevā nāma – arahanto vuccanti. ^9654

Dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, uposathakammaṃ katvā kattha upapajjanti? Dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ ^9655

samādiyitvā, uposathakammaṃ katvā appekacce khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, ^9656

appekacce brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce gahapatimahāsālānaṃ ^9657

sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce cātumahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce ^9658

tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce yāmānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, ^9659

appekacce tusitānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ ^9660

sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. ^9661

2. Āyuppamāṇaṃ ^9662

1022. Manussānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Vassasataṃ, appaṃ vā bhiyyo [appaṃ vā bhiyyo vā ^9663

(syā. ka.) dī. ni. 2.7]. ^9664

1023. Cātumahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yāni mānusakāni paññāsa vassāni, ^9665

cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo [rattidivo (ka.) a. ni. 3.71]. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo ^9666

māso. Tena māsena dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. Tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni pañca vassasatāni ^9667

cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Navuti ^9668

vassasatasahassāni. ^9669

Tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yaṃ mānusakaṃ vassasataṃ, tāvatiṃsānaṃ ^9670

devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyo māso. Tena māsena dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. ^9671

Tena saṃvaccharena dibbaṃ vassasahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ ^9672

gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Tisso ca vassakoṭiyo saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassāni. ^9673

Yāmānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yāni mānusakāni dve vassasatāni, yāmānaṃ ^9674

devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyo māso. Tena māsena dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. ^9675

Tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni dve vassasahassāni yāmānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ ^9676

gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Cuddasañca vassakoṭiyo cattārīsañca vassasatasahassāni. ^9677

Tusitānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yāni mānusakāni cattāri vassasatāni, tusitānaṃ ^9678

devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyo māso. Tena māsena dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. ^9679

Tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni cattāri vassasahassāni tusitānaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ ^9680

gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Sattapaññāsa vassakoṭiyo saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassāni. ^9681

Nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yāni mānusakāni aṭṭha vassasatāni, ^9682

nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyo māso. Tena māsena ^9683

dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. Tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni aṭṭha vassasahassāni nimmānaratīnaṃ ^9684

devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Dve vassakoṭisatāni tiṃsañca ^9685

vassakoṭiyo cattārīsañca vassasatasahassāni. ^9686

Paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Yāni mānusakāni soḷasa vassasatāni, ^9687

paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ eso eko rattindivo. Tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyo māso. Tena māsena ^9688

dvādasamāsiyo saṃvaccharo. Tena saṃvaccharena dibbāni soḷasa vassasahassāni ^9689

paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ. Manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hoti? Nava ca ^9690

vassakoṭisatāni ekavīsañca vassakoṭiyo saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassānīti. ^9691

Cha ete [chapi te (syā.)] kāmāvacarā, sabbakāmasamiddhino; ^9692

Sabbesaṃ ekasaṅkhāto, āyu bhavati kittako. ^9693

Dvādasa koṭisataṃ tesaṃ, aṭṭhavīsañca koṭiyo; ^9694

Paññāsa satasahassāni, vassaggena pakāsitāti. ^9695

1024. Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ ^9696

bhāvetvā brahmapārisajjānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? ^9697

Kappassa tatiyo bhāgo. ^9698

Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ ^9699

bhāvetvā brahmapurohitānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? ^9700

Upaḍḍhakappo. ^9701

Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā ^9702

mahābrahmānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Kappo [eko kappo ^9703

(syā.)]. ^9704

1025. Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvetvā ^9705

parittābhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Dve kappā. ^9706

Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvetvā ^9707

appamāṇābhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Cattāro kappā. ^9708

Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā ^9709

ābhassarānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Aṭṭha kappā. ^9710

1026. Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvetvā ^9711

parittasubhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Soḷasa kappā. ^9712

Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvetvā ^9713

appamāṇasubhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Bāttiṃsa kappā. ^9714

Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā kattha upapajjanti? Tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvetvā ^9715

subhakiṇhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. Tesaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Catusaṭṭhi kappā. ^9716

1027. Catutthaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvetvā ārammaṇanānattatā manasikāranānattatā chandanānattatā ^9717

paṇidhinānattatā adhimokkhanānattatā abhinīhāranānattatā paññānānattatā appekacce asaññasattānaṃ ^9718

devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce vehapphalānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, ^9719

appekacce avihānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce atappānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ ^9720

upapajjanti, appekacce sudassānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce sudassīnaṃ devānaṃ ^9721

sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce akaniṭṭhānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce ^9722

ākāsānañcāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce viññāṇañcāyatanūpagānaṃ ^9723

devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti, appekacce ākiñcaññāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ ^9724

upapajjanti, appekacce nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjanti. ^9725

Asaññasattānañca vehapphalānañca devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Pañcakappasatāni. ^9726

Avihānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Kappasahassaṃ. ^9727

Atappānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Dve kappasahassāni. ^9728

Sudassānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Cattāri kappasahassāni. ^9729

Sudassīnaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Aṭṭha kappasahassāni. ^9730

Akaniṭṭhānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Soḷasa kappasahassāni. ^9731

1028. Ākāsānañcāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Vīsati kappasahassāni. ^9732

Viññāṇañcāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Cattārīsa kappasahassāni. ^9733

Ākiñcaññāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Saṭṭhi kappasahassāni. ^9734

Nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpagānaṃ devānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? Caturāsīti kappasahassānīti. ^9735

1029. Ukkhittā puññatejena, kāmarūpagatiṃ gatā. ^9736

Bhavaggatampi [bhavaggaṃ vāpi (syā.)] sampattā, punāgacchanti [puna gacchanti (syā.)] ^9737

duggatiṃ. ^9738

Tāva dīghāyukā sattā, cavanti āyusaṅkhayā; ^9739

Natthi koci bhavo nicco, iti vuttaṃ mahesinā. ^9740

Tasmā hi dhīrā nipakā, nipuṇā atthacintakā; ^9741

Jarāmaraṇamokkhāya, bhāventi maggamuttamaṃ. ^9742

Bhāvayitvā suciṃ maggaṃ, nibbānogadhagāminaṃ; ^9743

Sabbāsave pariññāya, parinibbanti anāsavāti. ^9744

7. Abhiññeyyādivāro ^9745

1030. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati abhiññeyyā, kati pariññeyyā, kati pahātabbā, kati bhāvetabbā, ^9746

kati sacchikātabbā, kati na pahātabbā, na bhāvetabbā, na sacchikātabbā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati ^9747

abhiññeyyā, kati pariññeyyā, kati pahātabbā, kati bhāvetabbā, kati sacchikātabbā, kati na pahātabbā na ^9748

bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā? ^9749

1031. Rūpakkhandho abhiññeyyo pariññeyyo na pahātabbo na bhāvetabbo na sacchikātabbo. ^9750

Cattāro khandhā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na ^9751

pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9752

Dasāyatanā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. Dve āyatanā ^9753

abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na ^9754

bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9755

Soḷasa dhātuyo abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. Dve dhātuyo ^9756

abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na ^9757

bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9758

Samudayasaccaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ pahātabbaṃ na bhāvetabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ. ^9759

Maggasaccaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ. ^9760

Nirodhasaccaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ na bhāvetabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ. ^9761

Dukkhasaccaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ, siyā pahātabbaṃ, na bhāvetabbaṃ, na sacchikātabbaṃ, ^9762

siyā na pahātabbaṃ. ^9763

Navindriyā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9764

Domanassindriyaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ pahātabbaṃ na bhāvetabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ. ^9765

Anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ bhāvetabbaṃ na ^9766

sacchikātabbaṃ. Aññindriyaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ, siyā bhāvetabbaṃ, siyā ^9767

sacchikātabbaṃ. Aññātāvindriyaṃ abhiññeyyaṃ pariññeyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ na bhāvetabbaṃ ^9768

sacchikātabbaṃ. Tīṇindriyā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, ^9769

siyā na bhāvetabbā, sacchikātabbā. Cha indriyā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā ^9770

bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9771

Tayo akusalahetū abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. Tayo ^9772

kusalahetū abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, na sacchikātabbā, siyā na bhāvetabbā. ^9773

Tayo abyākatahetū abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na ^9774

sacchikātabbā. ^9775

Kabaḷīkāro āhāro abhiññeyyo pariññeyyo na pahātabbo na bhāvetabbo na sacchikātabbo. Tayo ^9776

āhārā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na ^9777

bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9778

Cha phassā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9779

Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso abhiññeyyo pariññeyyo, siyā pahātabbo, siyā bhāvetabbo, siyā ^9780

sacchikātabbo, siyā na pahātabbo na bhāvetabbo na sacchikātabbo. ^9781

Cha vedanā…pe… cha saññā… cha cetanā… cha cittā abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā na pahātabbā na ^9782

bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. Manoviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā pariññeyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā ^9783

bhāvetabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvetabbā na sacchikātabbā. ^9784

8. Sārammaṇānārammaṇavāro ^9785

1032. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati sārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati ^9786

sārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇā? ^9787

1033. Rūpakkhandho anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā sārammaṇā. ^9788

Dasāyatanā anārammaṇā. Manāyatanaṃ sārammaṇaṃ. Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^9789

anārammaṇaṃ. ^9790

Dasa dhātuyo anārammaṇā. Satta dhātuyo sārammaṇā. Dhammadhātu siyā sārammaṇā, siyā ^9791

anārammaṇā. ^9792

Dve saccā sārammaṇā. Nirodhasaccaṃ anārammaṇaṃ. Dukkhasaccaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^9793

anārammaṇaṃ. ^9794

Sattindriyā anārammaṇā. Cuddasindriyā sārammaṇā. Jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^9795

anārammaṇaṃ. Nava hetū sārammaṇā. Kabaḷīkāro āhāro anārammaṇo. Tayo āhārā sārammaṇā. Satta ^9796

phassā… satta vedanā… satta saññā… satta cetanā… satta cittā sārammaṇā. ^9797

1034. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati sārammaṇārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇārammaṇā…pe… ^9798

sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati sārammaṇārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇārammaṇā? ^9799

1035. Rūpakkhandho anārammaṇo. Cattāro khandhā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā ^9800

anārammaṇārammaṇā. ^9801

Dasāyatanā anārammaṇā. Manāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ. ^9802

Dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ. ^9803

Dasa dhātuyo anārammaṇā. Cha dhātuyo anārammaṇārammaṇā. Manoviññāṇadhātu siyā ^9804

sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Dhammadhātu siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā ^9805

anārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇā. ^9806

Nirodhasaccaṃ anārammaṇaṃ. Maggasaccaṃ anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ. Samudayasaccaṃ siyā ^9807

sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ. Dukkhasaccaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^9808

anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ. ^9809

Sattindriyā anārammaṇā. Pañcindriyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Navindriyā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, ^9810

siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā ^9811

anārammaṇaṃ. ^9812

Nava hetū siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Kabaḷīkāro āhāro anārammaṇo. ^9813

Tayo āhārā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Cha phassā anārammaṇārammaṇā. ^9814

Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā sārammaṇārammaṇo siyā anārammaṇārammaṇo. Cha vedanā… cha ^9815

saññā… cha cetanā… cha cittā anārammaṇārammaṇā. Manoviññāṇadhātu siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, ^9816

siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā. ^9817

9. Diṭṭhasutādidassanavāro ^9818

1036. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati diṭṭhā, kati sutā, kati mutā, kati viññātā, kati na diṭṭhā na sutā ^9819

na mutā na viññātā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati diṭṭhā, kati sutā, kati mutā, kati viññātā, kati na diṭṭhā ^9820

na sutā na mutā na viññātā? ^9821

1037. Rūpakkhandho siyā diṭṭho, siyā suto, siyā muto, siyā viññāto, siyā na diṭṭho na suto na muto, ^9822

viññāto. Cattāro khandhā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. ^9823

Rūpāyatanaṃ diṭṭhaṃ, na sutaṃ na mutaṃ, viññātaṃ. Saddāyatanaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ, sutaṃ, na mutaṃ, ^9824

viññātaṃ. Gandhāyatanaṃ… rasāyatanaṃ… phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ, mutaṃ, ^9825

viññātaṃ. Sattāyatanā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. ^9826

Rūpadhātu diṭṭhā, na sutā na mutā, viññātā. Saddadhātu na diṭṭhā, sutā, na mutā, viññātā. ^9827

Gandhadhātu… rasadhātu… phoṭṭhabbadhātu na diṭṭhā na sutā, mutā, viññātā. Terasa dhātuyo na diṭṭhā ^9828

na sutā na mutā, viññātā. ^9829

Tīṇi saccāni na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. Dukkhasaccaṃ siyā diṭṭhaṃ, siyā sutaṃ, siyā ^9830

mutaṃ, siyā viññātaṃ, siyā na diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ na mutaṃ, viññātaṃ. ^9831

Bāvīsatindriyā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. Nava hetū na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. ^9832

Cattāro āhārā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. Satta phassā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. Satta ^9833

vedanā… satta saññā… satta cetanā… satta cittā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viññātā. ^9834

10. Tikādidassanavāro ^9835

1. Kusalattikaṃ ^9836

1038. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ ^9837

kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā? ^9838

Rūpakkhandho abyākato. Cattāro khandhā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Dasāyatanā ^9839

abyākatā. Dvāyatanā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Soḷasa dhātuyo abyākatā. Dve dhātuyo ^9840

siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Samudayasaccaṃ akusalaṃ. Maggasaccaṃ kusalaṃ. ^9841

Nirodhasaccaṃ abyākataṃ. Dukkhasaccaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ. ^9842

Dasindriyā abyākatā. Domanassindriyaṃ akusalaṃ. Anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ kusalaṃ. ^9843

Cattārindriyā siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā. Cha indriyā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. ^9844

Tayo kusalahetū kusalā. Tayo akusalahetū akusalā. Tayo abyākatahetū abyākatā. Kabaḷīkāro āhāro ^9845

abyākato. Tayo āhārā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. Cha phassā abyākatā. ^9846

Manoviññāṇadhātusamphasso siyā kusalo, siyā akusalo, siyā abyākato. Cha vedanā… cha saññā… cha ^9847

cetanā… cha cittā abyākatā. Manoviññāṇadhātu siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā. ^9848

2. Vedanātikaṃ ^9849

1039. Pañcannaṃ khandhānaṃ kati sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, kati dukkhāya vedanāya ^9850

sampayuttā, kati adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā…pe… sattannaṃ cittānaṃ kati sukhāya ^9851

vedanāya sampayuttā, kati dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, kati adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^9852

sampayuttā? ^9853

Dve khandhā na vattabbā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, ^9854

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi. Tayo khandhā siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā ^9855

dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. ^9856

Dasāyatanā na vattabbā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, ^9857

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi. Manāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā ^9858

dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ. Dhammāyatanaṃ ^9859

siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya ^9860

vedanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vedanāya ^9861

sampayuttantipi, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttantipi. ^9862

Dasa dhātuyo na vattabbā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, ^9863

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi. Pañca dhātuyo adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, ^9864

kāyaviññāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. ^9865

Manoviññāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā ^9866

adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. Dhammadhātu siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā ^9867

dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā na vattabbā ^9868

sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya ^9869

sampayuttātipi. ^9870

Dve saccā siyā sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā. ^9871

Nirodhasaccaṃ na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttantipi, ^9872
